*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*



Babi na goma


*wannan page din naki ne FILLO YAR'MAMA, kiyi yanda yanda kike so dashi🤗*


"Allah yakarama ɗaukaka  ranka shidade wannan yarinyar ƴata ce wadda na haifa sunan ta Bahijja." mamaki bayya ne a fuskar Saifulislam yace. "Ƴarki ashe da rabon Abdulmajid yaga zuri'ar sa Allah ya rayata." 



"Ameen Ranka shidade bari mu koma bakin aiki marece nayi, Bahijja mutafi ko."? Shiko Malik sai jindadi yakeyi yaga Bahijja baisan abinda yasa ba yake jin ta tamkar wata ƴar uwar shi ta jini, shiko Saifulislam cema Sarauniya Zulaiƙa yayi. "Kinga Binta gaskia yarta ta birgeni gata ubaiwa ya kamata ace mun riketa dan ta samu inga tacciyar rayuwa umai kyau."  wani kallo taima mijin nata sannan tace. "Haba Abban Malik yina mu ina riƙon baiwa karfa ka manta ita baiwar muce." girgiza kai yayi, dan baiyi mamakin jin haka daga matar tashi ba yace "Haba Fulani minene dan mun riƙeta karfa kimanta suma bayi  ƴaƴane kamar kowa wai ke sai yaushe zaki daina tsanar bayi da zuri'ar Abdulmajid ki ɗabe aƙidar iyaye da kakan ni tunda basu sukaci amanar ku ba, ya kamata bayi su zama ƴantattun ƴaƴa a cikin wannan Masarautar Daborah haba banjin dadin haka kefa mace ce kuma ansan ku da tausayi."


Cikeda mamaki Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace. "Haba Saifulislam akan bayi zaka dinga tada jijiyar wuya ba ruwana da wani bayi ƴaƴa ne dan haka ba amana ko dangantaka tsakanina da bayi ko wani abu da ake kira rangwa me."


"Haka kike ce? Toh nidai a matsayina na mijinki na faɗamaki gaskia yanda kikaga ya dace haka zakici gaba da gudanar da mulki a cikin Daborah sai dai ki sani yanzun mun samu ilimin addini dai dai gwargwado ba kamar da ba da kakannin mu suka dinga mulkin zalinci ba kama hannun yaro ya kamata ace tunda Masarautar nan tamu mun karɓi addini munsan shari'a dai dai gwargwado muyi koyi da gaskia kibar aƙidar iyaye tabbas kinada iko akan kowa ne bawa a karkashin ikon ki yake sai dai kisani bai kamata a dinga wulakanta suba anacin zarafin su, dan haka ni zan shiga daga ciki in huta kiyi nazari Ummu Malik." yana gama faɗa ya shiga bedroom din shi, sai furzar da iska mai zafi takeyi shikan shi Malik  baisan yaga ana cutar da bayi ko kadan amman ba yanda zaiyi yasan laifin wani baya shafar wani kuma sun hukunta wannan Jafar din ya dace komai ya wuce, gani yayi mahaifiyar shi tafita a fusace, duk inda zata wuce bayi ne keta darewa a tsorace har tashiga sashen ta, shima nashi ɓangaren ya tafi jiki sanyaye......



Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta kwalawa Jakadiyya kira a ɗimauce ta shigo ɗakin Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tana kwance saman gado da kaganta zakasan tana cikin ɓacin rai, sawa tayi akira mata Binta, cikin rawar jiki ta tafi tasa aka kira Binta, tana cikin shara wata baiwa tazo ta gaya mata sakon Sarauniya Zulaiƙa saida taji mummunar faduwar gaba, duk jikin ta yai sanyi, cema Bahijja tayi tazauna wajen su Mairo haka ta tafi cikin sashen Sarauniya, koda ta shiga falo Jakadiyya ta iske kallan da taimata kaɗai yasa uwar bayi sake karaya, tare sukaje Jakadiyya ta shiga ta sanar da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa isowar Binta lokacin mai kula da shirin ta na tayata shiryawa saƙa mata sarkar wuya tayi sannan ta ɗaura bakar alqabba ta fito cikin izzah da gadara koda ta fito Binta naganin fitowar Sarauniya Zulaiƙa zubewa tayi ƙasa tana kwasar gaisuwa, amman saboda  rashin daraja ta mutu haka Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta taka Binta da ƙafa ta wuce ta kanta bayi da Jakadiyya suka take mata baya har ta zauna saman wata irin kujera ta alfarma, bayan Binta kamar ya buɗe haka ta iso wajen Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta duƙa kiran ta tayi da yatsa ta kara matsawa kusa da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta ɗaura kafar ta akan kafaɗar Binta duk bayin nan kansu ƙasa suna karajin tausan Binta duk da suma ba wani ƴanci ke gare suba, hadima keta mata fiffita wasu na mata tausa sannan kafar ta a saman Binta sai fidda iska takeyi  tana nuna izzah da gadara cikin isa ta fara magana. "Ke Binta."  Jikin Binta na ɓari ta amsa. "Allah yataimaki uwar dakina Sarauniya  mai adalci tuba nike Binta ta amsa na'am. "



Cikin gadara tace. "Waya baki damar kawo gaisuwa wajen Saifulislam bana hana ki kusanto zuri'ata ba da wannan wulakantatrar baiwar ba."? Tunda Binta taji abinda Sarauniya Zulaiƙa take kiran Bahijja bakaramin ɗaci maganar tai mata ba sai dai tasan basu da wani zaɓi ko yan'ci tunda suka zo a bayi marassa galihu take takara jin tausan ƴartata, tace. "Tuba nike ranki shidade rigima ce kawai ta yaro amman bazan kuma yawo da Bahijja kimin aikin gafara muntuba."



cikin kaskanci Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tasa aka kira mata Sarkin bulala yaima Binta bulala talatin ana cikin dukan ta Yarima MalikusSaif  ya shigo wajen mahaifiyar tashi sai dai bai jidadin abunda akaima Binta saida aka gama dukanta sannan ya  karaso ya gaida ya zauna tareda gaida mahaifiyar tashi, sauran bayin wajen ne suka gaida shi, itama Binta dake goge kwalla gaida shi tayi, amsawa yayi cike da tausayama ta, magana Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi bayan ta kori dukkan bayin dake wajen banda Binta tace mata. 



"Daga yau kada insake ganin kin kusanto wannan jakar ƴarta ki, a wajena ko mijina uwa uba ɗana Yarima Malik in kunne yaji."? Baiwar Allah Binta cikin sauri tace. "Allah ya huci zuciyarki bazan kuma ba ko cikin gidan nan bazata kuma zuwa ba, zata tsaya a turakar bayi da sauran dangin ta muntuba Allah yakara girma." Cikin kosawa Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace. "Ɓaceman da gani kuma kije kuci gaba da aikin da kuka saba sauran inga wani bai abinda aka saka shi ina fatan kin gane."? Saurin ɗaga kanta tayi ta amsa da "eh." nan Binta ta koma wajen su Mairo suna ganin ta sukasan ba lfy, amman ba damar da zatayi masu wani bayani saboda aikin da sukeyi ba dare ba rana, tana iso Sarkin horo ya lafta mata bulala taci gaba da aiki sai dukan su akeyi kamar wasu jakuna hatta Bahijja itama aikin take duk da bazata wuce shekara biyar ba a duniya  wannan kenan...... 




A Masarautar Waisu kuwa ba abinda sukeyi sai shirin yaki dan kullin training suke ma Jaruman su shiyasa ma suka kaima sauran Masarautu tayin hadin guiwa da Masarautar Raifasu da Masarautar Ƙaisar Yarima Haishir shine yake jagorantar Masarautar Waisu kullin burin shi taya zai yaki Yarima MalikusSaif wani masassaƙi yasa aka kira mashi yasa ya ƙera mashi mashi mai kaifin gaske haka yasa boka yayi mashi mugun sihiri aka zuba guba a jikin mashin boka Sarrilkus yace muddin Yarima Haishir ya soki Malikussaif da wannan mashin tabbas bashi ba kwana duniya, ba karamin dadi yakama Yarima Haishir sai kara shiri yakeyi yau ya kama sauran kwana biyu ayi yaƙi........ 



Ana gobe za'a hadu filin daga, a wajen Binta zaune take Mairo nagasa mata jikin ta dake zubda jini inda Bahijja ke kwalla duk da Bahijja bata da wani wayon kirki amman harta fara gane in mahaifiyar ta tana cikin damuwa, tunda Binta ta fahimci yarta tafara hankali tana shiga damuwa intaga bata walwala ta fara ɓoye damuwar ta, Abdulmajid ne ya shigo gidan yanda yaga matar shi cikin damuwa yasan abinda ke faruwa saidai yasan basu da rabon sa'a a rayuwar  su haka zasu ƙarata a matsayin bayi marassa ƴanci ko galihu a cikin Masarautar Daborah share kwallan idon shi yayi yazo yaba matar shi hakuri, jikin shi na kyarma ya tafi wajen Sarkin dawakai yasan muddin ya isa sai an mashi bulala talatin da safe hakama da yamma inzai dawo gida sai amma shi wata talatin ɗin harma ya saba da wannan wahalar da yake kwasa, shafa kan Bahijja yayi yace. 


"Ƴata kiɗauki kaddara da juriya tabbas kinzo a matsayin baiwa marar ƴanci, gashi kinfito a Masarautar da ba'a san darajar ɗan Adam balle bawa wanda ke kuma kin zama ƙasƙantatta sai dai ki sani Bahijja insha Allahu zaki zama mai gata wadda duk duniya za'a yi mamakin baiwa ta zama mai daraja Allah yaimaki albarka ƴata." murmushi tayi ma mahaifin nata tace. "Ameen Baba saika dawo kayoman tsaraba." tunda Bahijja tafaɗi kalmar tsaraba saida hankalin Abdulmajid yai matukar tashi karfin hali kawai yayi ya fita, shidai yasan yana iya yini baici komai ba saboda wajen aikin shi ko abinci ba'a basu, sai in Binta tayi aiki take kullo mashi sauran girkin da aka rage a leda wani bima ƴarsu zasu ba su kwana da ƴunwa ta ina zaiga tsaraba!?   Itama Binta tashi tayi zata tafi wajen aikin ta tahaɗama Sarauniya Zulaiƙa karin kumallo, tana tashi Bahijja tafara kuka saitabi mahaifiyar ta, da kyal ta lallashe ta takaita wajen wata baiwa marar lfy wadda ta rasa idon ta sakama kon duka da akayi mata wai ankama ta zata zubama Yarima Malik guba a cikin abinci haka Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tasa akai mata lahani tanaji tana gani akai mata ƙage, wajen Goggo Ma'u takai Bahijja itama dattijuwar baiwa ce tana da zuri'a samari da yan mata duk bayi dan dai mijin ta ya rasu, da safiya tayi zasu tafi wajen aikin wahala, ɗaya daga cikin su wanda ake kira da Adam shike kula da Malikussaif yana sashen shi, koda Binta taje wajen Ma'u gaisawa sukayi sannan ta kora mata bayani ta yarda nan Bahijja ta zauna tana gani mahaifiyar ta tafi kuma bazata dawo ba sai dare, gashi bata barma mata ko kwayar zarra ba da sunan abinci haka ta tafi da tausan ƴarta a cikin zuciyar ta..... 




Washe gari da safe shiri aketa yi za'a tafi wajen yaƙi tunda Saifulislam ya dawo aka shaida mashi da sakon da magauta suka aiko yace shima zaije a fafata dashi ba yanda Sarauniya Zulaiƙa batai ba yace sai yaje haka suka shirya kowa yasa kayan jarumai hakama Malik sannan ya ɗaura da kayan Sarakai itama Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta shirya zatayi rakiya har cikin filin Barri, Sarkin yaki da Waziri Dammur da sauran Jarumai kowa yahau dawakai, Malikussaif da sauran jarumai suka kama hanyar tafiya wajen da za'ayi yakin, itama Zulaiƙa da mijin ta sai wazirin ta da sauran bayin da za'a tafi dasu suka shiga ayari akai ta tafiya mai tsayin gaske har suka isa makeken fili mai ban tsoro Jarumai ne iya ganin ka na Masarautar Raifasu sunja sahu sahu sai commender din su a tsakiyar su, suma su Yarima Malik sahu suka ja suna facing abokan gabar su, saidai kamar yanda suka tsara kashi ɗaya ne suka shigo cikin filin Barri, Sarkin Masarautar Raifasu da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa suke ma juna kallan kallo Sarki  Baidulƙais cikin hassala yace. 





 *Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*



Babi na goma sha ɗaya





"Ke Zulaiƙa kisaka aranki yaune ranar karshen ki a duniya keda tawagar ki sannan sai na fille kan ɗanki Malik na sokeshi a jikin takobi na cinye antarsa ɗanya a gaban ki sannan kema in fille kanki abin wulakantar wa ingaje mulkin Masatautar Daborah in mai bayin ki bayi ne daga karshe in malleke duk wata dukiya da kuke tunkaho da ita kuma takobin Bairu zata zama a kalkashin ikona ni Sarki Baidulƙais bin Zadair hhhhh matsiya ta maza a gabanki ya Zulaiƙa."



Murmushi Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi ko ajikin ta duk da taga yawan mayaƙan da Sarki Baidulƙais ya tara dan yawa sai dai basu firgita Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ba ko kaɗan, Sarkin shela ne buga kugen yaƙi cikin kankanin lokaci Jaruman Masarautar Raifasu suka tunkaro su Malikussaif, saman dawakai suka taho da gudun gaske, koda MalikusSaif  yaga sun tunkaro su hannun shi ya juya koda Jaruman Daborah sukaga abinda Malik yaima su take masu takubba suka koma baya masu kibiyoyi suka dawo farko saida MalikusSaif  yaga sunyi rabi ya bada order aka fara yimasu ruwan kibiyoyi sai bazar da Jaruman Raifasu sukeyi, haka sukaita yimasu ruwan kibau kamar ba gobe, danko Malikussaif da yaja kibiya sai sama da talatin tafita haka sukaita kisan su kamar wasu kiyashi koda MalikusSaif  yaga sun kusa isowa wajen su take ya sake juya hannun shi masu takubba suka dawo farko masu kibaua suka koma baya kowa ya tsaya cikin shiri cikin kankanin lokaci Jaruman suka hade karab jikake anata kabtama juna takubba dan ko MalikusSaif  tsakiyar fili ya shiga shida Saifulislam suka dinga filli kawunan maza suma wa'ancan haka suka dinga kashe Jaruman Masarautar Daborah danko Malikussaif  wani irin sabon karfi yake ji wani uban tsalle ya doka ya sauk kan wani mugun bawa na Masarautar Raifasu danko Sarki Baidulƙais jiyake kamar yai hauka tsabar bakin ciki burin shi yaga kan MalikusSaif  a ƙasa yaki yai yaƙi baka ganin komai kasa sai jini ga kawunan mutane birgir kamar ruwa, saida su Yarima MalikusSaif  suka kusa karar da tagwar Sarki Baidulƙais sannan sukaga an turo masu wasu rundunar bakaramin tashi hankalin Sarauniya Zulaiƙa yayi ba ganin Jaruman Masarautar Waisu ne, a cikin ranta ko tana mamakin yanda akai masu zanba cikin aminci saboda basu san akwai wasu Jarumai na bayan na Masatautar Raifasu yaki yakara turnike wa danko Malikussaif  wani sabon karfi yaji ya taso mashi sake ƙutsa kai yayi cikin rundunar Jarumai sai sauke kan maza yakeyi baki daya mamakin Jaruman tar MalikusSaif  ta addabi Sarki Baidulƙais turo wani mugun bawa yayi don ya sauke mashi kan MalikusSaif  sai fafatawa da maza Malik yakeyi ko kadan baiga aibun wannan bawan ba, Zaidu ne yagan shi zai sokama Malik mashi a baya daga nesa ya halbo kibiya biyar suka kashe wannan babban bawan na Sarki Baidulƙais


Turniki yai turniki baka ganin kowa tsabar ƙura saida karar takubba da kakeji ankai minti talatin ahaka sannan kura ta ɗan lafa kaɗan kana hangen na kusa dakai ba lallai kagane na nesa dakai ba Saifulislam ne ya sare kan babban bafadan Sarkin Waisu Gabzur duk wani Jarumi dake Masarautar Waisu saida hankalin shi yai matukar tashi hakan yasa sukayi kan Saifulislam inda Malikussaif da Zaidu da sauran manyan Jaruman Daborah suka tare Saifulislam shima yaci gaba da gabzawa da magauta, shiko Sarkin yaƙi bairu koda yaga Jaruman Daborah sunja baya take ya naɗa commender a cikin kashi na biyu na Jaruman su da suka rabu gida hudu, yaba Yazid tuta, sannan da kanshi suka tunkaro inda Malikussaif yake koda Sarki Baidulƙais yaga sauran tawagar su Malikussaif, saida gaban shi ya faɗi duk a tunanin sa sunkare sai yaga harta linka ta farko yawa, afakawa sukayi ba abinda Sarkin Yaƙi yakeyi sai sauke kan maza kamar kiyashi duk wa'annan da karun da suka dirarma Saifulislam sun karar dasu kaf dan baka ganin uniform din Jaruman Masarautar Raifasu sai yan kaɗan hakan yasa suka gudu suka koma wajen layin da akaja wanda ya raba Masarautar su da tasu MalikusSaif  da zasu bisu Malik ya hanasu duk jikin Malik ya ɓaci da jini tsabar fatali da kawunan mutane da yayi Sarki Baidulƙais ne ya anshi mayik yafara magana a fusace. 



"Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tabbas kinzo da sihiri baiynanne keda tagawar ki har kuka ja da rundunar mutane na dan haka yanzun zan kara farmaku da ɗimbin mutanen da baku tanajesu ba wa'anda suka linka naki linkim ba ninki yau sai nasha ruwa da ƙoƙon kanki." cikin fushi ya ida maganar dan yayi mamaki sosai yanda aka kashe mashi jaruman Masarauta. 



Itako Sarauniya Zulaiƙa cikin gadara da izzah ta kaɗai kai tareda shu'umin murmushin rainin wayo bata ce komai ba hakan ba karamin sosama Baidulƙais rai yaiba, fitowa  Sarkin yaƙi yayi saiga wasu uwayen runduna sun bayya na suka shiga sahu iya ganin ka danko mutanen dake bayan su Malikussaif,  basu wuce dari ukku suko sunkai dubu biyu tsabar yawa daga cikin rindunar Baidulƙais harda ɗanshi Yarima Haishir dan Sarkin Waisu  sai Yarima Husbarul Baidulƙais duk sun shirya fuskar su a turnike danko Yarima Haishir jikin shi wani mugun mashi ne da yaji tsafi so ake ya soki. Malikussaif dashi,  duk sune a cikin sahun gaba baki daya rundunar su, kallan kallo aka dinga yima juna cikin kankanin lokaci aka buga kuge aka sake haduwa sai barin ta kubba akeyi wajen ya yamutse Sarkin yaki da Saifulislam sai zubda kan maza sukayi shiko MalikusSaif  kibiya yake ta halbawa yana kashe maza kamar kiyashi. 


Tund Yarima Husbarul Ƙais ya shigo filin da'ga yake kashe Jaruman Masarautar Daborah kamar me,  burin shi ya isa wajen MalikusSaif  dan cika kudurin shi na ɗaukar fansar kakan sa akan MalikusSaif, shima Husbarul ƙais haka yaita kasar maza yaƙi ya dauki zafi wanda su Yarima Malik sun zubda maza sama da dari biyar kasa suma ankashe masu mutane sosai koda Yazid yaga mutanan su sun rage cikin kashi ukkun da suka boye ya tado tawaga ɗaya sukai ɗari biyar shiya jagorance su inda ya naɗa sabon commender ya barshi da sauran tawagar yaba Lurwan, haka suka taho gadan gadan saida su Husbarul Ƙais suka tsorata, tsakiya suka saka su sai kisar su sukeyi koda Hasburulƙais ya fahimci ankusan karar dasu da alama basu da nasara a cikin wannan yaƙin da gudu yahau doki ya fiddo wannan mashin da yake ɓoyo yayi kan MalikusSaif  kowa yaki yai yaƙi ba wanda yagani shima MalikusSaif  yayi nisa sai kisa yakeyi can Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta hango Hasburul ƙais yayi kan ɗanta. 



gadan gadan da mashi, da sauri Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta zari takobi a kugun bafadan ta tashiga cikin filin yaƙi tafara kashe Jarumai shima Sarki Baidulƙais yana ganin tashiga ya shiga yaci gaba da kashe Jaruman ta, tafiya takeyi tana kisar maza burin ta ta isa wajen bawan nan dake neman rayuwar ɗanta, MalikusSaif  najuyowa jin karar da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi ganin za'a saukama yarima Malik mashi sai yaji an soka mashi, mashi a cikin ciki baki daya yafara ganin juwa koda Sarauniya Zulaiƙa taga ya caka mashi a ciki zaro kibiya tayi ta saita tsakiyar kan bawan nan ta ɗana mashi take ya mutu, Sarki Baidulƙais naganin ankashe mashi babban bawan shi gadan gadan yayi kan Sarauniya Zulaiƙa shiko Malik jini ne yafara ɗibar shi jinin har ya koma baƙi dan azaba amman a haka Malik ya daure yaci gaba da yaƙi danko Saifulislam yana ganin halin da ɗanshi yake ciki ya haukace takan mai uwa da wabi Yarima Haishir ne ya nufo Malik ganin karfin shi yafara karewa su Huzaifa da Sarkin yaƙi Baru suke tare mashi baya koda Hasburulƙais ya iso wajen Malik ɗaga katuwar takobin tsafi yayi zai sare kan Malik kamar  mai jinni haka Malik ya buga uban tsalle ya kaɓe takobin Yarima Husbarul ƙais take ya sauke kanshi ƙasa ko shurawa baiba wata irin gigitattar tsawa Sarki Baidulƙais yayi saida kowa ya firgita tsabr ruɗewa da tsoro take ya sararma Sarauniya Zulaiƙa  hannu aiko jini ya balle danma Allah yasa feɗarta yayi ita kuma ta soka mashi wuƙa a kafar shi ya faɗi kasa tasa takobi zata sare mashi kai ta kefata a ƙasa baki ɗaya yaƙi ya lafa ganin Sarkin su aƙasa ga gangar jikin Yarima Husbarul Ƙais ƙasa take yan Masarautar Raifasu da magoya bayan ta suka aje makaman su ƙasa sukayi surrender hatta Yarima Haishir yayi surrender yakara tsorata da lamarin Masarautar Daborah jikin tsawa mai ruɗarwa Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace. 




"Kai Baidulƙais nafaɗa maka sai nayi ajalin ka yanzun ya kagani mace mai kamar maza kallabi tsakanin rauwuna ya kagani yanzun ga kan ɗanka Husbarul Ƙais a kasa a wulakance ɗana ya kashe wawa ko kanada ja, gashi duk aran mayakan da kukayi Masarauta ta taci galaba akan ku, ko kana san in gwada maka yawan jarumai na gashi ka nuna ɗawaga da gadara duk mun karar daku." take Jaruman Daborah sukai ta fitowa kamar ba gobe dan yawa, sake a karaya sukayi. 



Taci gaba da cewa. "Dan haka yau dinnan kun zama karkashin ikon Masarautar Daborah sai yanda naga dama daku duk da mulkin ku baida mu Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ba ko wata dukiyar ku, Baru sokoman kan Yarima Husbarul Ƙais ka kawo mani shi nan." cikin kankanin lokaci aka kawo kan Yarima Husbarul Ƙais a gaban Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tasa kafa ta murje kan ta zuba mashi yawo take Sarki Baidulƙais ya rufe idon sa gashi kasa wanwar mace na mashi barazana lallai ya tabbata ya gama jin kunya haka ta wurga mashi kan ɗanshi sannan ta riko hannun Yarima MalikusSaif wanda ya kusa fita hayyacin shi, ta ɗaga hannun shi sama tace. "Ina alfahari dakai Malikussaif ina takama a matsayin ka na ɗana kuma ɗan Jarumai dan haka ina yabama kaf Jarumai na da hazikin mijina Saifulislam dan haka a sa bayi su gyara mana Masarautar Raifasu dan mu huta sannan duk asa igiyoyi ya ɗaure bayin su harda Sarkin su sai abunda muka yanke zamu aiwatar akan ku, lusarin namijin sakarai ja jiki mata a gaban ka tuf." haka ta sake tofa ma Sarki Baidulƙais yawu a fuska tabi ta kanshi ta taka ta wuce duk aka kama mutanen shi harda Yariman Waisu aka kulle da Sarki Baidulƙais suko suka shiga cikin Masarautar shi suka yada zango take Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta umurci bayin ta suka fara hada abinci harda uwar bayi da ƴarta Bahijja tazo da ita, danko Malik ana kawo shi wani ɗaki Sarkin yaƙi Baru yasa wuka ya farke rigar Malik jini ke zuba bakikƙirin koda aka kira wani malami yace guba ce aka saka a jikin mashin kuma zai iya mutuwa Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tasa aka kira mata Sarki Baidulƙais tasa mashi wuka a wuya ta fara tuhumar shi wayai  masu tsafi ga mashin da aka soki Malik sai raba ido yakeyi itako Bahijja koda Binta da sauran bayin da aka zo dashi ta kawo ruwan zafin da za'a gogema  Malik ciwo ai Bahijja na ganin shi cikin hali ta faɗa saman shi tana kiran "ya Malik karka mutu kabarni mai mutunci katashi." kowa ido yasa yana kallan Bahijja musamman Sarauniya Zulaiƙa da take maimaita kalmar Yaya a bakin ta, hannu Binta tasa ta ciro Bahijja a jikin Malik shiko yayi nisa, kauda kai Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi taima Binta nuni da hanyar fita taci gaba da magana. 



"Ba zakayi magana ba ko  kana bukatar iske danka wajen da ba'a dawowa."? 
Cikin kyarma ya girgiza kai ya gama tsorata bazai so ya mutu ba kodan ɗaukar fansar ɗanshi mafi soyuwa a gare shi,  sake daka mashi tsawa tayi tare da sara mashi wuka a hannu aiko jini ya ɓalle, ya rike wajen tana zaro idanu tace. "Zan iya kasheka akan Malik dan haka minti ukku ya rage maka da ka fanshi ranka, ko ni Zulaiƙa yanzun nan zan sauke kanka."


Shiko Saifulislam ya ruɗe ko magana yakasa ji yake inama ciwon Malik ya dawo kanshi dan har yafara fidda rai ga samuwar ɗanshi a ranshi, sake dakama Sarki Baidulƙais tsawa tayi har motsi wajen yakeyi ta ɗaga takobi zaka kashe shi yace. 



"Tsaya tsaya Zulaiƙa zan fada maki komai ki barni inji da abunda ke damuna zan faɗa."


A ɗimauce ta sauke takobi saman cinyar shi sannan tace inajinka yakai Baidulƙais faɗa mani." jikin shi yai sanyi yace....... 







*Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*


Babi na 12

*Sis jameey ki godema Allah, Allah yayi maki baiwar iya tsara labari ko wane irine walau na saurata zuwa na soyayya da kuma ban tausayi, wai dan Allah BAYI MA YA'YANE na yaƙi ne ko na soyayya dan na kasa gane na maiye dan yana son yafi IZZAH KO MULKI ma💃*


_Allaj sarki haka akeyi man wannan question din wai bayi ma ƴaƴane na yaƙi🤣, ae ba abunda bai kunsa ba ya ƙunshi yaƙi da zallar mulki da kuma soyayyar infact komai ma ya kushi fa, , sai dai ku sani nidai har yanzun nafi ganin dadin Izzah ko mulki ko kun mata da Sadaukin Burhaan shida zubainarshi da kuma Asma'u sannan ga Safiyya ina godiya sosai ina maki son fissabilillah  💓_



Banda raba ido ba'abinda Baidulƙais keyi ga zufa duk tagama wanke shi tsabar firgita da tsoro yace. "Tabbas mashin da aka tsoki Malikussaif ko shakka babu zai'iya rasa ranshi mashi ne na bakin karfen tsatsuba kuma bokan kuka shiyayi tsafin dan haka banda tabbacin ɗanki Malikussaif zai rayu yake Zulaiƙa wannan shine abinda nasani game da mashin da aka soki ɗanki Malik.!"

Cikin matsanan ciyar tsawa da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi sai da wajen ya ruɗe hatta MalikusSaif  da bai cikin hankalin shi saida ya bude ido sannan ya rufe tace. "Tabbas Baidulƙais yanzun wannan takobin zata sauke kanka in tumurmusa shi kasa injef gangar jikin ka dabbobi suyi fatali dashi umurni nike baka ya sunan bokan da yai wannan tsafin."? Ta ida maganar cikin tsawa da fushi,  ganin yanda Sarauniya  Zulaiƙa tai fushi yasa cikin tsoro Baidulƙais yace. "Bokan saman kukane dake dajin Gamki sunan shi boka Sarrilkus matsanancin tsababibin bokane dan haka bansan yanda za'ai yazo nan wajen ba abinda nasani kilan saidai  ki same shi can." a fusace Zulaiƙa ta kwaɗawa Sarki Baidulƙais wata irin razanannar ƙara ya kwaɗa saida bayin shi da jaruman shi wa'anda aka kama amatsayin ganimar yaki suka tsorata cikin bada umurni tasa aka kawo mata yariman Waisu Haishir tace atafi dashi sai su Yazid da Sarkin yaki Baru akawo mata boka Sarrilkus gabanta dawakai suka hau suka tunkari bakin dajin gamki dake karkashin masarautar Raifasu banda kwankwamai da aljanu ba abinda ke ɗawainiya da dajin haka suka dinga tafiya mai nisan gaske sannan suka fara kutsa kai cikin wannan masifaffan dajin saida kowa ya tsorata daganin dajin gashi ranace amman kaman dare tsabar duhu dajin da kayan firgicin dake ɗawainiya da dajin. 


wani uban ihu mai razanarwa ake kwaɗawa a cikin dajin, haka sukaci gaba da tafiya a tsorace kowa najin mugun tsoro a cikin zuciyar shi sai kutsa kai sukayi har suka shiga tsakiyar dajin wulgin wani bakin abu suka kagani ya sake firgita su sai kyarma sukeyi can kuma suka karajin angibtasu ga wata irin bakar guguwa data tunkaro su ko gabansu basu hange dukawa sukayi kasa suna buɗe idon su bayan ta lafa suka gansu wajen wani katon jan dutse da wata irin katuwar kuka sai wani bakin abu a gaban su shiba mutun ba kuma ba aljani dan fitsitsi bakin shi duk jini kuma yanada bindi, cikin kaɗuwa suka sadda kansu ƙasa bayan minti biyar suka tsinkayi wata irin kara mai gigirwa da tarwatsin wuta ta dunkule sai juyi takeyi takai minti biyar saiga wani wargajejen mutun bakin kirin ya bayyana da ido ɗaya tare da wasu kattin mutane marassa kyau sai surutai sukeyi take yarima Haishir ya zube kasa suko sukayi tsaye sai mamaki sukeyi ji sukayi an taɗesu basuga kowa ba suka faɗi kasa wata magana sukaji mai razanarwa ance. "Ku yan matasa harkun isa kuzo wajen bokan saman kuka kuki dukawa yanzun zamusa aljani ɗan tsitu yasha jinin ku tunda baku da biyayya hahhhhhha."sai waige waige sukeyi basu ga mai magana ba, can sukaji wata uwar kara ta tunkaro su a tsorace suka fara addua can boka Sarrilkus ya ɗaga hannu wannan guguwar ta dakata, cikin dakakkiyar murya mesa dajin girgiza yace. "Shine jankunne agareku karku kuma zuwa wajen na ba ɗa'a sannan ku koma kucema Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ba'aima Sarrilkus wargi inhar tanason ɗanta Malik ya rayu sai dai akawo a cikin Masarauta ta sannan sai yayi sati a cikin bakin dajin Gamki, inba hakaba daman aljani Darus gaf yake da fara zuƙar jinin Malik danma shidin Jarumi da take ya mutu dan haka kuɓaceman dagani tunkan dare yai maku sannan karwanda ya fita a cikin dajin nan ta gaba sai ta baya badan ku yan aike bane lallai dana kashe ku, ku zaman tarihi Hhhhhhhhhahhh."take ya bace sai wannan wuta dake juyi ga kura kowa ya kasa motsi cikin firgita suka fara tafiyar baya baya sai ganin abubuwan tsoro sukayi har da zakuna kamar zasu cinye su, sai su rufe ido sai addua sukeyi can wata guguwa tazo ta shuresu sai suk gansu wajen wani katon icce suka tafiya mai nisa sannan sukaga dawakan su, sai kyarma sukeyi suka hau bamai magana suka kama hanyar Masarautar  Raifasu.


*Niko nace muna daga gefe nida my fidos yar'mama da Autar safana gami da My Dejja, muna kallon yanda Momyn sultan take gudu tana sakin abun a wando saboda ita ba jaruma bace🌚 👹*

*dan ko Nennerh da Ummun Nana sai Asma'u magari da phatermarh kankia boye wa sukayi bayana😎*

Tafiya sukayi mai nisan zango sannan suka isa cikin Masarautar Raifasu, har cikin fa'da suka shiga suka iske Sarauniya Zulaiƙa  tana ganin su ta mike ta taresu tareda jefa masu tambayoyi. "Baru ya andace kuwa ya nike ganin ku, a sanyaye."!?  Ta ida maganar cikin tsoro dan duk hankalin ta baya jikinta karfin hali kawai take. 



Dukawa sukayi suka gaida Sarauniya Zulaiƙa sannan Sarkin Yaki yace. "Ranki shidade tabbas mungaza wajen tahowa da wannan hatsabibin bokan ko magana bata haɗamu ba yace bazai zoba saidai akai mashi Malik acan dutsen kuka." yana gama magana ya dukar da kanshi kasa, cikin gadara Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta iso wajen Baru tana zagaye shi tace. "Meyasa baku kawo mani kanshi ba."?  



Shiru sukayi duk sun kasa magana tsawar data daka masu yasa Yazid yin magana. "Allah yataimakeki girma da izzar Sarrilkus ya girmi tunaninmu......wani irin gigitaccen mari ta ɗauke Yazid dashi duk wanda ke wajen nan saida ya girgiza cikin fushi tace. "Badan kana jarumi naba abin tunkaho dana sauke kanka kasa, Sarrilkus din banza da wofi ni Zulaiƙa sainayi maganin shi muddin ɗana Malik ya mutu tabbas saina kashe Baidulƙais sannan insare kan yarima Haishir inga duk duniya wanda ya isa yayi jayayya da Zulaiƙa." tarin da Malik keyi kamar zai sheƙe yasa Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta isa wajen shi da sauri ta rungumo shi jikin ta tana kiran sunan shi. "Malik mene haka dan Allah kayi hakuri zaka samu lfy insha Allah bazaka mutu ba." cikin shaƙewar murya yace. "Umma kitaimaka ki yafeman cikina keman ciwo bazan tashi ba dan Allah,Umma ina Bahijja dan Allah kitaimaka kirike Bahijja a matsayin ƴarki, inna mutu Abba kayafeman dan Allah."


Rike shi sukayi suna hawaye Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta tace. "kadaina fadan haka Malik zaka warke." bakaramin tashin hankali Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tashiga ba, fita tayi taje wajen bayin Masarautar Raifasu tasa akaita dukan su sannan aka samu katon dutse aka ɗaura saman kirjin Sarki Baidulƙais shiko yarima Haishir takobi tasa ta daɓa mashi sannan ta samu takarda ta rubuta wasika taba Lurwan yakai Masarautar Waisu..... 


Tana dawowa tasa Sarkin Yaki Baru suka ɗauki Malik suka shiga dashi wajen wani ɗaki sannan aka kira wani malami yafara ba Malikussaif magani da addu'oi yayi mashi sannan yasha kullin haka wannan malamin keba Malik rubutu yana sha jikin shi ke fidda hayaki danko yana jin jiki sosai, a wajen Lurwan kuwa yana isa Masarautar Waisu akaima mashi iso wajen Sarkin su, yana zuwa fa'da yakai gaisuwa kallan tuhuma Sarki yai mashi sannan yakai gaisuwa yace. "Inagaisuwa ga Sarki mai dole ni ɗan aikene daga  Masarautar Raifasu ta hanyar Sarauniya Zulaiƙa, wani mugun faɗuwar gaba ta riski Sarkin dan yasan wasa ya kubcema maza dagaji ba sa'a a cikin lamarin Sarki Baidulƙais gashi ya bada aron Jaruman shi harda ɗanshi mafi soyuwa a gareshi da kanshi ya mashi wasikar ya karanta. 




 ```Nasan ko bance komai ba tabbas sakona yasa ka girgiza yau ina taron Jaruman ka dakake tunkaho dashi ina ɗanka mafi soyuwa a gareka shin ko kasan da jinin waye nayi maka wannan wasikar ba nakowa bane sai jinin ɗanka Haishir dan haka kayi gaggawar zuwa ka ceci danka ina nan Masarautar Raifasu ta zama kalkashin ikona harda aminin naka wawa da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa kake magana ina sauraran ka nan da awa huɗu."```



Yana gama karatun ya saki takarda yana keto zufa a zahiri ɗagama Lurwan hannu yayi alamin yatafi yana fita ya tashi yafara bala'i. "Ni za'a cima mutunci ɗana za'a wulakanta a rubutoman wasika da jinin ɗana lallai Zulaiƙa wuyanki ya isa yanka, ko kunsan Zulaiƙa tayi nasara a Masarautar Raifasu tabbas dole mutashi tsaye nafahimci tana son tayi abinda kakarta Sarauniya Yahanasu taimana dole mudakile wannan yarinyar da rundunar mu, kuma dole in halaka MalikusSaif  da hannuna dan haka yau zanje Raifasu zanmika wuya insamu in amso ɗana tunkan inrashi inyaso daga baya zan masu dirar mikiya in wargaza duk wata zuria da shiri na Sarauniya Zulaiƙa da hannuna zan kacakaca da Zulaiƙa saidai a halin yanzun tashiga gaban mu, naso in halaka ɗan aikenta nabarshi ne sabida jinin ɗana Haishir ina kira ga Sarkin Yaki kashirya anjima zamutafi wajen Sarauniya Zulaiƙa." 


*wata sabuwa kuma fa, gadai MalikusSaif can rai hannu ga Allah, ya yarima Haishir shima babu tabbas😂dan Sarauniya Zulaiƙa naga bata da niyar kyaleshi, ga boka Sarrilkus yana jiran a kawo mashi MalikusSaif a faɗarshi ko kuma aljani ya ida shaye jininshi 😭, Abunda dai sai addu'a  fa😎*



*ina godiya da comment dinku gami da shirhinku musamman ma BAYI MA YA'YANE COMMENT GROUP, ku sani comment dinku kawai ya isheni balle ma na sauran💞💓*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*




Babi na 13




Sarauniya Zulaiƙa keta yawo tarasa abinda ke mata ɗadi a duniya har yanzun jikin Malik hayaki yakeyi ga wani irin zafi da yayi kamar wuta, fita Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ce tafita itada Saifulislam sukaje wajen Sarki Baidulƙais, iske shi sukayi zaune shida jamaar shi a wulakance  kamar wani bawa Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ce ta tsugunno a gaban Baidulƙais tace. "Kashirya kare rayuwar ka.? Koko kana san kabi ɗanka cikin gaggawa."


Wani kallo yaima  Zulaiƙa yace. "Tabbas bakiyi karya ba tun yaushe Baidulƙais ya sallama rayuwa kwara in mutu zaifi kinga muddin kika kasheni tabbas ɗanki shima zai mutu cikin wahala da bala'i ya kikagani ya Zulaiƙa ko nawa salan ya birgeki dan haka ni ban shakkar mutuwa." ya ida maganar yana mata kallon zaki gane kuranki gami da Murmushin mugunta. 


Cikin tsawa Zulaiƙa ta ɗaga takobi zata sare kanshi Saifulislam ya rike hannun ta, dukawa yayi yace. "Baidulƙais miyasa kake faɗar haka tabbas dole bazaka ji tsoron kisa ba saidai kasani bazaka so ka cigaba da mulkin Raifasu inko har hakane ni Saifulislam naimaka alkawalin cigaba da mulkin Masarautar ka amman da sharadi sai in zaka sa Boka Sarrilkus zuwa wajen mu dan karya wannan bakin tsafin, dan haka ban bukatar kace komai kayi tunani zuwa gobe ina sauraranka." yana idawa yakama hannun Zulaiƙa da tagaji da hasala suka tafi wata uwar dariya ya saki bayan tafiyar su sannan yace. "anzo wajen."

 _Inkon Allah kunji abinda Sarki Baidulƙais yace ko minene burin shi kudai kubiyoni muji yanda zata kaya a cikin wannan ƙayataccen labarin na bayima ƴaƴane_

Bawan shi Jazbir yace. "Ranka shidade yakake gani zamuyi da wannan azzalumar Sarauniyar dan ɗaukar fansar ɗanka? kuma magajin gobe yanzun haka zamu zauna a cikin Masarautar mu muzama bayi marassa ƴanci?  amman gaskia Gurfanai baikyau ta mana ba a wannan zubda kima da daraja ne na Masarautar Raifasu ace mace ta maidamu bayi ya kamata musan abinyi Gurfanai yabaka nasara."

Nazari Baidulƙais yayi duk da yanajin maganar Jazbir har cikin zuciyar shi saidai a wajen shi yana takaicin yanda har musulmai zasuci galaba akan su, lallai kam sun gaza wajen Gurfanai tabbas sunji kunya." 

Kamar yanda kuka sani Masarautar Raifasu masarauta ce data shahara wajen gabatar da sarauta sai dai Masarautar ba a daular musulunci suke ba suna bautar wani katon icce dake cikin Masarautar baki ɗayan su, shiyasa suke rayuwa yanda suka ga dama, sannan ba abinda suka tsana irin addinin musulunci wannan dalilin yasa suka ɗauki tsana da karan batta akan Masarautar Daborah tunda suka anshi musulunci musamman Saifulislam da Malikussaif acewar su ta hanyar mahaifin shi har daular musulunci takafu a babbar Masarautar su ta nahiyar su ta kasar Gambia, wannan kenan.

Sai dai Masarautar tanada mayaƙa duk da sunsamu naƙasu akan artabar da sukayi da Sarki Jabbar na III wannan dalilin yasa suka kara tuso Masarautar Daborah a gaba suna san suga bayanta musamman yarima MalikusSaif. 


 ```wannan kaɗan daga cikin tarihin Masarautar Raifasu da kudurin su ga Masarautar Daborah kuma ga yarima MalikusSaif ya kashe yarima Husbarulƙais ɗan Sarkin Baidulƙais bari muga kalar cakwakiyar da zata wakana a tsakankanin waɗan nan Masarautu da suka tuso Masarautar Daborah a gaba muje zuwa```

Sarki Baidulƙais yace. "Tabbas Jazbir kafaɗi gaskia  dole muɗauki mataki ga wannan jakar Zulaiƙar saina maida Zulaiƙa ta zama baiwata kuma saita zama abin tausayi yanzun abinda baku sani ba dole sai Zulaiƙa tazo tanemi inmata hanyar da boka Sarrilkus zaizo gareta kodan rayuwar ɗanta nan ne wasan zaifara dan haka karka damu dani take zancen itafa macace dole inmaida hankalin ta kamar na kifi ko kaza na rantse da girman Gurfanai saina halaka  Zulaiƙa da hannuna tabbas ku rubuta ku'aje da Baidulƙais take magana." ya ida maganar yana wata irin dariya da mugunta da ƙeta. 



A ɓangaren Zulaiƙa kuwa fitowa tayi tahau saman doki ta warci takobi ita da Saifulislam gudu kawai suke shekawa kamar ba gobe fita sukayi cikin Masarautar Raifasu suka tunkari bakin dajin Gamki suna shiga cikin kurgunmin dajin ba abinda suke gani sai tsabagen duhu ga wata uwar kara da hayaniya na tashi gwanin tsoro amman haka Saifulislam suka cigaba da tafiya cikin dajin ko tsoro basuji wani uban wulgi suka gani ya wuce ta gaban su, cikin gafin nama Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta kai uwar sara da takobi, take takobin ta lauye ji sukayi baki ɗaya anɗago su sama daga saman doki anyi wurgi dasu saman katan icce Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta faɗa tashi tayi da sauri ta ɗauko lauyayyar wuƙar duk wani irin bakin jini daskararre, taɓeta akayi ta faɗi kasa akadinga janta kasa gashi bata ga mai jantaba, da sauri Saifulislam ya tasu yai saiti ɗana kibiya a saitin da akejan Sarauniya Zulaiƙa da wani uban ihu yaji ankwaɗa sannan ya rungumo Zulaiƙa sai addua yakeyi itama binshi takeyi sai wulge wulge da ihu ake masu, duk tsoro ya kama zuciyar su, wata uwar dariya suka tsinkaya ankai minti talatin ana abu guda  sannan sukaga wani irin ingarman zaki a gaban su, a gigice Saifulislam ya zaro takobi ya wullama wannan zakin ya sameshi a kafa wata irin kuwwa da gurnani zakin nan yayi ya tunkaro Saifulislam, cikin gafin nama Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta ɗauko takobi tayi saitin wuyan zakin nan ta ɗana a saman makoshin shi rigijib ya faɗi sai shure shure yakeyi wani tankameman mutun sukaga zakin ya koma cikin kankanin lokaci ya ɓace, rike hannun Saifulislam Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi ta rufe idanta da sauri suka ɗauki takobin suka fara gudu zasu fita cikin dajin Gamki sake wurgi akayi dasu suka gansu wajen wani ruwa ga maganganu marar sa dadi anayi cikin kausassar murya akace. "Zulaiƙa kinyi wauta dahar kafarki ta tako Masarauta ta batare da Malikussaif  ba kinzo ne dan ki ɗauki fansa to yau dinnan saikin san kinzo turakar Sarrilkus Hhhhhhh aljani ɗan tsitu aikin ka yanzun zai fara." kankame juna sukayi itada Saifulislam sukaita addua kamar ba gobe koda aljani ɗan tsitu yayo kan su Saifulislam, jisukayi guguwar ta lafa komai na dajin yai shiru buɗe ido sukayi sukaita addua ji sukayi magana a firgice. " zasu ƙonani ya Sarrilkus kataimake ni nakasa shan jinin su, ihuhu wulakantattu ne kaimani rai inbisu har masaukin su dan kashe su."

Take wata guguwa ta turnike kamar me duk sun ruɗe da kyal Saifulislam ya lalubo takobi ya rike a hannun shi ji sukayi kamar suna sararin subhana cikin kaftawar ido suka gansu a zubdasu saman wani yayi ga uban haske rufe idanun su sukayi sai hasken wuta ketashi ji sukayi ance. "Badai a dajin Gamki zaku dinga yimana addua ba, da kanmu zamu halaka ku dan haka ku shirya zuwan tawwagar Sarrilkus sai munga bayan ku, hhhhhhh.'' take sukaga wannan hasken wutar ya ɓace cikin tsoro Sarauniya Zulaiƙa takara rungume Saifulislam addua yai tayi ɗaukar wukar da suka yanki wulgin da akai masu yayi  sukaita tafiya mai nisa sannan suka hangi dawakan su, hawa sukayi duk zazzaɓi ya rufe Sarauniya Zulaiƙa haka suka tunkari Masarautar Raifasu cikin kaɗuwa da shakku fatan su susamu ɗansu Yarima Malik cikin aminci.

Suna isa cikin Masarautar suka wuce dakin da Malikussaif ke kwance su Yazid ne atareda shi sai Bahijja dakema Malik tausan kafafu zuwa yanzun kam takai shekara bakwai a duniya yanda sukaga su Saifulislam, saida suka tsorota jikin su duk jini kowa tashi yayi akai gaisuwa sannan malamin dakema MalikusSaif magani yace. "Ko andace da jinin ko wane halitta ne na dajin Gamki."? 

Jikin Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ba kyarma tace."andace."....."Malan andace sai dai bamusan ko andace da dai dai ba gadai takobin nan hannuna." cewar Saifulislam ya mikama malamin takobin karɓa yayi yasata cikin wata ruwa sannan ya tofa addua ya ɗauko ta sai hayaki takeyi ya buɗe sukar da akaima Malik a ciki ya zuba wannan ruwan dake ɗiga take wajen yayi baki sai halbe halbe Malik yakeyi yana fidda kumfa a bakin shi ga hayaki na fita baki ɗaya hankalin kowa ya tashi  tsabar  kaɗuwa hatta malamin da sauri Zulaiƙa ta rungumo MalikusSaif  jikin ta shiko Saifulislam ɗauko Malik yayi yana tayi mashi addu'a ji sukayi cak bugun kirjin Malik ya tsaya ko numfashi baiyi, da karfi Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta girgiza  MalikusSaif  wadda zufa ya gama wanketa dan tsoro da fargaba, ganin yanda su Yazid ke kwalla yasa Bahijja ta kama hannun MalikusSaif  tana kuka tana kiran. "Ya Malik katashi kada kabarmu." itako Zulaiƙa ta gama kiɗimewa da ganin halin da Malik yashiga kamar matattace sake girgiza shi tayi ta fasa uban kuka tana cewa....... 


*Salon kuyi man kuka haka labarina yazo sai kuyi hakuri dan koni Yar'mutan kankia mutuwa zanyi balle MalikusSaif 👹🌚*



 *Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*

*Sakon ta'aziya zuwa ga yar'uwa kuma ƙawata dan kin wuce na kira da suna Fan sai da yar'uwata Yar'gatan Momyn sultan, Allah ya jikan Baba yasa mutuwa ta zamo hutu a gareshi, ya jikan dukan al'umar musulmai Amin🙏*


Babi na 14





"Malik kaitaimaki rayuwata kaine farin cikina karka barni Ya Malik kaine gatan Masarauta Daborah na shiga ukku." wani mugun tari Malik yayi ba kaukautawa baki ɗaya bayin Masarautar su suka zagaye su sai kuka sukeyi masu Addu'a nayi kowa da abinda ke damunsa shiru ne ya biyo baya duk wanda kagani a cikin su kasan yana cikin matsanan cin damuwa Sarkin yaki Baru ne yafita wajen Baidulƙais yaje yana zuwa ya shako shi ya kawo shi wajen MalikusSaif  take Jarumai suka zagaje Baidulƙais da takubba magana Sarkin yaki yafara a fusace. "Ya Baidulƙais nasan kanada sirrinkan ganin Sarrilƙus dan haka ka kirawo shi zuwa ga yarima mai adalci mai kishin bayi da sauran talakawa karkayi anfani da wannan damar dan cimma burin ka saidai kasani karya wannan sihirin shi zaisa ka koma da mulkin Masarautar Raifasu kamar yanda kadimul islam ya faɗa."cikin girman kai da gadara yayi  dariya mai sauti kamar wani mahaukaci ya kalli Zulaiƙa tana jikin Malik sai hakin kuka takeyi wanda gwara gawa da Malik a halin yanzun, saida ya gama dariya mai isar sa sannan yayi tafi da hannun shi yace. "Zulaiƙa harkin saduda kenan ya kikaga ni ainafaɗa maki da inrayu da inmutu duk ɗaya naimaki alkawali yanda narasa Farin ciki kema kinyi bankwana dashi koda kuwa yarima Malik yarayu ya kikagani Zulaiƙa ina baki Shawara kibi shawarata tunkan dare yaimaki dan haka barganin kunci galaba akaina Zulaiƙa kiyi tunanin dole in miki biyayya aaa kinyi kuskure a halin yanzun ke kenema a wajena dan haka imhar kunasan yarima MalikusSaif  ya rayu sai kunbi sharuddana sannan."


Cikin gamsuwa da garaje Safulislam yace. "Mun amince nidai rayuwar ɗana nike bukata tafiyemani komai kafaɗi ko minene zan baka ya Baidulƙais." tafiya yakeyi cikin gadara yaje kusa da Malikussaif ya shafi fuskar shi sannan yace. "MalikusSaif  kenan ya kagani gwara matacce dakai ko? Naji abunda kace inason fansar ran ɗana Husbarul shine bukatata da farko inhar kunji zakuyi mani ita Shikenan ko yau din an Sarrilƙus yana iya zuwa saidai kuma dole ku tabbatar dani a kan mulkina da Masarauta ta, sannan inada bukatar bayi daga cikin Masarautar ku shikenan kungama mani komai ya kuka ga bukatar tawa saidai karku manta ku danganta ta da rayuwar ɗanku MalikusSaif da kuma bukatar tawa." shiru ne ya biyo baya duk da kowa yasan bukatar tasu mai wahala ce amman hakam baisa sunyi kasa a guiwa ba cewa Zulaiƙa tayi. "Sai dai kasani za'ai maka fansar danka amman bazan baka bayina ba sai dai za'a fanso maka konawa ake saida bayi a kasuwa in haka yaimaka shikenan."

Murmushin raini Baidulƙais yayi sannan ga girgiza kai. "Zulaiƙa banda bukatar wani bawa akasuwa daki bani bawa dari a waje gwara kubani bawa goma a cikin rundunar bayin ki,amman inkina ganin bukatata tafi rayuwar ɗanki mahaimmanci shikenan saiki zaɓa Zulaiƙa, zan iya tafiya  kafin kiyi tunanin da zai anfanar dake."


Juyawa yayi zai fita Safulislam yace. "Karka tafi Baidulƙais ai bukatar ka tafi komai sauki a cikin bukatar da kanema sai dai bayi kamar nawa kake so."?  wata irin shu'umin murmushi yayi sannan yakara kusa da Safulislam yace. "Naji dadin abunda kace sai dai a wannan fagen so nike inba Sarauniya Zulaiƙa umurnina tabi ya Abu Malik."

Kamar Zulaiƙa bazatayi magana ba tace. "Abinda mijina yace shine zaɓina dan haka kafada bayi nawa kake so." dariyar iskanci yayi mai sauti sannan ya kuma cewa. "Kina kyautawa kanki da kikabi umurnina Zulaiƙa bayima goma sun isheni, saidai inasan ki haɗaman da amintattun bayin ku guda biyu saidai banyarda kici amana taba yanzun nan zakiga Sarrilƙus yazo har wajen Malik ya kikace Zulaiƙa."


Cikin gansuwa ta amince take Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tasa Sarkin taska ya dibo kudi masu yawa wanda basu iya ƙayyadece su taba Baidulƙais sannan taba Sarkin bayi Abdulmajid  umurnin bashi bayi goma haka ya lissafo cikin rundunar bayin su, yaba Baidulƙais sai dai ya nuna guda a cikin su yace  baimai ba, cikeda da kosawa Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace ya canza da kanshi abin mamaki take Baidulƙais ya nuna BAHIJJA, dake gefen kafar Malik dan kamar ita ke jinyar Yarima Malik, duk yanda Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta tsani Bahijja saida taki amincewa da rabuwa da BAHIJJA musamman Safulislam danko Binta kuka tafarayi mai cinrai ganin za'a rabata da ƴarta duk wanda ke wajen saida ya girgiza  banda Bahijja da batasan inda maganar ta dosa ba, Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace. "Kacanza da wata amman banda Bahijja." cikin mugunta yace shifa da Bahijja zai  tafi kuka wuwi Binta keyi take Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tafara jin tausan Binta a rayuwar ta ganin  yanda za'a rabata da ƴarta saboda ta ɗanɗani ɗacin rabuwa da ɗa ganin zata rabu da ɗanta, cikin tausayi Zulaiƙa tace. "Gaskia muddin zaka ɗauki Bahijja saidai kahaɗa da mahaifiyar ta Binta katafi dasu." duk wanda. Ke wajen saida ya gamsu hatta Abdulmajid, maganar da Baidulƙais yayi shiya dawo dasu a cikin tunanin su. "Bansan mahaifiyar ta kurike ita nike bukatan koda  ace zaku hanani kudin fansar ran ɗana, a sanyaye Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace. "Binta duk da kasancewar ki baiwa ta wadda nafi tsana saboda wasu dalilai kinji abinda Baidulƙais yace, amman ba zan yake hukunci ba har sai abunda kike ce shi za'ayi dan ba zanso na rabaki da yar'ki ba." cikin sanyin murya Binta tace. "Haba ya uwar dakina wannan shike nuna rabani da ya'ta har abada kenan ya zanyi inama banzo abaiwa ba inama mutuwa nayi na huta inama banga wannan ranar ba saidai banda zaɓi sai abunda kukayi lallai Bahijja tun jinjirarki ajali ke bibiyar ki Bahijja ga mai tausanki bai lafiya bare ya baki kulawa ta musamman Allah yazama gatanki marainiyar Allah gashi anrabaki da iyayenki Bahijja tabbas har yanzun nakasa yarda da maganar da Yarima MalikusSaif  keyi na bayima 'ƴa'ƴane tabbas banga ranar da bayi zasu zama 'ƴa'ƴaba." ta karashe maganar tana kwalla da takaici sannan ta rungume ƴarta sai kuka sukeyi. 

Bayan kwana biyu, aka tashi lamarin Malik yakara ta'azzara hankalin kowa yagama tashi musamman Sarauniya Zulaiƙa da tagama fidda rai da rayuwar ɗanta Malik tashi yayi yana ta aman jini bakin kirin kamar gawayi ga numfashin shi yana ta ɗaukewa kamar zai mutu baki daya sun zagaye Malik cikin kankani lokaci banda kuka ba abinda Binta keyi ita da Bahijja ga Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tsoro da fargaba sun sa tafara suma danko Saifulislam yana rungume da Malik da yagama sheɗewa dan wahala kowa yagama sadudu da lamarin ana cikin haka Binta tafita da gudu zuwa wajen Baidulƙais tana isa ta zube sai uban kuka takeyi cikin gadara yace. "Baiwa mike tafe dake har haka."? cikin sarkewar murya Binta tace. "Na'amince da Bahijja tazama baiwarka kataimake mu karmu rasa MalikusSaif  a halin yanzun yana bukatar mu sadaukar da duk wani farin cikin mu." cikin jindadi yayi dariya yace. "Tabbas baiwa sai dai muje inji ta bakin  Sarauniyar ki." haka suka tafi yana gaba tana biye har ɗakin da Malikussaif  yake kwance lokacin Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tagama galabaita ta suma yafi ukku da kyal ta farfado sama da awa ukku sannan ta buɗe idonta ta azasu akan Baidulƙais da sauri ta mike kamar wata zakanya ta cakumi wuyan Baidulƙais takai mashi wani wahalalan duka takara dunkulo wani Saifulislam ya rike ta sannan tace. "Uban wa yakawo ka wajen ɗana kazo danka karasa mani shi."!? cikin sauri Binta tace "ya shugaba ta ni nataho dashi dan ceto ran MalikusSaif  fansa ga ƴata *BAHIJJA* kodan kulawar da yake mata sannan rayuwar Malik arziki ce ga kowa Allah kaɗai yasan wanda zai taimaka nan gaba ciki ma yake sai dai ina fargabar makomar addinin ƴata, ammn na amince ya ɗauki Bahijjaa matsayin baiwar shi." takarashe maganar tana kuka mai tsuma zuciya, mikewa Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi ta matso kusa da Baidulƙais tace. "Zamubaka Bahijja saidai kasani inada sharuɗɗan da zan gindaya maka sune na ɗaya karkayi anfani da damar daka samu a wajen mu wajen sauyama Bahijja addini ko kuma laalcewar mutuncin ta, sannan karka manta da yanda ka anshi Bahijja bayan fansar ta da zanyi da bayi dubu akan ta amman ka nace sai ita dole kabata wannan damar da nagindaya maka mutuncin ta da addinin ta, kaikayi naka itama tayi nata inhar ka amince ni Zulaiƙa zan cika maka alkawali."


Shu'umin murmushi yayi cikin gamsuwa yace "na amince fatana Bahijja ta dawo a karkashin  ikon Masarautar Raifasu hakan zaisa zuciya ta daina yin ƙuna akan rashin ɗana duk da  fansar shi da zakuyi."

Saifulislam yace. "Ni ban amince ba da fansar Bahijja a baiwa ba, akan Malik duka duka nawa Bahijja take da za'a rabata da mahaifiyar ta dan haka kafaɗi ko nawane ni Saifulislam nayi alkawali zan baka fatana abar Bahijja wajen mahaifiyar ta." wani kallo Baidulƙais yayi ma Saifulislam na baka da hankali inada burin nawa zan rabu da Bahijja baku da wayau a zahiri cewa yayi. "Aaaa ban bukatar dukiya ita nike bukata ta zama hadimata duk da naga ba shekarune da itaba amman ai zatayi anfani inhar baku shirya ba shikenan ku rike ɗanku da baiwar ku ni kuma inrike damata."


Da sauri Binta tace "Allah yataimaki shugaba na kayi hakuri kabar wannan maganar kome zai wuce inda har Allah yakawo Bahijja a baiwa ba ko shakka tana cikin kalubale ka rabu da ita da sannu zata saba da rashin iyaye zan mata addua dacewa akoda yaushe kamar yanda mahaifin keyi mata, kabari a ceci rayuwar Yarima MalikusSaif  narokeka."


Saifulislam baikuma cewa komai ba sai goge kwalla da yayi da tausan Bahijja a cikin zuciyar shi, nan Saifulislam yasa aka ɗauko wata jikkar kudi akaba Baidulƙais sannan aka yarje mashi Bahijja sai kuka su Yazid sukeyi shiko sai jindadi yakeyi mikama bawan shi jikar kudin yayi sannan yace. "Inada sharadina akan ku matukar kuka samu maganin cutar Malik ina gargaɗar kuda kuyi gaggawar barin Masarauta ta, sannan karku taɓaman mutane na da bayin ku da kuka bani guda tara duk da Bahijja goma in kun amince yanzun boka Sarrilkus zai bayyana a gabanku." take Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta amimce ganin yanda jikin Malik yakara tsanani, wani zobe ya ɗebe a hannun shi bakikkirin ya murza take wajen ya hargitse sai wata uwar ƙara ke tashi kayayyakin ɗakin suna ta fashe wa suna komawa toka kowa ya gama hargitse wa dan tsoro wata uwar guguwa da walkiyar tsafi ta turnike wajen Baidulƙais ne cikin tsoro da ya kiɗime mashi jiki yace........ 






*Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

Babi na 15


Baidulƙais yace."Sarrilƙus Danzir aljanni ɗansitu bayyanarku agareni dace ku nike jira hhhhhhh ihuhuhuhhhhh ni Baidulƙais."
Wata uwar tsawa aka saki mai razanarwa sannan wani katon toron giwa ya bayyana cikin kankanin lokaci ya rikiɗe ga koma tafkeken gadangare bayan shi da wasu manya manya mutane bakake guguwar takai minti talatin sannan wannan kadangaren ya koma wani basamudan mutun kowa saida ya tsorata sannan wata walkiyar tsafi ta taso ya ɗauki Bahijja da Baidulƙais su kuma sauran matsafan suka ɗauke bayin da akaba shi da Haishir wata magana ce aka fara mai ruɗarwa akace. "Sarauniya Zulaiƙa kin cika alkawali dan haka ga maganin ciwon MalikusSaif nan dan haka kusan yanda zakuyi anfani dashi muddin kuka samu matsala Malik zai iya mutuwa dan haka nan da awa ukku, ku gudu daga Masarautar Raifasu ko in turo maku rundunar aljanu hahhhhhh saini Sirrilƙus hhhhhhhh." 

Take ya ɓace sannan aka saki maganin ya faɗo a saman MalikusSaif da gudu Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tabi wannan walkiyar har sun ɓace, take Binta ta faɗi a sume ba rai dan fargaba Abdulmajid ya rungume matarsa ya saki marayan kuka sannan suka ɗauki maganin a daburce Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta mikama malamin dake ba Malik maganin lokacin hankalin Malik yagama futa daga jikin shi kowa yayi kanshi aka farayi mashi maganin cikin fargaba. 

koda su Baidulƙais suka ɓace ba inda suka sauka sai cikin ƙurgunmin dajin Gamki sai walkiya wajen keyi ga tartsatsin wuta yana tarwatsa,danko Bahijja tagama suma dan fargaba da tsoro baki daya jikin ta yayi sanyi ganin wata halitta da tayi agaban ta yasa ta kankame jikin ta tafara kuka tace. "Kumai dani wajen Umman ta tsoro nikeji!." fashewa da kuka tayi tsawa Baidulƙais yayi mata duk tsoro da fargaba ya gama kama ta sai ƙyarma takeyi ganin wata halitta tayi agabanta ita ba mage ba kuma ba kare ba addu'a Bahijja tafara a cikin zuciya tsabar firgita yasa tafara  fita waje bata sani ba, wata irin razanan nar tsawa Sarrilƙus ya dakama Bahijja har fitsari ya fara zubo mata a wando dan tsoro jin wata muguwar tsawa da magana mai kaushi tafara magana. "Ke yarinya kibar yi mana wannan abun naku inba hakaba yanzun nan zan maidaki ƙura abin shekarwa kinaji na." 

Da sauri ta ɗaga kai alamar eh haka taita kukan zuci ga tsoro ya kama zuciyar ta da fargaba, a wajen su Malik kuwa maimagani tsoro ya hana saka ma Malik a jiki gudun kadda a samu matsala a rasa shi addu'a yafara yana tofawa sannan ya rufe ido ya saka maganin wata uwar tsawace ta karaɗe ɗakin gwinin tsoro cikin jikin Malik hayaki ne baki ke fita sannan wani zare baki ya fita yayi sama ya ɓace sai haki yakeyi ga zufa tagama rufe MalikusSaif wata irin kyarma ya kama Malik kamar zai mutu, rufe shi Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi sukaita yimata addu'a kowa da abin da yake saƙawa a cikin zuciyar shi. 


Bayan wasu awanni Yarima Malik ne yayi ajiyar zuciya mai karfin gaske tareda salati abakin shi ya buɗe ido yana ta kallan mutane kamar wani baƙo can yace. "Umma ina nike mi nikeyi anan wajen."? 


Cikin farin ciki Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta rungume Malik, shima Saifulislam cikin farin ciki ya rungume ɗan nashi sai murna  yakeyi haka kowa yaita murna kamar ba gobe, dan murna haka malamin nan yasa akayi addu'a aka godema Allah, kuɗi  Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta mikama wannan malamin sai godiya sukema mashi haka ya tafi shiko Malik ruwa su Yazid suka haɗa mashi yayi wanka ya sake kaya, Binta ta shigo gaishe da Malik cikin sakin fuska ya amsa sannan yace. "Umman Bahijja ya fama da jama'a ina Bahijja batazo duba yayan nata ba."?  Cikin kulawa tace. "Nabarota wajen Baban ta, daman ta gaji."

"Gaskia ai kwara ta huta ina ruwan Bahijja ai tana kokari."  murmushi Binta tayi sannan ta tashi zata fita tsaya taga Sarauniya Zulaiƙa a bakin daki, cikin sakin fuska Binta ta gaisheta ta amsa karasowa tayi cikin ɗakin tareda ta dafa Malik sannan tace ya ɗauki abinda yake bukata zasu wuce gida, cikin jin dadi yace. "Yaww Umma yakamata mu koma masarautar mu hakanan."

Labari sukaci gaba dayi cikin so da kauna da misalin karfe biyu kowa ya shirya harda bayin su, suka fito suka ɗauki hanyar tafiya Daborah ko tsinke basu ɗauka ba da sunan ganimar yaki, itako Binta daurewa kawai takeyi hankalin ta na wajen ƴarta fatan ta Sarki Baidulƙais ya dawo da ƴarta a cikin Raifasu tasan insha Allah zata samu abinda takeso tsoro takeyi kar a halakar mata ƴa da wannan tunanin ne suka fita Masarautar  Raifasu zuwa Masarautar Daborah. 


tafiya sukayi mai nisan zango sannan suka isa Masarautar Daborah Sarkin kofa ya buɗe masu suka shiga kowa ya nufi sashen su bayan aje dawakan su da sukayi, itako Binta da Abdulmajid turakar bayi suka wuce su dasu ladidi da sauran bayin da akai tafiya dasu, jikin su a sage duk guiwowin su sunyi sanyi musammn da suga shiga ɗakin su sukaga ba Bahijja kuka Binta ta fasa maicin rai sai lallashin ta Abdulmajid keyi su Ladidi ne suka shigo sukaci gaba da ba Binta hakuri kayan wasan da Malik ya taɓa sayama Bahijja  ta ɗauko tana kallo sai kewar ƴarta takeyi ga zuciyar ta na dukan tara tara magana tafara cikin tashin hankali "Kaicona ni Binta nayi asarar bada ƴata da nayi munɓata lokuta masu nisan gaske wajen tanadama Bahijja farin ciki ashe a banza tunda mungaza tsayawa mu jajirce mu nunama duniya itama ƴace kamar kowa amman mungaza tabbas Bahijja bazata taɓa yafemana ba batayi dacen iyaye ba, kaicona."
Cikin kosawa ga bakin cikin rabuwa da ƴarsa Abdulmajid yace. "Haba Binta me kike faɗi anya kina cikin hankalin ki da tunani kuwa kinsan abinda kikeyi? Menene a cikin dan munbada ƴarmu zatayi bautar baiwa a wata Masarauta karfa kimanta Bahijja baiwa ce, Malik fa? Ko kin manta mu dukan mu a kalkashin ikon Sarauniya Zulaiƙa muke, karfa ki manta mu bayine kuma bamuda ƴanci, ina laifi dan munbada Bahijja akan fansar Malik karki manta yaron nan da ya fanshi ƴarki a lokacin da gaf take da mutuwa sannan ya fanshi iyayen ta duk dan farin cikinta damu kanmu kodan haka ba mun sadaukar da Bahijja ba akan Malikussaif ba, dan Allah kidaina wannan maganar sai indinga yimaki kallan butulu, kisan me yadace kifaɗa matata addu'a itace gatan Bahijja kuma da yardar Allah saita dawo cikin Masarautar Daborah."


Cikin jindadi da samun kwarin guiwa Binta tayi murmushi sannan tace. "Maraba da samun miji haziƙi jajirtacce kuma fasihi lallai Baban Bahijja kacika kutun harda rabin mutun yanzun nakarajin tafiyar Bahijja akwai nasara tunda Yarima Malik ya tashi sai addua zumuyi ma yarmu Bahijja tabbas ina jinjina mijina."


Rungume ta yayi yana shafar bayan ta sannan su Ladiyo suka zauna akai ta ƴar fira dasu kowa na faɗin albarkacin bakin shi har dare yayi kowa yayi sallama da junan su, suka tafi makwanci kafin zuwa asuba su koma bakin aiki kamar kullun...


Bayan kwana ukku, dajin Gamki ne yayi tsit bakajin kukan komai sai tsuntsaye ga duhu saidai duhun bai hana kaga na kusa dakai ba, Bahijja ce zaune saman wata itaciya ta maƙure waje ɗaya duk da ta daina jin tsoron dajin sosai ganin ba abinda ke cutar da lafiyar ta, wani bakin mutun ne ya kawo mata wani kalar abinci ba kyaun gani ya aje mata,  ko magana bai mata ba ya juya ya tafi tana kallan abincin nan ta rufe ido sai hawaye takeyi shiko Baidulƙais yana tareda da boka Sarrilƙus suna magana a saman tabirin dutsen kuka cema boka yayi. "Gurfanai ya taimakeka uban gidana yakamata ace yau nakoma Masarauta ta dan cigaba da gabatar da mulkina tareda sauran bayin nan, dan samun bayin Zulaiƙa bakaramin nasara bane a wajen mu munsamu babbar dama da zamu cimma burin mu akan Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta Yarima Malik."
Shiru Sarrilƙus yayi sannan yace."Kwarai kuwa shiyasa nace ka anshi wannan yarinyar amatsayin baiwa cikon ta goma akwai nasarori da yawa a tareda ita sannan taimaka aikin bauta na baiwa itada sauran bayin ka, yanzun zansa amaidaka Masarautar ka kai dasu komi kenan zan turo maka aljani ɗantsitu inkana bukatar taimako ina tareda kai, sannan duk da sunbaka fansar ran ɗanka Hasburu karsuyi tunanin angama da hannun ka zaka kashe Malik kaidai ku cigaba da yima Gurfanai biyayya akan ikona, hhhhhhh."

Shima Baidulƙais ɗin dariya yayibcikin jindadi, wani magani Sarrilƙus ya busa saiga wani bakin aljani ɗankarami ya bayyana a gaban Sarrilƙus cikin bada umurni ya nuna mashi Baidulƙais, kafin kaftawar ido suka ɓace harda bayin nashi ba inda suka ɓulla sai tsakiyar fa'da nan ɗantsitu ya diresu cikin kaushin murya yace. "Baidulƙais na cika  umurnin shugaba na sai dai kasani inada sharuɗɗan da zanginda yamaka, anjima da dare ayanka akuya mai gajeren bindi sannan ta kasance kai ido ɗaya ce ku yanka ta, a tsakiyar duhun dare shine kariyar mulkin ka, dan haka kakiyaye."

Yana gama faɗar haka ya ɓace bat, tashi Baidulƙais yayi ya hau saman karagar mulkin sa yana rufe ido da hannu ya nuna Bahijja alamar tazo tasowa tayi duk tsoro ya kamata duk da basujin maganar da aljani ɗantsitu yayi da Baidulƙais sudai sun gansu wani wajen na daban amman tana zargin Masarautar  Raifasu ce, koda ta isa dukawa tayi ta gaidashi ɗaukar kafar shi yayi ya ɗora saman kafaɗar Bahijja yaimata nuni da ido duk da ba wani sanin aikin bauta tayi ba, amman taga Umman ta naima Sarauniya Zulaiƙa haka, itama cigaba da danna kafar Sarki Baidulƙais tayi haka taitamashi tausa, mikewa yayi zaitafi baki daya bayin dake wajen suka baje ya takasu ya dinga yi har ya fita suma tashi sukayi ya haɗasu da Sarkin bayin Masarautar shi suka ya basu masauki da ayyukan su...... 

*∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆*

A Masarautar Daborah kuwa Yarima MalikusSaif ne ya shirya cikin wasu kayan saki na alfarma, fitowa yayi  cikin haiba da kwarjini ya tafi sashen Saifulislam iske mahaifin nashi yayi zaune saman sallaya da alama sallah ya gama bayan ya zauna ya gaishe da mahaifin nashi cikin kulawa ya kalli ɗan nashi yace. "Malik ya karfin jikin naka."?....."da sauki."ya amsa mahaifin nashi sannan yace. "Abbuh daman inasan kanemani alfarma wajen Umma inasan Bahijja ta fara yiman hidima." 


Sake kallan MalikusSaif  yayi sannan yace. "Malik dole sai Bahijja zatayi maka hidima ɗana."? Murmushi yayi sannan yace.  "Aaa Abbuh saidai nafi jin hankalina zaifi kwanciya akan sauran bayin."shima Saifulislam murmushi yayi sannan yace."Hakane sai dai kasani kasama ranka hakuri da Bahijj." a razane Malik ya kalli mahaifin nashi sannan yace!!!!



 *Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*


Babi na 16


Cikin lailashi MalikusSaif yace. "Abbuh miyasa inasan inba Bahijja kulawa dan Allah kaima karkace haka, kataimaka mani inasan intaimaketa." Kallon ɗa'n nashi Saifulislam yayi sannan yace."Nafahimce ka Malik nikaina banki na rike Bahijja a matsayin ƴata ba sai dai kasani bamu da zaɓi dole muhakura da kaddarar dake bibiyr mu." cikin mamaki MalikusSaif yace. "Abbuh wace kalar kaddara ce har haka? Take neman shigowa rayuwarmu."!? Ya ida maganar cikin tsoro dan rainshi ya fara bashi babu lafiya. 

Kama hannun MalikuSaif yayi sannan yace."Kwarai kuwa na rashin Bahijja ko kasan Bahijja ce ita fansa akan ka? har aka samu maganin ciwon ka?  tana hannun Sarki Baidulƙais kasan ba lallai tana Masarautar Raifasu ba, muna kyautata zatan tana hannun boka Sarriƙus wanna dalilin yasa nace kafidda rai da samun Bahijja a hadimar ka tunda ba a kalkashin mulkin Masarautar daborah take ba."

Wasu hawaye masu zafi suka wanke mashi fuska yace. "yanzun shikenan ba rana ba lokacin da Bahijja zata zama ƴantatar ƴa?  shikenan Abbuh sabida bukatar ku dan kuga ɗanku ya rayu a matsayin mutun zaku bada Bahijja dan tana baiwa hakan shine adalci ya mahaifiyar ta zatayi."? 

Cikin tausasa murya ya ƙara rike hannun Malik yace. "ɗana bamu matsama Binta dan tabada ƴarta ba da kanta ta zabi rabuwa da ƴarta da fansar ranka."
"Abbuh duk da haka tayi ne domin darajar ku a matsayin ku na iyayen
ɗakin ta kuma baiwar ku dole tayi hakuri har ta ɗauki Bahijja."
"tabbas Malik kayi gaskia sai dai kasani ni kaina kan Bahijja sai inda karfina ya kare." cewa yayi. "Abbuh yanzun zan tafi Masarautar Raifasu zandawo da Bahijja a tare dani koda zan rasa raina alkawali."


 _Wata sabuwa gadai abinda Malik ya faɗa koya lamarin zai kasance muje zuwa_

Saifulislam yace. "Aaa ɗana kada kafaɗi haka Masarautat Raifasu akwai sihiri da tsafi a cikin ta bayyananne kabari sai munsami illimin tunkaran Masarautat ta hanyar wargaza boka Sarrrilƙus saboda shike iko da Masarautar Raifasu, kaga dole munemi limin tarihi dan haka kayi hakuri muga yanda lamuran zasu kasance, sai mu tunkare su."


Hawaye keta zirara a idon Malik yana jin tausan Bahijja a zuciyar shi ko cewa yayi har abada bawa bawane baida wani ƴanci ko galihu, goge mashi kwallan idonshi Saifulislam yayi, nan yakama jan Malik da fira saidai baki ɗaya hankalin Malik na wajen Bahijja....

*∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆*

Masarautar Raifasu bayine keta aiki kamar ba gobe ga uban dukan su anayi da bulalai kamar jakai haka suketa kwasar duwatsu suna kaiwa cikin wani katon waje ba abinda kake gani sai tarin rairayi a cikin shi, duk wanda ya ɗauki dutsen zai ɗaura shi saman kirjin shi dogawara rike da zabga zabgan bulalai suna dukan su, danko jikin su duk ya fashe sai jini ke zuba ga uwar rana amman ba'a ɗaga masu ba ko abinci basu tsayawa suci, ranar haka bayin Raifasu sukayini sunayi wasu har suma sukeyi dan azaba da wahala, haka za'aita dukan su harsai sun farfaɗo suci gaba da aiki,  a wajen Bahijja kuwa tsaye take a gefen Sarki Baidulƙais inda wata baiwar shi takawo mashi abinci yaba Bahijja umurni ta zuba mashi ta tsugunna ta bashi a abinci kallan banza yaimata yaki ƙarɓa itako cewa tayi. "Ya shugabana tuba nikeyi ga abincin ka nan ya karaso kaci gurfanai ya taimakeka."


Ko kallo bata ishe Baidulƙais ba saima kishingiɗawa da yayi itako ganin yaki ansa ta ɗauka bai bukata haka ta juye abincin cikin kular da aka kawoma Baidulƙais abinci, ta koma gefe ta tsugunna, abinda Bahijja tayi ba karamin ɗagama sauran bayin wajen hankali yai ba, itako ko ajikin ta hakan yasa Sarki Baidulƙais ya fusata kiran ta yayi ta hanyar hannu yai mata yafice hakan yasa ta gane kiranta yakeyi, tashi tayi taje gaban Baidulƙais ta tsugunna tace. "Ranka shidade gani.....wani wawan mari ya kwashe Bahijja dashi take tasaki wani razanannn kuka duk ta ruɗe kara ɗaura mata wani gigitaccen mari yayi, take Bahijja ta fara sakin fitsri dan azaba, nan ya kira uwar bayi, itama ya wanketa da maruka har ukku yaimata nuni da ta fita da Bahijja dan bata gwaji akan sharuɗɗan shi, "tuba mukeyi Sarki mai dole gurfanai yaja girman ka." cewar uwar bayi takama hannun Bahijja da tagaji da kukan zuci suka fita ba inda ta wuce da ita sai taskar bayi suka shiga ɗakin ta suna shiga ta kara kifawa Bahijja mari sannan tace."Ke dan ubanki ni zaki jazama bala'i wajen Sarki baki da hankali zaki mika mashi abinci ke zaki dinga bashi da hannun ki har sai ya koshi kina jina?  sannan kuma daga yau duk wani motsin Sarki a kalkashin ki zaki dinga kulawa shashasha dake mai mugun hali ɓaceman dagani kar in ɓallaki."

Dasauri Bahijja ta fita ta shiga masaukin su tana kuka tana tuna Umman ta da Baban ta, Yarima Malik ne ya faɗo mata arai a zahiri tace. "Ya Malik da kana nan ba wanda zai cutar dani, Allah yabaka lafiya yarima mai adalci mai kishin bayi da talakawa."ji tayi ance. "Kwanciyar uban me kikeyi bazaki je kici gaba da aiki ba ko saina zaneki a wajen nan."?  cewar uwar bayi, cikin kyarma Bahijja tafita tana bata hakuri sannan takoma wajen Sarki Baidulƙais ta tsugunna ta ɗibi a abincin tafara bashi yana karɓa yana yaguna yana kuma kin karɓa har hannun ta saiya sage sannan zai karɓa haka har ya koshi yana cikin abincin karshe ya firzama Bahijja a fuska rufe idon ta tayi sannan ta goge tace. "Tuba nike ya shugabana biyayya a gareni shine gatana girma yakara shiga a wajen ka inagodiya shugabana."

Haka rayuwa takasance a wajen Bahijja kullun cikin fuskantar wulakanci takeyi a wajen Sarki Baidulƙais ba dare ba rana wani lokacin indare yayi haka uwar bayi zata kwara mata botikim ruwa a jiki ta sakata wankin kayanta ba karamim tsanar Bahijja tayi acewar ta dan Baidulƙais ya zaɓeta matsayin hadimar shi, wannan dalilin yasa ta hana Bahijja sakat a cikin Masarautar Raifasu, kullin cikin zulumi take kwana take tashi. 


Bayan wata huɗu zuwa yanzun Bahijja takara girma sosai, zaune suke a fa'da ta duka wajen Sarki Baidulƙais ya ɗaura kafar shi a saman  kafaɗar Bahijja tana mashi tausa, anata fadanci banda maganar tsafi ba abinda ake gudanar wa a cikin Masarautar Raifasu, Baidulƙais ne yace. "Sarkin gida aje taskar bayi a ɗauko mani bayi ukku daga cikin bayin Daborah aljani ɗantsitu zaiyi anfani dasu."

Koda Bahijja taji abinda Sarki Baidulƙais yace hakan ba karamin tsorata ta yai ba shiru kawai tayi ga zullumi bata gama tunina ba aka kawo bayin ukku maza biyu mace daya a gaban idan ta aka yanke hannun wata akasamu wata ƴar kwalba akasaka jinin a ciki zobe Baidulƙais ya murza cikin kankanin lokaci wajen ya haɗe da guguwa take aka bace da waɗan nan bayi hakan yasa tun Bahijja na guntse kukan ta har ya kuɓuce mata tasan bala'in dake wajen boka Sarrilƙus, shiko Sarki Baidulƙais sawa yayi akaima Bahijja bulalai akan tayi mashi kuka a cikin fa'da sannan yasa aka tafi da ita wajen horon bayi. 

*∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆*

A wajen Yarima Malik kuwa baida magana saita Bahijja yau tunda ya tashi ya sama kanshi niyar fara binciken tarihin Sarrilƙus dan yasan ta hanyar da zaibi wajen yakar su ya amso Bahijja dan yana ji Bahijja akwai babban alamari a tareda ita, shida Huzaifa sai Yazid suka tafi har cikin gidan tarihin Daborah,suna isa maigidan tarihin ya gaishe dasu suka shiga suka tambaye shi litattafan tsafi da tarihin Masarautu na Gambia ya nuna masu ɗauko litattafan sukayi wajen ukku suka fara dubawa inda kowa ke nema ko zadu dace da samin tarihin Masarautar Raifasu da boka Sarrilƙus....

Suna cikin dubawa Yazid yaga wani littafi na tsafi da aka rubuta shi sama da shekaru dubu huɗu a duniya kuma yafi karfi a Masarautu haka sukaita dubawa suna ganin kayan ban mamaki ranar har yamma basu dace da litattafan tsafin da zasu samu sirrin boka ba, hakan yasa Malik fadama Sarkin gidan tarihi dalilin zuwan shi, bakaramin mamaki Dawud yaiba saidai yananin da yaga Malik bazai iya musu dashi ba hakan yasa yace. "Naji abinda kace magajin gobe saidai kasani kuje ku dawo zuwa nan sa sati ɗaya zan duba maku, in ban samu ba kuzo zanbaku kadan daga cikin abinda nasani akan Sarrilƙus da dajin gamki."

Cikin fushi da garaji Malik yace. "Sati yaimani nisa dan kallan shekara nike mashi saidai na baka nan da jibi zan dawo dafatan zaka taimaka yanda ya kamata."...."na amince Allah yakara girma Allah yakaimu jibin lafiya." cewar Dawud, haka suka tafi sashen Yarima Malik ko abinci ya gagari Malik kullin hankalin shi na wajen Bahijja. 

Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ce zaune a saman kilisar ta ana mata tausa ga hadiman ta sai fiffita suke mata wasu kuma suna ta aiki, ɗan aike ne tareda Jakadiya tai mashi isu har gaban Sarauniya Zulaiƙa dukawa sukayi kowa ya kai gaisuwa wajen Sarauniya, ɗaga masu hannu tayi sannan ta kalli Jakadiya da alamar tambaya, kanta kasa tace. "Ranki shidade kinyi bako daga Masarautar Waisu, Allah yataimakeki shiyasa na kawo shi gareki Allah yakarama Sarauniya lafiya da nisan kwana." kallan shi Sarauniya Zulaiƙa takarayi duk da baibari ta haɗa ido dashi ba amman bata aminta da bokon ba, "daga ina ɗan saurayi."? 
Kallan sakare yaima Sarauniya Zulaiƙa yace. "Daga Masarautar Waisu uban gidana yace inbaki wannan sakon." kallan tsaf Sarauniya Zulaiƙa taimashi na sakanni taba Jakadiya umurnin ansar wasikat ta hanyar kallo, amsa tayi ta duka tamikawa Sarauniya Zulaiƙa, budewa tayi dafara karantawa. 

 _Nasan zakiyi mamakin jin wannan sakon daga gareni to ba abin mamaki bane duk da fuskar ki ta bayyana hakan a zahiri, dalilin kuwa shine kinsan ba wata alaƙa tsakanin makiya kuma abokan gaba guda biyu na zauna nayi nazari naga dacen da yake a cikin tarayyata dake dan haka ina mubaya'a a wajen ya Sarauniya mai adalci ina sauraranki daga Sarkin Waisu zuwa ga Sarauniyar Daborah_


Kallan bawan nan tayi sannan tace. "Inakaje kabashi wannan sakon sannan kace mashi Sarauniya Zulaiƙa taji sakon shi ammn kudurin shi abin korarwa ne a wajena dan haka zaka iya tafiya."

Magana bawan nan zaiyi Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta ɗaga mashi hannu haka ya haɗe maganar shi ya tashi ya fita ba inda yaima tsinke sai Masarautar Waisu yana zuwa ya wuce fa'da ana ta fadanco koda Sarki ya yagan shi baki ɗayan su suka mike Yarima Haishir ne ya tareshi tareda da jefomashi tambayoyi kala kala yace. "Sunbu ko andace?  Ina fatan zako mana da labari mai daɗi? Kuma a wane yanayi kabaro Sarauniya Zulaiƙa."!? 

Kallan shi Sunbu yayi sannan ya gilgiza kai mikama Sarki  sakon wani jikka da ta bashi sannan yace.!!! 




 *Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*
