*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*





Babi na 17




"Ina mai bakin cikin sanar dakai kin amincewar Sarauniya Zulaiƙa akan kudurin mu, sannan tace lamarin ka kurarreni a gareta kuma abin watsar wa saidai tabani wannan jikkar bansan meki ciki ba ya shugabana Gamji ya taimakeka ina tuba."

Girgiza kai Humzu yayi cikin bakin ciki marar misaltuwa yace. "Naji abinda tace saidai ba nan gizo ke sakar ba yazama dole mu sauya wata hanya da zamu jawo Zulaiƙa a jikin mu dan mu ha'inceta dan haka Yarima Haishir ya zama dole ka ƙulla abota da Malikussaif  kuma ka nuna kanasan shi da zuciya ɗaya ta hakane kawai zaisa Zulaiƙa ta baka mahimmanci tunda kana son ɗanta harda amince dani daga karshe fa marmasu mu darkake su kamar yanda sukayi ga kakan mu Sarki Faihasu inafatan kun fahimta."? 

Cikin gamsuwa Yarima Haishir yace. "Ranka shidade munji abinda kace saidai ya zamuyi da gammayar da mukayi da Masarautar Raifasu da Ƙaisu bayan haɗin guiwa kai karfi da mukayi dasu baka ganin hakan tamkar cin amana ne a wajen su."?  tashi Sarki Humzu ya tashi daga saman karagar mulkin shi ya iso gaban ɗan nashi ya tallabo fuskar shi yan shafawa cikin so da kauna yace. "Ɗana kenan rabu da maganar Sarki Baidulƙais da rundunar shi tareda Sarkin Ƙaisu a halin yanzun batasu nike ba saboda sun gaza,  ta Zulaiƙa nikeyi gata mace amman kullin saitasa gaban maza ya faɗi naso ace Malikussaif  ya mutu amman kwatakwalan ci irin  na Baidulƙais yasa yakasa harda basu magani wannan dalilin yasa ko Masarautar shi ban kara waiwaya ba, kaima kamanta dasu nasan bada jimawa ba zaizo ya sameni da wata bukatar zai gane waye Humzu kuma me nataka intakamar shi boka Sarrilƙus nikuma takama ta Bokanya Zuruka kaga banda wata damuwa ɗana gamu da tarin mayaka danma nabashi wasu saidai sakaci irin na Baidulƙais yasa anhalakamani jama'ata, kaidai ka kiyaye abinda nace maka yanada kyau ka samu lokaci kaje Daborah wajen Malikussaif dan gaida shi."


Murmushi ɗauke a fuskar Haishir ya rungume mahaifin shi sannan ya ɗauki jikar da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tabada ya buɗe bakomai bane sai dalleliyar takobi da sauri ya fiddota duk ta haske masu ido saidai ashe ta Masarautar suce, takobin Sarki Faihasu kowa saida yai mamaki duk da bata ce komai aman yagane manufar Zulaiƙa mikama Sarkin fa'da yayi yace akaima Sarkin gidan tarihin Masarautar ya aje a wajen adana makamai na tarihi sai fidda iska da huci Sarki Humzu yakeyi a haka kowa yabar fa'da bai kuma cewa komai ba yana tunanin yanda zai bullowa lamarin Sarauniya Zulaiƙa

*∆∆∆∆∆∆∆∆*

Masarautar Raifasu kuwa tunda Sarki Baidulƙais yasa wawan Sarki hukunta wasu bayi ba abinda  akeyi musu banda azaba dan ankamasu da laifin guduwa ciki kuwa harda Bahijja itako Bahijja Baidulƙais yasa akaima ta bulala hamsin yasa aka kaita wani ɗakin duhu aka rufe ta, danko matar Sarki Baidulƙais gimbiya Yazira sawa tayi aka dinga watsama Bahijja ruwan zafi abinka ga fara duk jikin ta yayi jajir jini ya tashi saman fatar ta, suko sauran bayin shanyasu akai a rana aka azamasu duwatsu a saman kirji ana dukan su, ana ɗiga masu narkarka dalma afata sai ihu sukeyi ba dare ba rana cikin azaba suke, a gadarance Baidulƙais yasa aka kawo mashi Bahijja a turakar shi wanda yasa bayin dake wajen suka fita kallan ta yayi duk da bata hada shekara goma ba amman akwai halitta dan inka ganta zakayi zatan takai shekara shabiyar a duniya dan kirar jikin ta duk ta fara bayyana ga gashi samn baya kwance inbakasan Bahijja ba tabbas zakayi tsammani balarabiya ce da kyawunta da dirin ta, koda aka wulla Bahijja cikin ɗakin Baidulƙais ya rufe kofa, ya kafeta mayyun idanun shi tafiya yayi gabanta yana shafa jikin ta duk karancin shekarun Bahijja hakan baisan takasa gani abinda yake shirin aikatawa da ita ba, tunawa tayi da huɗubar mahaifiyar ta tace mata koda Ya Malik dinta ne kada tabari ya taɓa mata jiki mutuwa zatayi, baki daya takara tsorata jin bakin shi a nata ya danneta jikin bango yana sumbar ta ko motsi batayi na dangin ta da suka fara fitowa yafara dirza, kwalla ihu tayi ta gartsa mashi uban cizo jin zai halaka ta ba shiri ya saketa duk ya rikice sai kuka takeyi takama ja da baya baya zuciyar ta kamar zata faɗo kasa dan tsoro da fargaba, shiko ta fasa mashi hannu ya duƙe sai zugi yake mashi gadan gadan yayi kan Bahijja itako ganin haka yasa ta faɗa bathroom din ɗakin shi ta saka kuba sai garza kuka takeyi tana addu'a tarasa dalilin dayasa zai hukunta ta hanyar cutar ta yana mintsilinta harda kama mata baki, tasan yau saita mutu tunda har ya taɓata tunda ba babanta bane tana cikin kukan bacci yai gaba da Bahijja can cikin bacci taji yana ta gamrar kofar yana mata barazana shiko dafe yake da mara yagama kwaɗaituwa da Bahijja kuma ya sama ranshi kota wane hali saiya kashe kishir ruwar da ta dame shi akan Bahijja tun fara ganin shi da ita, itako kururuwa ta shiga yi dai dai da gimbiya Yazira tayo hanyar ɗakin mijin ta, da sauri taji ihun mace dakin mijin ta da karfi ta banka kofar ɗakin ta shiga tsaye ta iske shi daga shi sai wando ya rike ciki, da sauri ya waigo ya ganta harara ya watsa mata, ya cigaba da bugun bathroom din tabbas tasan mijin ta nabin mata amman banda bayi tayi mamakin wacece cikin kewaye takowa tayi duk ta tsorata da yanayin shi, ta fara magana tace. "Ki buɗe kofar kinji ba abinda zai maki." itako Bahijja jin muryar mace tasan bai wuce gimbiya yasa ta buɗe kofar da sauri ta koma bayan Yazira da karfi ya fizogata ya manna a jikin yace ta barmashi ɗaki ai baiwar shi ce kuma mallakin shi zai iya komai da ita, dubara ta faɗo mata a rai, tace. "Haba kamarka mai dole Sarkin Raifasu ba girman ka bane a ji kaima ficiciyar yarinya kamar Bahijja fyaɗe tabbas girman ka zai zube ga idan duniya shawarar da zanbaka me zai hana kabari takara girma sai ka maidata kayan hutawar ka, nasan lokacin zata fi maka anfani."



A fusace kamar zaki ya kara haɗe bakin shi dana Bahijja kamar ƴar baby take a hannun Baidulƙais sannan ya jefar da ita ya kalli Yazira "kin kyauta da kika fahimtar dani wannan shine abinda ya dace." banzan kallo ta watsa mashi sannan ta fidda Bahijja waje ta dawo tana bashi baki tana shafar shi, kamar mai jira ya fara sarrafa Yazira ranar akanta ya kare, itako Bahijja komawa tayi taskar bayi ta kwanta duk zazzaɓi ya kamata sai kyakyarwa takeyi, bacci ya ɗauketa tana cikin bacci taji an kyarama ta ruwa asaman fuska a firgice ta mike uwar bayice tace taje gimbiya Yazira na kiran ta shashen ta duk da batasan me zatayi mata ba saida taji cikin ta ya juya, kakkaɓe ruwan kunnan ta tayi ta tafi, a wani katan ɗaki ta isketa tanacin tufa, dukawa Bahijja yayi  ta gaida ta, watsama dubzar tuffa tayi a saman fuskar ta dukar da kanta tayi ta goge sannan tace. "Godiya nike uwar dakina Bahijja natuba gurfanai ya huci ranki."

Jitayi ta watsa mata yatsu a cikin ido ta ɗaureta da mari cikin fushi tace. "Ke baiwa harkin isa shiga ɗakin mijina wato ke mazinaciya ko."?  Mamaki ya ishe zuciyar Bahijja da ta kasa gane mitake nufi da mazinaciya jitayi ta kara kifamata mari da sauri ta dawo tunanin ta hakuri tafara bata kamar me amman sai kifa mata maruka takeyi take fuskar Bahijja ta kumbura har ta fasa mata baki, kuka ta fashe dashi nan takira uwar bayi tace akaita cikin taskar bayi aimata dukan tsiya haka akaja Bahijja kasa tanata ba gimbiya Yazira hakuri itako Uwani uwar bayi daman ta tsani  Bahijja ko tambayar ta dalilin laifin ta batai ba da kanta taita sakarma Bahijja mari saida ta  sauke mata bulala hamsin take jikinta yakara tsanan ta da zafi na zazzaɓi, sannan tasaka wanki, haka ta jibgi aiki tana cikin aiki Jakadiya ta aiko wata baiwa sa'ar Bahijja taje tana kiranta, a gajiye tace aiki takeyi jin abinda tace yasa uwar bayi jiki na rawa tace ta tafi, haka suka tafi har ta faɗa tayi baki tana isa ta riketa suka isa wajen Sarki Baidulƙais tun shigowa Bahijja yake haɗe yawo ya kafeta da ido itako tsoran shi takeyi taki ta. kalle shi dukawa tayi ta gaidashi sai tanɗe baki yakeyi ganin abinda yakema Bahijja yasa Jakadiya taji jikinta ya mutu tasan manufar sa, itako tana tausan Bahijja dan kaf bayi itace karama mai karancin shekaru, haka ta basu waje ya kara tanɗe baki yana shafa fuskar ta zuwa wuyanta kara matsowa yayi yafara sunsuna ta da sauri taja baya maganar gimbiya Yazira ta faɗo mata arai tace aranta ko abinda Baidulƙais ke mata shine zinar ji tayi yace. "Bahijja bani abinci."


Jikinta na rawa tafara bashi da hannun ta sai kama mata hannu yakeyi da harshe yana tsotsa itako sai kwalla ke yawo a fuskar ta tana neman tsarin Allah daga kaidin Baidulƙais harya gama yace taci tace ta koshi kamota yayi ya kara mannata a jikin shi yana wasa da yatsun ta ya shafi dogon wuyanta mai tafiya da ranshi yaji jikinta kamat garwashi, yace. "Baki lfy ne."? 


Zamewa tayi a jikin sa ta zauna kasa duk ta tsorata da abinda gimbiya Yazira taimata tace mashi ."eh."cikin kulawa ya kira mai kula da maganin shi ya miko mashi wani jiko na maganin hausa ya mika mata ba musu ta ansa tasha sannan yace akira mashi da Jakadiya tana shigowa ta gaida shi yace tamaida Bahijja gida bata lfy haka ta kamata suka tafi sai magana Jakadiya kema Bahijja saidai tai nisa a tunani samun wata inuwa tayi ta zaunar da Bahijja tace. "Bahijja bakiji inata magana miyai maki."? 


A tsorace tace. "Ranki shidade bakomai abinci." nabashi takarasa maganar kwalla na zarya a fuskar ta, cikin rashin yarda tace "ƴata kada kiboyeman ba abinda zan maki kuma bazan fadama kowa b." sannan Bahijja tace. "Shafa mani jikina yakeyi kuma yama tsotsan baki na gimbiya Yazira takamani dashi lokacin na ɓuya a ban ɗaki hakan yasa ta kubutar dani daga mintsina dayayi a nan ta gwada mata kirjin ta da hannu na koma dakin mu, sai ga uwar bayi tai kirana ina zuwa gimbiya taita mari na sannan tasa akaimani dukan tsiya tace mani ni mazinaciya ce dan Allah Umma me mazinaciya ke nufi inajin tsoro." banda kwalla ba abinda Bahijja keyo tausan ta yakara kama Jakadiya cikin kulawa tace. "Saboda kina bari mijin ta na wasa da jikin ki shiyasa tace maki mazinaciya sannan karki kara bari yana taɓa maki jiki, kinaso ki mutu."? 


A rude Bahijja tace" Aaa Umma."...'' inhar hakane kada ku kara bari ko hannun ki ya rike." cewa tayi "to Umma daman haka Umma na take cemani ba bani ni Bahijja shikenan mutwa zanyi yanzun."guntse dariyar da takama Jakadiya tayi ganin yanda ta ɗaura hannu akai tana gabza kuka duk ta rikice da ka ganta kaga yarinya sharaf, cewa tayi. "Ai yanzun bazaki mutuba saboda mutuwa tabaki bashi saidai kada ki kara kinji gurfanai ya kiyaye gaba tashi.' mutafi haka Jakadiya takaita har ɗakin ta sannan ta samu wata baiwa dattijuwa Saude ta bata amanar Bahijja cikin murna ta ƙarɓa daman tun farkon ganin ta da Bahijja taji taimata kuma tanasan ta ɗanyi mata wasu yan tambayoyi na gaba kaɗan  da haka ta cigaba da aikin ta, ita kuma Jakadiya ta tafi, bacci yasake ɗaukar Bahijja uwar bayi ce ta shigo dakin matar zata kara kwarama Bahijja ruwa ta hanata tace Jakadiya tace abarta tayi bacci ta huta kar atasheta cikin bacin rai Uwani tace...... 






*Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*



Babi na 18




"Kai amman dai anyi tsinannar rayinya anya bata tareda sihiri baiyanan ne, Sarki Baidulƙais yakaita kusa dashi, ga Jakadiya tana bata kulawa, anya ba tsafi tayiba shiyasa kowa ke sonta, gaskia saina kassarama ƴar nan rayuwa ga ƴaƴanmu amman itakadai ce abar so." itadai Saude kallan Uwani kawai takeyi tana mamakin hassada irin tata, duk da itama tanada zuria amman ba abinda ya shafeta da Bahijja inbaka taimaketa baka kassarata ba, cikin jin haushi tace. "Kika kafeni da idanu wato ke bazaki ɗauki matakin ba kenan akan wannan ƴar kwikwiwon yarinyar ba ko."?  cewa tayi"haba shugaba kece fa uwar bayi yakamata ace kaf bayi da bako da ɗan gidana sun zama cikakkun ƴaƴanki menene riba dan kinsaka ido akan jikar ki Bahijja."
"Karki rainaman hankali anyi ita Bahijja ba bil Adama bace?  dan kawai tanada kyaunki rabu dani ina ganin girmanki matsayin ki na mataimakiya ta baccin haka da na raina maki hankali sai kinsha mamakina sakarai." tabar ɗakin sai sababi takeyi itako Saude kallan Bahijja tayi  dake bacci duk tausan ta yakamata tasan duk wanda uwar bayi ta tsana saiya sha ukuba dukko girman mace da haka ta cigaba da hidimar ta. 



 _~DABORAH~_ 




Malik ne zaune wajen Dawud Yauma sun gama duba littafin da suke nema basu samu ba hakan yasa Dawud yace suje wajen Lawal mai kula da manyan litattafan tarihi musamman na tsafi cikin gamsuwa Malik yace. "Shikena bari mutafi saidai kace zaka faɗamana abinda kasani dan gane da Sarrilƙus."
"Eh kwarai kuwa munyi hakan daku saidai yanzun bari mukoma wajen wancan iccen inmana shinfiɗa sai in fadama ka kilan kasamu wani ɗan ilimi dangane da Sarrilƙus." haka suka tashi yai masu shinfiɗa kowa ya zauna shima ya zauna yana kallan Malik yace. "Kamaido hankalin kaji Labarin Sarrilƙus da yanda akaita artaba dashi musamman Masarautar Daborah wanda har yanzun ake ciki dayin ta." 




LABARI




A shekaru dubu biyu da suka gabata zamanin Sarauniya Maimoon ƴar Sarki Khamil jikanya ga Jabbar ɗan Sarauniya Yahanasu ta II anyi hatsabibin boka da tsafin sa ya karaɗe nahiyar Gambia a lokacin ba addini a cikin Masarautar Daborah, wata rana Sarauniya Maimoon tana gudanar da mulkin ta a fa'da saiga wani bakin mutun marar kyawun fasali ya kawo mata magani yace taba mahaifin ta Khamil saboda baida lfy har anfidda rai wannan dalilin yasa ta cigaba da rike sarautar mahaifin ta, koda wannan mutumin ya fadama Maimoon kudurin shi bata kawo komai aranta ba ta karɓa saboda anmaida tsafi ado kuma abin tunkaho bayan aiyima Sarki Khamil magani bayan kwana biyu kamar ya warke ashe an turo mashi bakin aljani ya dinga zukar jini Khamil har ya mutu, lallai mutuwar Khamil ta ɗaga ma Sarauniya Maimoon hankali har andanga na bayan wata daya aka naɗata a matsayin Sarauniya Daborah. 



Bayan shekara ɗaya da kafuwar daular mulkin Maimoon sako ya bayyana a cikin fa'da ta hanyar halboshi da mashi ya kafe a bangon fa'da kowa saida hankalin shi yai mummunan tashi musamman Sarauniya Maimoon.


Shiru ne ya biyo baya sannan Sarauniya Maimoon taba magatakarda umurnin ɗauko sakon, fizogo kibiyar yayiya zare takarda dukawa yayi yaba Sarauniya Maimoon ta bashi umurnin karanta sakon dake jikin takardar. 


 _Sarauniya Maimoon ina hankalin ki yatafi da zaki ɗauki magani kiba Khamil bayan tsafin sake jikin shi yai sanadiyar mutuwar Sarki Khamil dan haka kasani da sannu sai mun karar da tsatsan ku a doran ƙasa, sannan kijira guguwar yakina daga mutane harda aljanu nine Sarrilƙus saina lalata Masarautar Daborah da abinda kuka taka dake da bokan ka Ƙuduru sain karar daku sannan in ɗauki wakur Barri inkaita Masarautar Raifasu hhhhhhh!"_



Da sauri Sarauniya Maimoon ta mike tana alajabin yaudarar da akaimasu tabbas dole saita ɗauki mummunan mataki akan Sarrilƙus saidai abinda baka sani ba Malik bakaramin mataki Sarauniya Maimoon ta ɗauka ba akan Sarrilƙus!"

Ɗan nazari Malik yayi sannan yace. "Amman ina mamakin wannan mutumin daman haka ya share shekaru masu yawa a doran duniya kodai aljani ne."? 

Murmushi Dawud yayi yace. "Sarrilƙus mutun ne ba aljani ba sai dai kasani ba ainihin sunan sa kenan ba, Sarrilƙus wani zoben tsafi ne da ya shafe saman da shekaru dubu biyar tunkan a kafa daular mulkin Daborah yake, ainahin bokan sunan shi Gamki duk wanda yake anfani da zoben shine ake kira da Sarrilƙus shi kuma dajin gamki saboda bokan yasa ake kiran dajin da gamki  shi kuma sunan dajin zusu, lokacin da gamki ya kama dajin da aljanu sai suka hana kowa huɗɗa da dajin sai su kadai shiyasa ake cewa dajin gamki, shi kuma bokan zobe na tsatsubar kukar saman dutse ake kiran shi da Sarrilƙus, yanada yara matsafan gaske, duk wanda ya mutu sai su naɗa sabo abashi wannan zoben ana kiran shi da Sarrilƙus, kaji takaitaccen tarihin Sarrilƙus."


Numfasawa Yarima Malik yayi cikin gamsuwa yace. "Amman naji sai dai shi wannan bokan menene ainahin sunan shi."? 

"Eh gaskia kayi tambaya mai anfani ainahin sunan shi Ƙibbu sannan shine boka na casa'in dake mulkin dajin gamki kuma zoben Sarrilƙus na hannun shi duk da akwai aljanin dake kula da wannan zoben shida rundunar aljanu, har yanzun dajin gamki da kake gani Malik inakaje da rana kamar dare yake tsabar tsafi abinda baka sani ba Malik zuwa dajin gamki ba riba saboda aljanu sunyi tasiri a cikin shi saidai ban tunanin akwai mahalukin dake rayuwa a cikin shi saidai matsafa da sauran aljanu kuma duk a ƙalkashin ikon boka Sarrilƙus yake."



Yazid ne yace . "amma taya zamu samu damar yakar wannan hatsabibin bokan."?  wata dariya Dawud yayi yanai masu kallan yara manyan gobe yace. "Yazid kenan faɗa da aljani ba dadi kuma duk wanda yataka wuta bazai kuma waiwayar taba, kaga yaki da Sarrilƙus tamkar baraza da rayuwa ne dan haka bansan ta yanda zaku bullowa wannan bokan ba saidai bari inbaku taƙaitaccen tarihin yaki da Sarauniya Maimoon tayi da Sarrilƙus akan fansar ran mahaifin ta Khamil ɗan Sarki Jabbar." 



Gyara zama sukayi sannan Huzaifa yace "munajinka ya Dawud." cigaba da magana yayi. "Kamar yanda nafaɗa maku sakon da Sarrilƙus yasa aljanu suka halboma Sarauniya Maimoon tunda ga ranar Maimoon ta shiga cikin damuwa kullin kudurin taya zata kawo karshen Sarrilƙus haka ta tara bokaye nadaga sassa daban daban na kasar Gambia duk wanda yazo yayi yan dube duben shi sai yace bazai iya tunkarar Sarrilƙus ba dan yafisu aljanu da tsafi mai girma daga cikin wannan bokaye da Sarauniya Maimoon ta tara haka suka gama tsafe tsafen su suka tafi ta sallame su amman a banza a wofi, saidai abin mamaki washe gari haka aka tashi a alajabi da tsoro cikin ƴaƴan Sarauniya Maimoon, Jumal aka wayi gari ankashe shi batareda wukaba, jikin shi aka zana Sarrilƙus sannan aka duba kirjin Yarima Jumal ba zuciyar shi da harshen shi tabbas faɗamaku kalar tashin hankalin da Maimoon da sauran yan Masarautar Daborah suka shiga ɗaukar lokaci haka ta rungume Jumal tana ta kuka mai tsuma zuciya tana birkita shi taga takarda anrubutata da jini tana karanta wa ta sume ba rai dan firgici."


Waziri Shuzab ya ɗauka ya karanta wasikar wanda mamaki ya gama bayyana a fuskar waziri yakara maimaitawa yayi a gaban kowa. 



 _Kina mamakin ganin gawar ɗanki ko? Ba abin mamaki bane yanzun haka Maimoon zuciyar ɗanki da harshen sa anba aljani ɗan tsitu dan ya wargaza alkadarin ku sannan kuyi gaggawar mika wukar Barri wajen Sarki Gursu na Raifasu in bahaka ba zamuyi maku dirar mikiya sai munkarar da kowa da komai naku nasan komai zai burgeki kina ganin jinin ɗanki rubuce a takarda ko.? Ina sauraranku keda kazamin bokayen ku, duk tsafin da zasuyi a banka innaga dama lokaci daya zan halakansu dake kanki Maimoon ki kiyaye! Sarrilƙus ke magana._


Baki daya aka shiga ruɗani aka binne Jumal sannan aka fara ba Maimoon kulawa takai sati ko magana batayi a cikin Masarautar Daborah, da yake ƴaƴan Sarauniya Maimoon huɗu ukku maza ɗaya mace, haka akaita bata jike jike har ta hakura saidai Maimoon bata daina yunkurin yakar Sarrilƙus ba, bayan wata bakwai komai lfy ba wata damuwa aka wayi gari ba takobin Barri a cikin gidan tarihi take Sarauniya Maimoon ta fille kan Sarkin tarihi ta haɗa gangamin yaki mararsa iyaka ta tunkari Masarautar Raifasu taimasu dirar mikiya anyi musu asara marar kyawun gani haka yasa Sarkin Raifasu ya murza wani zobe take aljani ya bayyana nan ya fada mashi abinda ke faruwa, cikin kankanin lokaci rundunar yaki ta karu harda aljanu kasan yanda lamarin iska yake duk da ankashe rabi ta kwata da jaruman Raifasu amman daga baya aka fara yima Jaruman Daborah ɗauki ɗai ɗai duk da ba'a ganin masu yakin da aka fahimci da jinni haka yasa. Sarauniya Maimoon taba wazirin ta wukar Barri da ta anso wajen yariman Masarautar na lokacin har ta kashe shi, daman sunzo da bokan su, hakan yasa ya ɓace shida waziri sukabar wajen ta hanyar ɗauke takobin Barri  Sarauniya Maimoon ta gama kufula shiga tayi wajen Sarki suka fara fafatawa sai sarrafa wannan zoben yakeyi yaki na kamari danko saida yaima Sarauniya Maimoon kaca kaca harya ɗauka ta mutu yana cigaba da sarrafa zoben shi tasa takobi ta datse ma Sarki hannu ya faɗi kasa take wajen ya ɗauki kuwa marar dadin sauraro abokan artabar tasu suka dinga bacewa yan sauran jaruman Raifasu suka dinga guduwa dan tsira da ransu, duk da haka ba laifi akwai sauran Jarumai fa yawa na Daborah duk da ankashe da yawa haka aka dinga neman Sarauniya Maimoon har cikin fa'da a kwance aka sameta cikin jini ga Sarki Gursu akwance da hannu ɗaya yana shirin mutuwa dai jini ke zuba cikin sauri aka ɗauki Sarauniya Maimoon nan ta tace a zare zoben a taho dashi Masarautar Daborah, sai kuwa Sarki Gursu yakeyi yana nuna kada su taɓa wannan zoben amman haka aka tafi dashi sai zafi ke fita a jikin shi har cikin Masarautar Daborah aka ajiye zoben kowa yaita murna anci yaki gwanin dadi saidai abin da baku sani ba Malik ɗaukar wannan zoben da akayi saida ya jazama Masarautar Daborah da abinda ke cikin fitina da baku tunani dan itace ainahin durkushe war Masarautar Daborah har izuwa yanzun."

''Kowa ya gama karaya da lamarin Sarrilƙus cikin jimami Yarima yace miya faru har haka."?  cikin tausayi da jimami harda saukar kwalla a idan Dawud yace.... 




*Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BAYI MA 'YA'YA'NE*

 *~Touching heart story💔😭~*

              _By_
*Jameey Yar'mutan kankia*

https://www.facebook.com/107980080946102?referrer=whatsa

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*




Babi na 19

"Sarauniya Maimoon tana zaune a cikin fa'da tareda manyan fadawan ta suna shawarwarin yanda zasu ɓullowa lamarn Sarrilƙus kawai sukaji fa'da ta ɗauki rawa da kuiwa ga wani irin hasken wuta da ya hargitse wajen tare da da guguwa mai duhun gaske wadda baka iya ganin gaban ka dan tsoro kowa ya gama fita hayyacn shi wannan guguwar ta ɗauki lokaci mai tsawo tana zagaye fa'da sannan wani katon bakin aljani ya bayyana a sifar mutun marar kyaun gani da fasali, koda mutanen mu hankalin su yadawo jikin su suka fara gane mutane hakan yasa da sauri suka mike tsaye dan firgitar ganin bakin mutumin dake tareda su, itako Sarauniya Maimoon take ta mike tayi wajen wannan aljani a daƙile duk da ta tsorata amman hakan bai hana ta nuna ita shugaba bace, koda Sarauniya ta isa wajen bakin mutumin ta tsaya gaban shi tace. 

"Miya kawo ka cikin fa'da ta mi kake nema."?  maimakon yaba Maimoon amsar tambayar ta sai ya fashe da wata irin mahaukaciyar dariya saida kowa ya duke tsabar kaɗuwa da tsoro yakai minti goma sannan ya dakata, duk da haka Sarauniya Maimoon bata fasa kallan shiba ido cikin ido, miko mata hannu yayi sannan yace. "Ki azamani zoben da kika ƙarba a hannun Sarki Gursu nine bawan zoben inba haka ba komi ya biyo baya kuka jama kanku ya Maimoon."

Cikin fushi tace. "Kai bawa marar yanci ba zan bada zobe ba kayi abinda kaga dama inhar ka isa tunda kace kaine shugaba mai kula da wannan zoben." ba tareda ya jinkirtaba ya ɓace ya tafi, kowa yayi mamaki sunyi tsammani sai ya ɗauki mataki marar kyau haka sukaci gaba da shawarwari kan lamarin har suka kammala Sarauniya Maimoon takoma sashen ta ita da hadiman ta, har dare yayi ba wani abun da ya biyo baya saidai banan gizo ke sakar ba, Sarauniya Maimoon na tareda da ɗanta Muntazir saiga baiwar gimbiya Hunaisa ta shigo a firgice ta zube a gaban Sarauniya Maimoon tana garxa uban kuka, a tare suka mike tana tambayar ta ko lfy, cikin shassheƙar kuka duk muryarta ta dakushe tace. "ki gafarceni ya Sarauniya ta, na nemi uwar dakina banganta ba, bata cikin wannan Masarautar tuba nike." wani uban duka Maimoon takaima isa ta rushe da kuka mai cin zuciya Yarima Muntanzir ne ya fita ya sanar da sarkin gida cikin kankanin lokaci kowa yasan da ɓatan gimbiya Hunaisa aka baza jarumai a cikin daren nan ba inda ba a duba gimbiya Hunaisa ba amman ba amo ba labari ta ɓata har gari ya waye ba wanda ya runtsa a cikin Masarautar Daborah. 


Haka Sarauniya Maimoon taita kuka baci basha har aka wuni cur, danko Jaruman mu tunda suka bazu neman gimbiya Hunaisa basu dawo ba saida rana ta faɗi duk inda ake sa ran ganin ta ba aganta ba ko kaɗan, lamari ya ta'azzara ga Sarauniya Maimoon duk ta lalace ta rame a yini biyu kaɗai kulawa hadiman ta sukaci gaba da bata, suna cikin jimamin ɓatan gimbiya Hunaisa saiga sako an harbo shi ta hanyar mashi ya soke a saman gadon Sarauniya Maimoon, danko bakaramin tsoro ya kama kowa ba, banda Sarauniya Maimoon dan ta shirya zuwan wannan lokacin, zaro mashin tayi ta fiddo wasikar dake jiki ta karanta. 



 _Maimoon dama kindai na wahalar sa jama'arki akan nekan Hunaisa tana hannuna ki rike zobe ni kuma in rike ta, dan haka kisa a ranki bazan taɓa bada taba saidai gawar ta sannn ki saurari zuwana cikin kankanin lokaci._ 

Mikewa Sarauniya Maimoon tayi tafito waje tasa sarkin shela ya tara mata Jarumai ta sanar dasu sakon boka Sarrilƙus,tasa a haɗa mata gangamin yaki suka tunkari dajin gamki, dan karɓo gimbiya Hunaisa, kamar dai yanda kuke shirin yi Ya Malik abin mamaki suna tunkarar wannan dajin ba alamun shi sai wani uban fili duk rairaye masu yawan gaske, duk irin subbace subbacen bokan wannan lokaci bai iya gano alkadarin dajin gamki ba, haka suka gaji suka dawo gida, ko awa ɗaya basu kulla ba sukaji  ana kaɗa kugen yaki a cikin Masarautar Daborah, kowa ya shiga alhini suma Jaruman mu fita sukayi a shiga sahun yaki duk da Jaruman da sukazo yakar Daborah basu kai namu yawa ba kuma da alama Jaruman Masarautar Raifasu ne, Sarki Gursu yafara magana. 
"Sarauniya Maimoon kinsan fansar ƴarki ko? Wannan dalilin yasa kukakai hari dajin gamki yanzun munsame ku tareda jama'ar wannan zoben da kuke renawa yanzun mizaki iyayi ya Maimoon."? 

Kallan kallo akafara tsakanin Sarauniya Maimoon da Sarki Gursu batace komai ba, hakan yaba Sarki Gursu damar sawa aka buga kugen yaki, akafara fafatawa tsakanin Sarauniya Maimoon da Sarki Gursu kowa yana kokarin yaga bayan ɗan uwan shi, baki daya jinin Jarumai kan hakaifa yake duba da yarjejeniyar dake tsakanin Masarautun guda biyu duk wanda aka kashe Sarkin su, sun zama bayin wannan Masarautar, ba karamin kokari Sarauniya Maimoon takeyi ba lokacin ji take ranta fansa ne akan kwato yar ta, koda fafawar tasu tai tsamari hakan yasa Sarauniya Maimoon tasamu nasarar wullar da wukar hannun Sarki Gursu ta faɗi kasa hakan yasa ta taɗe shi ya faɗi ta ɗaura mashi wukatar a wuya take Jaruman Masarautar su suka duka kasa sukaima Sarauniya Maimoon biyayya, tace. "Kai Gursu matsiyaci dakai kaskantattace me zakace ga macen da taci galaba akan ka ko zaka iya bada izini a fiddo mani ƴata a matsayin ka na bawa na ko saina fille kanka a gaban bayinka kuma Jaruman Masarautar ka."? 

Cikin kyarma yace ."za'a bada gimbiya Hunaisa amman da sharadi sai ta barshi shida Jaruman shi a matsayin ƴantattu sunkoma Masarautar su." take  za'a basu gimbiya Hunaisa hakan yasa ta amince a lokacin burinta taga ƴarta mafi sowa a gareta, magana aka fara ba tareda sunga kowa ba. "Maimoon kisake shi kuma kibar kowa ya tafi Masarautar shi ga ƴarki can cikin fa'dar Daborah kin amincewa da umurnin mu shi zaisa ki rasa ƴarki baki ɗaya da komai naki." cikin kaduwa ta saki Sarki Gursu ta kama hanyar tafiya fa'da saidai kash basuda amana, tana isa ta iske ba Hunaisa a cikin fa'da hakan yasa da sauri Sarauniya Maimoon ta shiga sashen ta ba inda ta wuce sai cikin ɗakin ta, a hankali ta fara kallan kasan ɗakin jini ne ta gani duk ya ɓata wajen bakaramin firgita Sarauniya Maimoon tayi ba, har tuntuɓe takeyi dan sauri ta isa saman gadon ta ganin ƴarta shimfiɗe cikin jini yai bala'in ɗaga mata hankali saurin ɗago gimbiya Hunaisa tayi ta kwalla uwar kara mai firgitar wa tace."Hunaisa kitashi ki tabbatar mani da cewa ba mutuwa kikayi ba karki barni ina kaunar ki, Hunaisa ki karyata zatona akan abinda zuciya ta ke rawaita mani na rokeki Hunaisa."

Cikin ɗacin rai Sarauniya Maimoon ta ida maganar ta sake fashewa da malalacin kukan da ba hawaye dan azaba, koda Yarima Muntazir ya karaso cikin ɗakin jiki na ɓari ya rungumo ta a jikin shi ba irin girgizawar da basuma gimbiya Hunaisa amma ko motsi batayi shima ƙin amincewa da mutuwar ta yayi cikin kankanin lokaci waziri da Sarkin gida suka shigo cikin ɗakin jin ihun da Yarima Muntazir ya saki cirko cirko sukayi ganin yanda Sarauniya Maimoon taki yarda da mutwar ƴarta yasa suka tabbatar mata da mutuwar gimbiya Hunaisa a kiɗime ta zaro takobi tace duk wanda yakara cewa gimbiya Hunaisa ta mutu tabbas saitayi ajalin shi kowaye shi kallan kallo aka shiga yi sakanin Sarkin gida da waziri kowa nasan yin magana amman sun kasa hakan yasa ɗaya bayan ɗaya suka zare suna jimamin halin da uwar gijiyar tasu ta shiga. 


a lokacin da suka fito daga sashen Sarauniya Maimoon tamkam suka iske ƴan fa'da kowa da kalar tambayar da yake masu sun kasa amsa tambayar da akaimasu shiko Yarima Haris yana tunkaro sashen mahaifiyar tashiya iske cinci rindon mutane tsaitsaye kowa yai jugum a hankali ya iso Wajen yana kallan su ɗaya bayan ɗaya ganin yanda idanun Sarkin gida suka kaɗa hakan ya tabbatar mashi ba lafiya da sauri ya karasa jiki a mace ya tambaye shi ba'asi ba yanda zaiyi haka ya zayyanewa Yarima Haris duk abinda ke faruwa, kowa hankalin shi ya gama tashi danko mata da kananan yara suka hau kuwa da kuka jin labarin mutuwar gimbiya Hunaisa a ruɗe Yarima Haris ya faɗa cikin ɗakin Sarauniya Maimoon yanda ya iske su ya tabbatar da basu cikin hayyacin su musamman Yarima Muntazir saboda abokin ta gwaicin gimbiya Hunaisa ne shiyasa shakuwar su tayi yawa tafiya yakeyi kamar kasa bata iya ɗaukar shi, yana isa ya ɗauki zanin gado ya janye mahaifiyar tasu akan ta ya rufe fuskar gimbiya Hunaisa ya koma gefe ko kuka ya kasayi dan firgita da ganin irin kisan gillar da akaima kanwar shi da kallo Sarauniya Maimoon tabi Haris gyaɗa mata kai yayi alamar dagaske mutuwa tayi ba mafarki suke ba, dan ya fahimci basu gasgata mutuwar gimbiya ba, jin yanda aka shaki wuyan shi ya dawo dashi dogon tunanin shi kallan Sarauniya Maimoon ya tsaya yi ganin kamar ta haukace ta rasa tunanin ta "karya ne Haris ƴata bata mutuba ka tabbatar mani sa haka, kace Sarrilƙus bai galaba akaina ba ka tabbatar ma mahaifiyar ka da hakan karkace ƴar uwar ka ta mutu." wasu zafafan hawaye suka fara sintiri a idon shi, ganin halin da ta shiga a hankali ya janye ta suka bar ɗakin ya kaita wani yana lallashin ta sawa yayi akai mashi kiran Jakadiyya tana isowa ta zube yabata umurnin kulawa da Sarauniya Maimoon, sannan ya koma haka aka ɗauki gawar gimbiya Hunaisa aka tafi binneta danko rabata da Yarima Muntazir bakaramin artaba akasha ba, lallai Masarautar Daborah tayi rashin gimbiya mai adalci a wancan zamanin kowa na kaunar ta.


Yarima Malik ne ya kalli Dawud sannan yace. "Ya aka kare da Sarauniya Maimoon kuma wane mataki ta ɗauka da a wajen Sarrilƙus."? 

Numfasawa yayi sannan yace. "Kwarai kuwa Malik abunda nike shakkar sanar maku kenan dan akwai taɓa zuwa a cikin lamarin amman zan faɗa maku abinda nasani dan ku kiyaye sannan kusan hanyar da zakubi wajen karɓo Bahijja."


Saida aka kwashe sama da wata ɗaya sannan aka samu hayyacin Sarauniya Maimoon, bayan ta dawo cikin tunanin ta tafara haɗa jaruman Masarautar ta suka fara shirin tunkarar Masarautar Raifasu dan sun san danjin gamki yafi karfin tunanin su, saida suka ɗauki sati guda cur suna shirin yaki kafin nan suka kama hanyar tafiya Raifasu kwanan su biyu suka isa tareda turama Sarki Gursu sakon Sarauniya Maimoon, wannan karon ba karamin rikiɗa yayi ba da ganin rundunar mayakan Sarauniya Maimoon,da sauri ya tada gangamin yaki ba wata yar jejeniya data shiga tsakanin su aka kaure da yaki baji bagani dan Sarauniya Maimoon da tawwagar ta a wannan lokacin zukatan su a bushe yake dan ɗaukar fansar ran gimbiya Hunaisa da sukazo ɗauka a Masarautar Raifasu kuma sun san dole boka Sarrilƙus zai ɗauki mataki a kansu, kunsan miyafaru a wajen yakin." Huzaifa ne yace. "Aaa bamu sani ba ya Dawud." cewa yayi.... 




*Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*




Babi na 20



"A lokacin da suka shiga cikin Masarautar Raifasu, aka fara fafatawa tsakanin jaruman su, baji ba gani yaki yayi yaki Yarima Muntazir da Yarima Haris su sukaja tawagar jaruman Daborah, haka sukaita shiga cikin rundunar Raifasu sunai masu kisan gillah kamar ba gobe daman can Yarima Hasir barde ne na yaki gashi baida tsoro, suna cikin wannan yakin Sarki Gursu ya fahimci Yarima Hasir na neman karar mashi da Jarumai inya shiga cikin da'ga yana iya karar da rabi dan jarumta baiji bai gani danko Sarauniya Maimoon tana gefe ita da hadiman ta kura tai kura yaki yai yaki baka ganin kowa banda ƙura sai karar takubba da masu, sai sauke kan maza akeyi, ana cikin haka Yarima Hasir ya fille kan ɗan Sarki Gursu yasa mashi ya soke shi ya ɗaga shi sama yayi magana mai karar gaske, duk wanda ke wajen saida ya dakata dan firgita dajin amon maganar Yarima Hasir yana cewa. "Kai matsiyacin Sarki asararre kana ina ko zaka iya shaida wannan kan waye? Ko baku fahimci wannan kan na jahilin ɗanka bane wai shi Jarumi ko har kake tunanin zai iya haɗa kafaɗa da Yarima Hasir na sare kansa kuma na soke shi ko kana da abinyi a halin yanzun ban faɗa maku ran gimbiya Hunaisa fansa bane a garemu toh yanzun wasan zai fara." yana gama faɗa yayi wurgi da kan Yarima Busru ba inda ya tsaya sai gaban Sarki Gursu jikin shi na kyarma ya duka ya ɗauki gundun kan yana mamakin irin cin kashin da Yarima Hasir yaima ɗansa, wata gigitattar ƙara ya saki yana buga ihu duk wanda ke wajen yakin nan saida ya girgiza daga Jaruman shi harna Daborah har girgiza kasar wajen keyi ga wata irin siraci da hayaki na fita daga bakin shi da gudu yayo kan Yarima Hasir suka game baki daya wajen ya sake harkewa da kurar yaƙi sukaita fafatawa har tartsatsin wutar takubbai kakeji kamar aradu, itama Sarauniya Maimoon shigowa tayi ganin yaki yana kamari, ranar anyi ɗauki ba dadi jini ya koma kamar ruwa shiko Sarki Gursu ganin bazai iya cin galaba akan Yarima Hasir ga ba zobe a hannun shi balle ya kira rundunar aljanun boka Sarrilƙus ya tallafa mashi ji yayi baki ɗaya jikin shi ya fara kyarma ga gajiya duk ta addabi rayuwar shi wani wawan sara yakawo ma Yarima Hasir ya sare shi a saman kafaɗa take jini ya balle ba kyaun gani ganin haka yasa Sarauniya Maimoon tayo kan Sarki Gursu Yarima Muntazir dake ta fama da abokan gaba ya rike mahaifiyar su ya maida ta wani waje yana bata baki gudun kar wani ya illata ta shiko Yarima Hasir jini ne ya ɗibe shi dukawa yayi ƙasa kamar mai neman sikewa hakan yaba Yarima Hasir dama Sarki Gursu ya rugo da gudu zai sauke kan Yarima Hasir aiko cikin gafin nama Yarima Hasir ya sarema Sarki Gursu kafa take ya faɗi da kafa ɗaya ya sake ɗaga takobi ya fille gudar kafar kwaɗa uwar kara yayi da uban ihu mai ruɗarwa take ya ɓace bat kamar aljani da Jaruman shi sukaga abinda ya faru daman gashi ankusan karar dasu suka fara guduwa suko Jaruman Daborah suka dinga binsu da ruwan kibau waanda suka sha yan kaɗan nan sannan Sarauniya Maimoon tasa aka cinnama kayan tsafin Sarki Gursu wuta ranar saida akaima Masarautar Raifasu kaca kaca, dan ko kaɗan yakin nan baizomasu da dadi ba, saidai abinda baku sani ba koda Sarki Gursu ya ɓace yana hannun boka Sarrilƙus kusan abinda ya biyo baya."? Cikin sauri dasan jin karshen labarin yakin Yarima Malik yace. "Kai muke sauraro ina alajabin bacewar Sarki Gursu ammn nasan da sannu zamuji komai."

Jinjina kai Dawud yayi sannan yace.

"take boka Sarrilƙus ya haɗa gangamin yaki marar kyaun gani ya tunkari Masarautar Daborah suna isa cikin Masarauta kowa bai huta balle ya koma mahallin shi sukaji magana mai tada hankalin mai sauraro ana cewa. "Maimoon kina nufin kunci bulus harkina takama ke jajirtatta ce toh kisani ayau zan shafe babin ki a doran kasa baki ɗaya keda ƴaƴan ki baki ɗaya kashe Yarima Busru kamar lalata komai ne a rayuwar ku." waige waige suka shiga yi amman basu ganin komai basu gama alajabi ba suka ga kan bokan su kasa take kowa ya wartsaka da takubba  sai ɗauki guda ake masu ganin abin bana wasa bane alamu sun nuna ma Sarauniya Maimoon da jinni suke faɗa kuma lallai zasu iya karar dasu yasa ta fara magana da karfi cikin fushi tace. "Kai karamin ɗan isa lusari inkai kacika Sarkin tsafi gamki ka bayyana kanka kuma kasa jaruman ka su bayyana mana kansu a fafata taya zakuyi yaki ba'a ganin ku."?  a take Sarrilƙus yasa suka bayyana a sifar wasu gawurtattun mutane ba kyaun gani haka aka cigaba da fafatawa saidai basujin sara da duka dan girma da karfi tun suna da karfi har suka saduda ganin abin yana tsamari yasa Yarima Hasir shiga gidan tarihi ya ɗauko wukar Barri ya shigo filin da'ga duk wanda ya sara da wukar sai ya wargaje haka yaita kasar mutanen boka Sarrilƙus duk sara ɗaya da zaiyi sama da goma na iya bacewa shiko Sarrilƙus sai gabza ihu yakeyi kamar shi ake sara ana cikin haka aka kashe Wazirin Daborah



intakai cemaku labarin Sarauniya Maimoon da Sarrilƙus da irin artabar da sukayi haka akaita yaki har dare ba'a gama wannan artabar ba, saida aka kusan karar da jaruman Masarautar Daborah saboda yakine da akeyin shi harda aljanu danma Yarima Haris yana ɗauke da takobin Barri shine saukin Jaruman na Daborah amman abinda baku sani ba yaki da aljani ba dadi ana cikin kafta yakin da saida aka kwana ana abu ɗaya daman dukkan su ba wani addini ke akwai ba a zamanin ba abinda suka sani sai sihiri da tsafi shine adon su haka Sarrilƙus ya kashe Yarima Muntazir wata irin riki tatar tsawa Haris ya saki ganin ankashe mashi dan uwa, wata walikiyar tsafi ce ta gilma ta wajen wani uban tsalle ya doka da takobin Barri ya sari wannan walkiyar tsafin da ya gani take wajen yayi dundum banda kara da ihu ba abinda ke tashi cikin walkiyar bayan komai ya lafa Yarima Haris yayi kan Yarima Muntazir ta dugu shida Sarauniya Maimoon rige rige sukeyi da sauri ya rungumo shi ya fasa malalacin kuka mai sosa zuciya na rashin ɗan uwan shi da yayi, yana cikin kukan wani uban guguwar duhu ta tunkaro su, da sauri ya tashi ya ɗauki takobin Barri ya soketa shida wukar sukayi wanwar ba rai Sarauniya Maimoon ta ɗauki wukar akaci gaba da artaba da ita, saidai nasarar da aka samu ashe wannan walkiyar da ya sara da wuka ya yanki Sarrilƙus, sannan soka wukar da ya sake yi a karo na biyu ya soke shi a wuya wannan dalilin yasa yaƙin ya ɗan lafa na wasu lokuta, itako Sarauniya Maimoon ta kashe mutanen tsafin Sarrilƙus bila adadin tana buga masu takobi suna watsewa kamar kura daman duk na tsafi ne rashin sa'a aka halbo kibiya ta dira a kirjin Sarauniya Maimoon hakan ba karamin tsorata yan Daborah yai ba Ghali Sarkin yaki shi yaci gaba da jan ragamar bayan ɗauke Maimoon da sukayi ita da Haris dan basu kulawa koda Yarima ya farfaɗo yaga mahaifiyar shi rai hannu ga Allah bakaramin kidimewa yayi ba ya rungume ta sai kuka yakeyi ganin ba magani zai mashi ga yaki na kara kamari haka ya fita ya anshi takobi yaci gaba da yaki wutace ta kama gidan tarihi inda aka aje zoben Sarrilƙus ashe ɗauko shi aljani ɗan tsitu yayi daman shine musabbabin yakin da ake fama da shi shiki gamki yana kwance rai yakusa fita cikin fushi Yarima Haris ya ɗauki takobin Barri saida ya datse kan Sarrilƙus daman tunda ya soke shi ya koma mutun daga guguwar da yaima Yarima Haris, ihu dankuwa iri iri su suka cika wajen kafin kace tak, Sarrilƙus ya ɓace ashe tawagar shi ce suka ɗauke shi ganin kisan gilan da Yarima Haris yaima uban gidan su, marar kyaun gani, yan tsiraran Jarumai suka rage mana hakan yasa kowa ya tafi wajen Sarauniya Maimoon rai na neman kubuce mata aka hau bata kulawa ta musamman kaji takaitaccen tunkarar boka gamki Sarrilƙus da Sarauniya Maimoon tayi. 


Kallan shi Yarima Malik yayi ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace. "Lokacin sallah ya gabato saidai inada tambayar da zanmaka."

Cikin gamsuwa Dawud ya kalli Malik yace "Allah yataimaki magajin gobe girman kane inajinka."


"Lokacin da aka kashe Sarrilƙus na wancan zamanin akwai abinda ya sake bullowa sannan a lokacin Sarauniya Maimoon ta mutu kuma ya aka kare da Sarki Gursu bayan rasa sassan jikin shi da yayi, sannan zoben da suka ɗauka  anbar masu ko kuwa ankyale su tunda nasu bayan tafka babbar asara da zoben ya janyoma wannan Masarautar."?




"Lallai kayi tambaya mai kyau amman duba da lokacin sallah ya karato bari in takai cemaku wannan tarihi."


Cigaban labari, koda suka ɗauki boka gamki suka binne take aka naɗa sabon shugaba tsabibi ne na gaske wanda haryafi wanda ya mutu hatsabiban ci na gaske, haka aka mallaka zoben a Sarki Gursu kamar yanda kuka sani har yanzun Masarautar Raifasu bata daina aiki da tsafi ba sannan duk wanda yake mulkin Masarautar yana karkashin ikon Sarrilƙus shiyasa ake ba Sarkin zamanin zobe yana anfani dashi duk da babban zoben yana jikin boka Sarrilƙus dashi yake sarrafa komai, koda suka gama binne shi aka naɗa sabo yaci gaba da gudanar da mulki sannan suka ɗauki Gursu duk da yana da nakasa suka maida shi yacigaba da gabatar da sarauta kamar yanda suka tsara, a wajen Yarima Haris kuwa hankalin shi yai matukar tashi bayan sun binne Yarima Muntazir kusa da kabarin gimbiya Hunaisa suka dawo gida ana jinyar Sarauniya Maimoon saidai abinda baku sani ba tabbas tafara samun sauki sai Sarrilƙus ya turo aljani ya shanye mata jini tas, saida ta mutu sannan suka bayyana ma Masarautar Daborah shine ya kashe Sarauniya Maimoon a wannan karon Haris bai yunkurin ɗaukar fansa ba saboda yana son ya dawo da darajar Masarautar Daborah bayan durkushewar da tayi na rashin manyan mayaka da kuma kayan tarihi dan ma Masarautace mai kayan adana tarihi daban daban ba waje ɗaya ba, shiyasa Yarima Haris ya hakura amman yasha wahala kafin ya dangana da rashin mahaifiyar shi da yan uwan shi tundaga Yarima Jumal har zuwa Yarima Muntazir da gimbiya Hunaisa da ya rasa, hakan yasa bayan wani lokaci aka naɗa shi Sarkin Daborah bayan wata biyar komai ya kafa ya ɗauki Jaruman shi na amana guda goma duk dashi sukaje Masarautar Raifasu suka kaima Sarki Gursu hari saida sukai mashi gunduwa gunduwa sannan suka kashe Jaruman shi suka kama bayi sannan suka kone gidan tsafin Raifasu wanda suke kira da gidan tarihi a wajen su, tabbas sun masu asara, sannan ya aje wasika zuwa ga Sarrilƙus kamar haka. 


 _Daga Sarki Haris nasan zakayi mamakin ganin wannan sakon saidai ba abin mamaki bane duba da wanda ka taɓa Sarki Haris ke magana kuma ka kiyayi shiga hurumin Masarauta ta da jamaata inba haka ba yanzun wasan zaifara_ 


Haka ya dawo yacigaba da gudanar da mulki kamar yanda ya saba, duk da suna cikin shirin hari akoda yaushe, har aka ɗauki tsawan shekaru goma ba'a sake kawo ko wane hari ba a cikin Masarautar Daborah, har Sarki Haris ya samu haihuwa ƴaƴa ukku, da Maimoon sai Hunaisa inda Muntazir shine babban ɗan Sarki Haris a ranar nan Maimoon ne aka aiko mashi da sakon yaki daga Masarautar Waisu duk da yai mamaki ganin ba dasu yake arta baba, ashe Masarautun sun yi hadin guiwa dan cimma burin su akan Sarki Haris da zuriar sa musamman akan gimbiya Hunaisa da ya ɗauki son duniya ya aza mata. 


Murmushi Dawud yayi yace. "Yarima kaji abinda zan iya faɗi akan labarin ko tarihin Sarki Haris  na IV da tawagar shi da yanda Sarauniya Maimoon ya tayi artaba da Sarrilƙus."


"Hakane saidai wane buri suka ɗaura akan gimbiya Hunaisa"? Mikewa Dawud yayi ya dafa kafaɗar Malik sannan ya kuma cewa. "Idan mukace zamuci gaba da labarin Sarakuna da Sarauniyoyin da akayi a cikin wannan Masarautar gaskia bazai kareba, saidai wani lokacin ranka shidade." haka Yarima Malikussaif  dasu Huzaifa suka mike suma suka tafi cikin al'ajabi da fargabar halin da Bahijja ke ciki. 




*Yar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*


*NO EDITED ❌*

Babi na 21&22



 
 _RAIFASU_ 




Bahijja keta aiki kamar ba gobe ga wata irin zazzaɓi daya adda beta amman a haka take tayima gimbiya Yazira wanki tun safe take abu ɗaya ta gama wannan aikin za'akaro mata wani tun tanayi cikin jin dadi harta fara sarewa uwar bayi ce tazo ta watsa ma Bahijja kayaiyaki masu yawan gaske ta hauta da masifa. "Ke Bahijja wace irin sakarar yarinya ce ke wai? tun yaushe kike abu ɗaya toh nabaki nan da minti talatin muddin baki karasa ba sai jikin ki ya gayamaki sakara kawai."

Itadai Bahijja batace komai ba haka ta dinga jibgar uban wankin ta, tana kwallah ga ƙudunta nai mata uban ciwo, Zainab ce tazo wajen ta tana kallan ta tana aiki dukawa tayi ta fara tayata da sauri Bahijja ta rike hannun tace. "Kirufaman asiri Zainab kinaso baba Uwani ta dukeni dan Allah kibari nagode." ɓata rai Zainab tayi tana cewa. "wai Bahijja sai yaushe zaki daina yin wannan addinin naki ne inkika bari akajiki za'a iya illataki fa!" 



Murmushi tayi tace. "Zainab kowa yai addinin sa kinga kitafi wajen aikin ki kada baba Uwani tazo ta sameki anan wlh inajin tsoro!" shikenan amman kibari ko shanya inmaki." ba yanda Bahijja ta iya haka tabar Zainab tacigaba da yimata shanya suba ƴar fira jefi jefi jin maganar uwar bayi tayi akanta tana cewa. "Ke Bahijja kin raina mutane ko? Ke kuma Zainab uban wa yakawo ki nan ina kikabar aikin naki"? "Baba uwani nagama nawa aikin shiyasa." cikin faɗa uwani tace. "Ɓaceman dagani badab uwarki ba da sainai maki dukan tsiya daga yau idan kika ƙara taya Bahijja aiki sai jikin ki ya gayamaki maza kibani wuri." da sauri ta tafi jikin ta na ɓari, ta juyo kan Bahijja ta fara duka tana dungurin ta "dan ubanki wace irin kidahuma ceke, bance karki bari a tayaki wanki nan ba? sannan kuma banace karki bari lokacin dana ɗibar maki ya cika ba baki gama ba"? 


Sai kuka Bahijja keyi tana ba Uwani hakuri amman saida tasa bulala ta gama zanar Bahijja sannan taci gaba da wankin ta, ba ita tagama wankin ba sai marece liƙis tana gamawa ta shiga cikin sashen gimbiya Yazira, ta isketa kwance asaman ƙayatattar shimfidar ta hadimai nai mata fita dukawa tayi tace. "Ranki shidade nagama wankin."  a wulakance ta kalli bahijja kamar taga wani kashi ta yatsuna fuska tace. "Yiman tausan kafata." nan Bahijja taci gaba da yimata tausa sai kyakkyarwa jikin ta keyi zazzaɓi ya rufeta ringis kwalla ne suka suɓucema Bahijja sai a saman kafar gimbiya Yazira kallan tuhuma ta fara jifar Bahijja dashi ƴan cikin ta taji sun kaɗa take taji wani irin zawo ya kamata, jikin ta na ɓari tace. " tuba nike ranki shidade ban kula ba.....ai bata ida rufe bakin taba taji saukar mari mai firgutar wa, ta dafe wajen tana cigaba daba gimbiya Yazira hakuri, sake wanke ta da wani marin saida bakinta ya fashe wani irin juwa da ta dinga ɗiban Bahijja takasa ko magana, cike da kyama gimbiya Yazira ta kora Bahijja kamar mai jira haka ta fita sai jiri ke ɗibar ta a daddafe ta tafi waje tana tafiya juwace ta kadata kasa su  Zainab da Indo suka tadda Bahijja kwance nan suka ɗauketa suka kaita gida....... 





_DABORAH_




Shiko yarima Malik cikin dare yana kwance yana bacci sai yan juye juye yakeyi mafarki yayi, ga Bahijja nan, tana ta kuka dogarawa suna dukan ta da bulalai tana kwara amai, kamar zata fitar da ƴan cikin ta wani shirgegen mutun ya gani ya fisgota yana cewa inbata bata kanta ba zai sare kanta, dai dai nan Malim ya tashi da addua bakin shi, sai uban zufa yake ketawa cikin daren ya tashi yayi wanka sannan ya ɗauro alwalla yayi nafila da addu'oi sannan ya koma ya kwanta yana tunanin halin da Bahijja ke ciki yasakama ranshi zuwa gobe zaije ya nemo Bahijja duk inda ta shiga a cikin kasar Gambia..... 



Hakadai  Malik ya kwanta rabi bacci har asuba tayi koda ya tafi masallaci bashi ya dawo sashen shiba sai wajen goma wanka kawai ya sake kaya sannan ya karya yatafi wajen Sarauniya Zulaiƙa, koda ya shiga a falo ya sameta itada Jakadiya gaishe shi hadiman ta sukayi samun wuri yayi ya zauna nan Jakadiya ta sallami hadimai aka barshi da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa, kallan shi tayi tsaf take tagane ɗan nata yana cikin damuwa, a hankali cikin disashshar murya ya gaida ta, ta kada daina kallan ɗan nata cikin kulawa tace. "Malik meke damunka."?  Shima kallam mahaifiyar tashi yayi ya gilgiza mata kai alamar bakomai, cikin rashin yadda Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace. "Karya batai maka kyau Malikussaif kafaɗaman ni mahaifiyar kace."? 


"Ummu ina cikin damuwa kullin da Bahijja nike kwana ina tashi ina shakkar halin da zata tsinci kanta a wajen miyagun mutanen nan.! " a sheƙe ta kara kalan shi dan ita harta fara mantawa da batun wata Bahijja can, ɗan karamin tsoki tayi wanda saida mmakin hakan ya bayyana a saman fuskar Malik, sannan tace. "Kawani damu da lamarin Bahijja ince ita baiwa ce ko? Mene a cikin dan an sadaukar da rayuwar baiwa akan uban gidan ta, ai naɗauka dakai da kaya duk malakar wuya ne kaga dakabar maganar Bahijjan nan!!!"



Wani irin mummunan faɗuwar gaba ta riski Malik yana mamakin kalar butulci irin na mahaifiyar shi, ko kuma yace wane irin tsana taima Bahijja a duniya, a zahiri sai ya riko hannun ga yace., "Ummu kidaina faɗan haka kiga yanda yarinyar nan iyayenta suka sadaukar da ita akaina!" "yimin shiru Malik saime dan an sadaukar da baiwa akanka ince uwarta ma ana iya sadaukar wa bare ƴar tatsitsiyar baiwar yarinyar nan, dan haka ka manta da rayuwar ta."


Wani irin ciwon kai ya dirar mashi a take bakin shi na rawa yafara magana. "Ummu dan Allah kibari inje in ɗauko Bahijja ina tausayin halin da zata tsinci kanta, kuma kinga iyayen ta zasu damu kodan addinin tama kimin lamani!" fusgi hannun ta tayi "sai me kaga ka fidda aranka ni Zulaiƙa banajin zan iya bari kaje ɗauko wata ƙasƙantattar baiwa a matsayin na magajin  Masarautata dan haka daga nan kabar wannan sakaran zancen Bahijja me da zaka damu kanka!"


Dai dai nan Uwar bayi Binta ta ɗauko tiredin abinci zata kawoma Sarauniya Zulaiƙa jin abinda take cawa yasa jikin Binta yahau bari har ta kusa da ɓarar da abinci wani matsiyacin kallo Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta watsa mata jiki a mace tazo ta aje abinci sannan ta gaida Sarauniya Zulaiƙa da yarima Malik, fuska sake ya amsa yana gaida ta, kallan da Sarauniya taimata kaɗai yasa ta gane nufin ta ɗaukar flat tayi ta zuba mata abincin sannan zata zubama Malik yace mata karta samashi a koshe yake, sake kallan ta Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi hakan yasa ta zubo ruwa a kofi ta mikoma Sarauniya Zulaiƙa, ganin uwar hararar data wurgamata yasa kofin hannun ta ya suɓuce mata ya wanke ma Sarauniya Zulaiƙa jiki ba Binta hatta Malik saida yaji gaban shi ya faɗi, ahi Binta tafara tana kwallah amman ko ajikin Zulaiƙa tayi kamar ta yafe mafa tacigaba da yima Malik gargaɗi akan Bahijja ko nauyin Binta bataji, shiko yakasa ko motsi dan kunya data addabi Malik, saida tabari Binta ta kara sakankance wa ta waketa da mari lafiyayyu har guda ukku, wani uban kara ta saki tana bata hakuri sai kyarma takeyi tace. "Allah yahuci zuciyar uwar gijiya ta ban kulaba na jikaki kimin aikin rai Binta na tuba Allah yataimakeki!" ɗaga mata hannu Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi cikin gadara tace. "Wato ni zakiyima hauka hankalin ki yana ina zaki zubamani ruwa, jikin kine zai gaya maki dani kike magana!"


Ganin yanda ran mahaifiyar shi ya ɓaci tana wulakanta mutanen da sukayi masu halacci dan kawai suna bayi akan wata hujja tata marar usuli, yasa Yarima Malik yace. "Allah yahuci ran Sarauniya kiyimata aikin rai taba kula ba hakan bazata kuma faruwa." kallan shi kawai tayi sannan tabata umurnin kwashe kwanukan abincin ta tafi, itako Sarauniya Zulaiƙa sai huci take tana kara tsanar Binta da ƴarta a cikin ranta, haka Yarima Malik yagama bata hakuri sannan yaimata sallama ranshi ba daɗi ya tafi sashen shi ya aika wata baiwar shi taima shi kiran Huzaifa.......



Koda Aliya taje wajen Huzaifa iske shi tayi zaune dasu Lurwan a wajen dawakai cikin wata bagala suna fira duƙawa tayi tagaida su, sannan ta sanarma Huzaifa kiran Malik, da sauri ya mike yasan ba lafiya tunda har yaimashi aike, kusan tare suka isa, shiya shiga cikin bedroom din Malik ita kuma taci gaba da ƴan aikace aikacen ta, iske shi yai kwance yana kalan silin alamar mai tunni yakai wajen minti biyar baisan da shigowar Huzaifa ba jin antaɓa shi yadawo dashi a cikin tunanin daya ninkaya, zama yayi gefen abokin nashi cikeda tambayoyi masu tarin yawa yake kalan Malik shima kallan shi yakeyi bakin shi yaimashi nauyi ga wani tuƙuƙi ya tokare mashi ƙahon zuciya Huzaifa yai karfin halin jefoma Malik tambayoyi. "Allah yataimaki yariman Daborah bansaba ganin wannan yalwatattar kyakyawar fuskar taka a cikin damuwa ba irin wannan abokina faɗaman mike faruwa duk jikina yai sanyi mike damunka"? 


Take idanun Malik sun canza launi ya kara ɗaura ma Huzaifa  su, saida yasa ya gilgiza sake jefomashi tambayar Huzaifa yayi, cikin dakewa Yarima Malik yace. "Mezakace da mutanen da akaima halacci suka sakanta da saɓanin hakan"?  ɗan shiru Huzaifa yayi na dakiƙa yana nazartar abokin nashi, ya tabbata koma mene da mahaifiyar shi yake yasan inba ita zata saka Malik a cikin yanayi ba ba wanda ya isa ya jefa Malik cikin wannan yanayin kaf Masarautar Daborah, "Allah yataimaki magajin gobe zan iya saka wannan mutanen a layin masu ajizanci na ɗan Adam." 


Wani wawan kallo Malik ya jefama Huzaifa marar kyaun gani sannan yace. "A iya nan kawai zaka saka su."?  "Kwarai kuwa abokina sannan zan masi uzuri watakil wani lokacin in zukatan mutanen ya huci suyi halacci ga waanda sukai masu kwatan kwacin hakan." mikewa Malik yayi ya goya hannayen shi a bayan shi yana zagaye ɗakin yakai minti goma yana abu ɗaya sannan ya dafa kafaɗar Huzaifa yace. "Lallai Huzaifa sai yanzun nagane banbancin ka da Yazid a matsayin ka wanda ya tashi a gidan sarauta zaka faɗa mani haka!" bakaramin mamki Huzaifa yaiba da Malik ke wannan maganar, "ban fahimce kaba abokina koda Yazid iyakar abinda zai faɗa maka kenan." ɗaga mashi hannu Malik yayi dan harya gaji da magana, shiko Huzaifa baiyi mamaki sanin halin abokin nashi yanda magana ke mashi wahala aiyai kokarima a wannan lokacin, duk da yana san yasan dalilin dayasa Malik ke wannan tambayoyin, haɗa ido sukayi da Malik, yasan cewa akwai tarin tambayoyi da Huzaifa kesan yimashi dan yaga hakan kwance a saman fuskar shi, "Ummu ta tsani mahaifiyar Bahijja kasan tanani zuwa ɗauko Bahijja bayan tace baiwa ce marar galihu abin wulakantar wa, Huzaifa sai yaushe zan nunama mahaifiya ta illar wulakanta ɗan Adam sai yaushe zatasan darajar bayi shin ita ba uwa bace sai yaushe Ummu zata _gane cewa *BAYIMA* *'ƳA'ƳA'NE*_ kamar kowa"? 


Ganin halin da Yarima Malik ke ciki saida yasa Huzaifa ya tausaya mashi badan yasan halin abokin nashi ba nasan taimakon bayi da talakawa dayace son Bahijja yakeyi amman bai fatan hakan ta kasance, dan yasan Malik zai fuskanci kalubale wajen mahaifiyar shi da sauran ƴan fa'da musamman wazirin Daborah wato mahaifin Huzaifa, amman a zahiri numfasawa yayi ya tausasa murya yace. "Kayi hakuri Malik zanma mahaifina magana kodan kusancin dake tsakanin shi da Ummu, nasan zai shawo kan wannan matsalar saidai ya kamata aima Bahijja adalci kodan yanda iyayen ta suka sadaukar da ita akan ceto rayuwar ka, ya kamata Sarauniya takawo karsheb tsanar da tayima iyayen bayi kodan haka, sannan yakamata agane sun zama bayin amana bakamar ba baya ba." 
"Abinda nikeso insanar da mahaifiya ta kenan Huzaifa saidai kaje ka nemoma mu mafita bansan mahaifina yaji wannan maganar duk da shima sona ya rufe mashi ido ya manta da rayuwar Bahijja!"

Sake dafa kafaɗar Malik yayi sannan yace. "Allah yakara girma karka saka damuwa a ranka zamu amso Bahijja kodan addinin ta da darajar ta, kuma kasan bamu da ilimi ko dubarar tarar Sarrilƙus kuma kasan hatsarin sa!" wani shu'umin murmushi ya saki duk da baiyi mamakn jin haka daga Huzaifa ba sai ya samu kanshi da bashi amsa" har yanzun na fahimci baka san matsayin Bahijja a cikin zuciya taba ko Huzaifa? Inasan zan samu hutu inka fita kasanar ma Aliya kartabari kowa ya sake zuwa ganina ace ina bacci sai anjima abokina sannan kayi kokarin sama mana mafita." gyaɗa mashi kai kawai yayi ya kama hanyar barin ɗakin harya kama handle din kofa zai fita ya tsinkayi maganar Malik a kunnuwan sa. "Ka kwatanta tarayyar ko dangantakar dake tsakanin da gimbiya ka dan tata da Bahijja akaina, saina jika." murmushi Huzaifa yayi ma Malik sannan ya rufo mashi kofa ya fita yana ba Aliya sakon Yarima Malik ya wuce wajen mahaifin shi..


 _Masarautar Waisu_

Yarima Haishir ne zaune awajen mahaifin shi suna magana akan harkar kasuwancin da zasu kulla da Masarautar Ƙaisu. "Haishir inasan kasamu lokaci kaida hadiman ka kukai ziyara wajen Sarki Sibbur kaji yanda harkar kasuwancin mu zaya kasance, sannan inasan inkadawo zamukai ma Sarki Baidulƙais ziyara."


Kallan mamaki Yarima Haishir yaima mahaifin nashi yai mamakin jin yace zasu kaima Sarkin Raifasu ziyara bayan yasan kudurin mahaifin shi akan Masarautar Raifasu....shiko Sarki Lahazu yanda yaga ɗan nashi yai shiru kamar mai nazari yasan yana da tambayoyin da yake son yi mashi hakan yasa yace. "Kana mamakin zamuje wajen Baidulƙais ko? Kabar mamki akwai abinda nikeson idona yagane mani a cikin masarautar shi daga ranar zan ɗauraka akan kudurina kaidai ka maida hankali akan abinda na saka, in komai yai kyau a lokacin wasan zai fara ɗana kaine farin cikina Haishir kaida ka dage kazam garzo kuma Jarumi kamar yanda labarin Malikussaif ya karaɗe duniya musamman kasar mu ta Gambia da nahiyar mu baki ɗaya!" wani shu'umin murmushi Yarima Haishir yayi yana mai jin dadin abinda mahaifin nashi keyi sanin kanshi ne ba wanda yakeso yaga bayan a kaf duniya sama da Yarima Malik, da wannan tunanin yace. "Gamji yataimakeka Sarki mai dole karka damu ba abinda zai gagari Yarima Haishir ka kara sama ranka alamun nasara akoda yaushe." cikin jin dadi ya rungume ɗansa jikin sa sukaci gaba da maganganun su rabi duk akan Masarautar Daborah ne da kudurin su akanta....... 




 _RAIFASU......._ 




Koda Bahijja ta tashi da kyal take tafiya ba laifi zazzabin ya sauka ba kamar jiya da dare ba dan da ita ta kwana kamar bazata kwana ba, uwar bayi ce ta biyo ta ɗakin su ta tasheta, itadasu indo suka tafi aiki kamar kullin ko abinci bata ciba, haka suka tafi a hanyar tafiya wajen Gimbiya Yazira suka haɗu da Zainab itada Umman ta gaida sukayi sannan suka tafi suna fira jefi jefi har kowa ya tafi wajen aikin shi, itadai Bahijja koda ta jiyo muryar uwar bayi na rabama bayi aiki kasa shiga tayi dan tsoro tasan zata bata aiki wanda yafi karfin ta, ta rasa dalilin dayasa jinin ta bai haɗu dana Uwani, takai minti goma tana tsoron shiga, jin antaɓata yasa Bahijja takusan suman tsaye saidai Jakadiya tagane tana mata murmushi, dukawa Bahijja tayi tagaida Jakadiya, cikin sakin fuska ta amsa sannan ta kamo hannun Bahijja suka shiga cikin sashen Gimbiya Yazira, duk bayin dake wajen suka zube suna kwasar gaisuwa wajen Jakadiya harda uwani itako bahijja kanta kasa ta kasa kallan uwani dan tsoro tashi sukayi kowa ya kama aikin gaban shi nan Jakadiya tacema uwani, yau tabata Bahijja zata taya masu abinci aiki a madafa, ba haka Uwani taso ba, amman bazata iya musu da Jakadiya ba, haka suka tafi madafa Bahijja sai murna takeyi daman ga uwar yunwa dake ɗawainiya da ita lura da hakan yasa Jakadiya takaita Madafa, koda suka isa saida tasama Bahijja abinci taci ta koshi sannan tace ma uwar tuwo ga yarinya ta taya ta aiki amman karsu bata mai wahala koyon girki zatayi, nan uwar tuwo taba Bahijja yankan albasa suka cigaba da dafa abinci, sannan Jakadiya ta koma wajen Sarki Baidulƙais dan gabatar da nata aikin........



A bangaren Masarautar Daborah kuwa Saifulislam ne wajen dawakai yana ran gadi dan mike kafafunsa da yakejin sun masa tsami, suna cikin tafiya ta gefen taskar bayi yaga Sarkin bulala yakama wata dattijuwar mata yanata dukan ta kamar zaikashe ta, da sauri Saifulislam yasa bawan shi Jamil ya kira mashi Sarkin bulala, yana zuwa ya zube kasa yakai gasuwa gun Saifulislam,tambayar da ya watsa mashi saida yasa jikin shi yafada ɓari, inda inda yafara ya rasa mizai cema Saifulislam, daka mashi tsawa yayi yace. "Badakai nikeba."? 


"Ranka shidade kaimin aikin rai natuba!" wanke shida mari yayi yace badakai nike magana ba." "ranka shidade wannan baiwar da kake gani ba wani abu yasa ba duke ta umurni ne daga Jakadiya, tuba nike ranka shidade." tisa keyar sa Saifulislam yayi ba inda suka tsaya sai bangaren Sarauniya Zulaiƙa acan ya tadda Jakadiya tana ganin yanda Saifulislam ya shigo saida taji gabanta ya mummunar faɗuwar gaba, kiran Jakadiya yayi ta duka ta gaida shi, kallan kallo aka dinga yi tsakanin Jakadiya da Sarkin bulala "ranka shidade ina tuba ni nasaka shi hukunta Laure saboda bata zuwa aiki kullin shiyasa." bakaramin ɓacin rai ya taɓa Saifulislam ba yace. "Miyasa ta haka zaku hukun tata dan haka zaku ɗandani kuɗar ku, kina jagora a cikin Masarautar nan amma kike sa ana zalinci, Sarkin yari ya kira yasa aka kai mashi Jakadiya saitayi kwana ukku, sannan yasa aka zane Sarkin bulala shima aka kaishi gidan yari duk abin nan agaban Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tasan halin mijinta sarai bayasan shashanci da zalinci, amman ta ɗauki aniyar saita hukunta Laure tunda akanta har Saifulislam ya hukunta Jakadiyar ta..... 




a wajen Bahijja kuwa tunda suka gama haɗama Sarki Baidulƙais abinci mai kula da kai mashi abinci tazo da bayi suka ɗauki abinci, itako Bahijja tafiya zatayi sai uwar tuwo taga anmanta basu ɗauki kayan itacen da zasukai ma Sarki Baidulƙais, dakatar da Bahijja tayi tasa ɗaukar tire din fruit tace takaima Sarki, ba dan taso ba ta ɗauki tiren har bakin shiga falon Sarki, tunawa tayi da gargaɗin da gimbiya Yazira tai mata akan mijin ta, shiga tayi kanta kasa duk jikin ta yayi sanyi akan sa'a ta iske gimbiya Yazira kishingiɗe da hadimai sai Sarki Baidulƙais daya zauna ana mashi fiffita, tunda Bahijja ta shigo Sarki Baidulƙais ya kafeta da mayun idanun sa, masu furgitarwa yana kallan dirin da Bahijja ke dashi, daman kayan jikin ta rigace doguwa sai kallabi data ɗaure kanta dashi, Jakadiya da gimbiya Yazira sukabi Baidulƙais da kallo ganin yanda yake lashe baki yana kallan Bahijja, harta iso bata yarda ta kalli su ido da ido ba, dukawa tayi ta aje kayan sannan ta gaida su, a hankali ta mike zata bar wajen har takai bakin kofa taji Sarki Baidulƙais ya kira sunan ta, jikin ta na rawabta juyo ta taho har hardewa takewa ta duka gaban shi, a hankali ta kalle shi, aiko suka haɗa ido da gimbiya Yazira tai saurin kawar da nata a hankali duk abin nan Jakadiya na kallan abinda ke faruwa, jin maganar Sarki Baidulƙais ya dawo da Bahijja daga ruɗewar da tayi yace. "Miyasa kika daina zuwa wajena bayan umurnin dana baki ba kula da abinci na? Ko har yanzun baki warke ba Bahijja." duk ra daburce tace. "Ranka shidade ban lafiya shiyasa Bahijja ba tuba."


"Daga yau ke zaki dinga kula da abincina tun daga safe har rana.'' gyaɗa kai tayi alamar eh, itako gimbiya Yazira ji take kamar ta kashe Bahijja ta huta musamman da tasan kudurin mijin ta akan Bahijja lallai dole sai ta ɗauki mataki akan ta, haka ta gama zuba masu abinci abin mamaki sai ya sake kallan Bahijja alamar ta bashi abaki, ba yanda ta iya haka ta dinga ɗiba tana bashi har haɗawa yake da hannun ta kamar maye, ko cokali bai bari ta bashi dashi saida hannun ta, itako gimbiya Yazira tagama kulewa ko abincin takasa ci aranta ko cewa take taya zaace ga ta amman baiwa tana ba mijin ta abinci a baki saitayi ajalin Bahijja, ganin yanda Sarki Baidulƙais ke sumbatu kamar wani maye yasa gimbiya Yazira ta saki kofin dake hannun ta ya wanke Bahijja a fuska ta mike ta fita a fusace kamar zakanya, shiko yanda yaga soɓo ya wankema Bahijja fuska wani iri yaji Jakadiya ce tazo tana gyara wajen ɗaga mata hannu yayi alamar ta daina, itako Bahijja idanun ta duk sunyi jajir kamar garwa shi yanda soɓo ya zubar mata a cikin ido, ji Bahijja tayi an rike ta kamar a mafarki harshe Sarki Baidulƙais yasa yana lashe soɓon fuskar Bahijja danko Jakadiya ta tsorata dan karfin da Allah yaba Bahijja ta hankaɗe Sarki Baidulƙais da sauri ta mike tana murtsuka ido shiko shekeke yake kalan ta yana lashe harshe baida buri da ya wuce  lashi zumar Bahijja tun daga ranar da ya ɗaukota har zuwa yanzun ganin idan Jakadiya yasa Baidulƙais maida maitar shi yasa suka fita yana maida numfashi sai sake sake yake ta yanda zai cimma burin shi akan Bahijja......



Itako gimbiya Yazira koda ta koma sashen ta ba inda ta wuce sai ɗakin ta faɗawa tayi saman gado  ta fashe da malalacin kuka mai sosai zuciya ganin yanda mijin ta ke neman baiwa kamar Bahijja, koda uwar bayi ta ganta da sauri tayo kanta isketa tayi kwance tana kuka da sauri ta karasa tana tambayar ta damuwar ta, kamar jira tafara magana. "Haba Uwani ni Baidulƙais zai wulakanta ace da karamar yarinya zai so, baiwar tashi marar ƴanci yake nema dan haka uwani sama da shekara talatin kina taredani kuma kinsan surruka na ni nasaki ki takurama rayuwar Bahijja daga nan wajen aiki har taskar bayi kisa ta lalace ta wulakance har miyi nasarar raba mijina da ita dan zan iya kasheta!" 
"Ranki shidade duk abinda kike so shi nikeso dan haka kibar komai a hannuna da sannu saina wulakanta Bahijja daman batai minba bansanta tun ranar dana fara ganin ta naga tana takama sa kyaun ta kamar tafi ƴaƴan mu usuli saina ilata maki ita uwar ɗaki na." ajiyar zuciya gimbiya Yazira tayi dan tasan sheɗancin Uwani shiyasa ta kalleta cikeda gamsuwa





Itako Bahijja ba inda suka tsaya sai ɗakin Jakadiya sai garza kuka takeyi kamar ranta zaifita, dafata Jakadiya tayi tana mai tausan ta ganin karancin shekarun ta amman ana neman lalatata da kyal tasamu ta daina kukan da takeyi tana sauke ajiyar zuciya har baccin wahala yai awan gaba da ita..... 




Shiko Yarima Malik kisingiɗe yake ga tarin kayan marmari a gaban shi amman yakasa ci dan tunani daya gama addabar zuciyar shi ba abinda yake banda aikin tunanin Bahijja a cikin zuciyar shi, dan raban da yaci abinci harya manta tun jiya da daddare ko baccin kirki ya kasayi, sallamr da akayi shi ya katse shi a dogon tunanin da yatafi sakin murmushi yayi yana mai matsawa gefe inda Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta zauna kusa da ɗan nata, shiko dan danan ya marairaice yana kwantar da kansa a saman  kafaɗar ta yana gaidata cikeda shagwaɓa itako sai shafa sumar kanshi takeyi da tai kwance tai luf luf gwanin ban shi'awa saboda Yarima Malik ya gaji da haɗuwa cikin kulawa tace. " Malik meke damunka naganka ka saki ka rame kana taɓaman zuciya in inaganin ka cikin wannan halin"? ɗago kai yayi yana kallan mahaifiyar tashi yace. "Ummu kibar damuwa ba komai a raina kawai kaina ke saramani kuma nasha magani, yanzun so nike ni samu hutu shiyasa yau baki ganni nazo wajen kiba." badan ta yarda ba ta kyale shi ta lura baisan  yawan magana, cikin kulawa takama hannun ɗan nata ta zaunar dashi saman ƙayataccen gadon sa tace ya kwanta, ba musu ya kwanta dan baisan ta zauna a wajen shi yasan yanzun zata fara zagin Bahijja shi kuma baijin dadin hakan da takeyi akan iyayen Bahijja, yana kwantawa ta rufe shi da bargo ta tofa mashi addua sannan ta tafi yana jin so da shauƙin mahaifiyar sa saidai baisan  halayen ta, da hakaya dinga tunanin shi.... 



Shiko Huzaifa koda yaje wajen mahaifin shi ya sanar mashi da yanda sukayi da Yarima Malik sannan ya rokamashi alfarma. "Abba dan Allah kayi wani abu akai ka shawo kan Ummu ta bari muje mu nemo Bahijja dan rayuwar ta."


Numfasawa yayi yana gilgiza kai yace. "Bazaka ganeba ko kaɗan Sarauniya Zulaiƙa bazata bari ya kusanci Bahijja ba dan ko kaɗan bata son tsatson Jabir ko kuma ince Abdulmajid na fahimci Malik yana wasa da kalar kiyayyar da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa kema bayin Masarautar nan, dan haka ka kiyaye gaba nine wazirin Masarautar nan kuma nine mahaifinka Huzaifa kaida kanwar ka Zahra'u ku kaɗai na haifa dan haka bansan ka saka kanka cikin wannan cakwakiyar dake tareda Bahijja dan bata da kima a idon Sarauniya Zulaiƙa da ya wuce baiwa wulakan tatta abin yarwa." "Abba.....ɗaga mashi hannu yayi alamar baisan jin komai ya kara da cewa. "Ban faɗamaka wannan maganar ba dan ka tisa kaje ka gargaɗi Yarima akan ya fidda Bahijja a ranshi imma tausan ta yake yaci ace ya bari tunkan dare yai mashi dan haka kana iya tafiya!" mamaki ya gama kama Huzaifa bai tunanin jin haka daga bakin Mahaifin nashi ba, sallama yai mashi ya tafi jiki a sanyaye zuwa wajen Yarima Malik akai sa'a ya iske shi ya gama shiryawa yana fesa turare masu kamshi ganin abokin nashi yasa Malik ya karasa saman kujera ya zauna ya maida hankalin shi akan shi, yakai minti ukku kafin ya fara magana saida ya faɗa mashi komai wata uwar zufa ke ɗawainiya da Malik kamar bai wanka ba haka yake jinshi bakin shi na rawa ya kalli Huzaifa yace!!!. 


*Ƴar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*



Babi na 23&24




"Huzaifa ya akeson inyi da raina miyasa Bahijja batazo duniya a sa'a tun tana jinjira rayuwar ta ke garari daga mutuwa zuwa rayuwa har zuwa yanzun da ba'asan wane hali take ba, wlh Huzaifa matukar ina numfashi ban mutu bazan bar rayuwar Bahijja ta gayyara ba ta wulakan ta musamman ta bangaren addinin ta." shima Huzaifa shiru yayi dan yasa rashin taimaka rayuwar ta ba karamin asaraba ne kuma yasan haɗarin da take ciki, dubara ce ta faɗoma Hauzaifa yace. "Inhar kana son Bahijja ta dawo a Masarautar Daborah a karo na biyu ka kwantar da kanka wajen Ummu ka nuna mata kaima ka manta da rayuwar Bahijja daga baya sai mu zame ba wanda ya sani ku ɗauko ta, zuwa lokacin mungama samun makaman yaƙar Sarrilƙus kaga kai bakasamu saɓani wajen Mahaifiyar kaba."


kallan Huzaifa yake kamar mai nazari sai ya samu lokaci da gyaɗa kai alamar amincewa, tashi yayi zai shiga bathroom dan sake watsa ruwa ya waigo yacema Huzaifa. "Taya zanga Umman Bahijja."?  da mamaki Huzaifa ke kallan Malik ganin hakan a fuskar shi yasa Huzaifa ya karaso a gaban abokin nashi sannan yace. "Kadaina kudirin ganin Umman Bahijja baka tunanin kar Sarauniya ta ganka da ita bayan tasa baka da wani alaƙa da ita." koda zataimin aiki"? Cewar Malik, shiko Huzaifa murmushi yayi yace. "bayan bata jerin hadiman ka karka manta Sarauniya tasan bazaka iya saka Binta aiki ba kodan yanda kake daraja su, kaga tana ganin ka da Binta zatayi zargin wani abun daga karshe ka jefata cikin mawuyacin hali a cikin Daborah, kaga Yarima kabar tunanin haɗuwa da Binta a wannan lokacin da Sarauniya ta maida hankalin ta wajen ka!" wannan karon ko cikin fushi Malik yayi maganar harsaida Huzaifa yai mamaki. "Kaga bawai shawarar ka nike nema ba, umurtar ka nikeyi dan nasan abinda nikeyi nasan dalilin dayass nikesan ganin Umman Bahijja!"



Gilgiza kai Huzaifa yayi yace. "Allah ya huci zuciyar magajin gobe girman kane yanda kake so haka za'ayi, badan bamu da ikon shiga taskar bayi ba da na kirawo maka ita da kaina amman zansa baiwata ta kirata yanzun nan." baijara cewar Malik ba ya fita ranshi ɓace shima Malik ya fahimci Huzaifa yayi fushi, amman ya basar tareda shigewar sa wanka...... 


 _RAIFASU_ 



Itako Bahijja kara girma takeyi duk jikinta ya murɗe tazama cikakkar budurwa dan inbaka santa ba saikayi zatan takai shekara sha takwas a duniya wanda duka duka yazun ta shiga shekara sha huɗu da haihuwa yanzun takara kama kanta sosai kullin faɗan da Jakadiya ke mata kenan wadsa ta zama kamar uwa ga Bahijja dan wani bima har kwana takeyi wajen ta tunda ta fahimci yanayin halittar Bahijja bata bari tana wasanni irin na yara da takeyi da kawayen ta, kasancewar tafisu girna sosai da komai dan ko mace taga Bahijja sai taji ta burgeta balle ɗa namiji, yau din ma tana zaune wajen Jakadiya tanai ma Bahijja tsifa baki ɗaya gashin kanta ya rufe fuskar ta bata ganin ko gabanta, Jakadiya ke sharce mata gashi sai kuka takeyi dan zafi, da kyal ta iya ɗaurema Bahijja gashin suna gamawa ta ɗaura ɗan kwalli wanda kamar ma bata saka ba kasancewar kallabin baida girma kuma bazai iya rufe duka sumar kan Bahijja bayan Jakadiya batayi ba dan taga ta rufema Bahijja gashi duka ba amman bai rufu ba, da haka dai sukasha koko da kosai suka tafi wajen aiki da Bahijja bazata je wajen Sarki Baiulƙais ba Jakadiyabta takura mata sai taje a tare suka tafi har ƴan kwalla Bahijja keyi dan tafara tsoran shi a ranta, suna shiga shi kuma ya fito daga bedroom dinsa zuwa falo yayi tozali da kyakkyawar fuskar Bahijja mai haukata shi take shi'awarta ta addabe shi yaji numfashin shi na neman shiɗewa baki ɗayan su suka duƙa suka gaida Sarki amma ko kaɗan baiji gaisuwar da suke mashi sai kallan ta yakeyi, jin shirun yai yawa yasa Bahijjabta ɗago ta aza idanta a fuskar Baidulƙais taga yana aikin kallan tsoro yakara kamata hakan yasan Jakadiya ta mike tacema Bahijja taje yau wanki zataima gimbiya Yazira, da sauri Sarki Baidulƙais yace. "Kar akara bata aiki irin wannan ta shiga ta gyaraman ɗaki." tsoro yakara kama Jakadiya murya na rawa tace. "Ranka shidade Balki itake gyara maka ɗaki kuma yanzun tana ciki shi takeyi!" kallan dayai ma Jakadiya ysa ta kama kanta sannan yace. "Sallamar ta daga aikin Bahijja ne da kula da abinci na tundaga Madafa har zuwa cina kuma ba shawara nike baki ba Jakadiya!" yanda taga yai maganar yasa ta ce toh, nan ta kama hannun Bahijja zuwa ɗakin Baidulƙais sai kallan ɗakin Bahijja takeyi gani take kamar komai ya sauya bata fatar shiga cikin shi, aiki ta fara nan Jakadiya takara tsorata Bahijja karta bari ko yatsan ta yakama  inba haka ba zatayi ciki harda kwallan Bahijja duk ta tsure lokaci guda mahaifiyarta ta faɗo mata a zuciya kasancewar huɗubar ta kenan a gareta share kwallan tayi tana cigaba da aikin ta

"Kidaina faɗar haka Bahijja barin Masarautar nan tamkar rasa rayuwar kine dan Baidulƙais bazai raga maki ba da ace nasan kudurin shi akan ki kenan da ban bari a raboki daga Masarautar kiba da kuma iyayenki duk da kasancewar ki baiwa kamar yanda kike a halin yanzun!" kallan ta Bahijja tayi kamar mai tuna wani abu ta sake fashewa da kuka tace. "Baba kibari intafi wajen  Ya Malik nasan shi bazai cutar dani ba, nan kasheni zasuyi basu kyaunata ko kaɗan basu tausayi na."


Itadai Jakadiya bata kuma cewa komai ba sai aikin lallashin Bahijja da takeyi zatayi magana sukaji Balki tacema Jakadiya Sarki na nemanta ba yanda ta iya haka ta mike ta tafi wajen Sarki Baidulƙais kamar jira tana zama ko gaisuwa basuyi ba yafara tambayar ta. " miyasameta dagaske ta mutu!" yanda ya ke tambayr ta shine ya kara bama Jakadiya mamaki, amman aitasan kodan ya cimma mugun nufin shi akan Bahijja bazai so ta mutu azahiri tace. " bata mutu ba saidai tana cikin yanayi akalla saita kai sati biyu tana hutawa akafin ta dawo bakin aikin ta." cikin damuwa ya tambaye ta abinda ya sameta, shiru tayi tana tunanin kalar amsar da zata bashi, sai cewa tayi. "faɗuwa tayi ta fashe kai shine ta samu doguwar suma." Shiru tayi kamar mai nazari badan ya yardaba yace. " bakomai amman kibari sai taji sauki ta dawo aiki anjima zan aiko Sarkin gida ya kawo mata magani kije kibata kulawa ta musamman sai kinjini Gamji ya bata sauki."da amin ta amsa ta tafi tana aljabin Baidulƙais duk rashin tausan shi amman yana jin tausan Bahijja koda yake dan ya cimma burin shi, shiyasa yace ta huta da wannan tunanin ta tafi wajen Bahijja 




*Ƴar mutan Kankia ce❣️*
