*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*


Babi na 25&26

*Afuwan! Ina neman afuwa ga yan BAYI MA 'YA'YA'NE COMMENT GROUPS🙏, ku sani inajin dadin comment dinku sosai amman ina busy shiyasa bani baku reply ina jindadin shirhin da kuke sai da bani da lokacin da za'ayi firar dani, amman ku sani comment dinku da shirhinku ke sa nike two post a rana nagode ku cigaba da zuba comment din dai😍❣️*

Washe gari da safe Bahijja ce kwance saman cinyar Jakadiya tana bata labari itako sai dariya takeyi zuwa yanzun gani take kamar itace mahaifiyar ta itama haka Jakadiya ta ɗauka a cikin zuciyar ta, bakaramin kulawa take ba Bahijja ba tamkar ƴar da ta haifa da cikin ta, suna cikin firar su Zainab ta shigo ta duba Bahijja da jiki wanda izuwa yanzun ta samu sauki sosai, nan Jakadiya ta ɗan fita zagayawa Zainab ta matso kusa da Bahijja tace. "Bahijja ashe wai da Sarki aka kamaki shiyasa gimbiya Yazira taimaki dukan tsiya ke ko miyajamaki."

Jikin Bahijja ne yai mugun sanyi ta rasa wane munafuki ya faɗama Zainab sauran ta koma mujiya a cikin wannan azzalumar Masarautar. "Ke ina kikaji wannan labarin waya faɗamaki."? 
daburcewa Zainab tayi tana rawar murya tace. "Wajen uwar bayi naji tana faɗama Lawisa da su Balki suke maganar kinganni nan ba ruwana."

Hawaye masu zafi suka wanke ma Bahijja fuska tarasa mike mata dadi a rayuwar ta, murmushin karfin hali tayi batace komai ba, duk taji ta tsani rayuwar da ta sameta, koda Jakadiya ta dawo kallan Bahijja kawai tayi tagane tana cikin damuwa zama tayi ta fara tambayarta damuwar ta sai tace bakomai Baba kawai kaina naji yana ɗanyi mani ciwo kallan tuhuma taima Zainab dan bata yarda ba cikin kamun kai Zainab taima Bahijja sallama ta tafi tana godema Allah da Bahijja bata faɗama Jakadiya ba. 


Shiko Yarima Haishir tunda ya tashi yake yan kalle kalle ko zaiga kyakkyawar baiwa sunan da yabata kenan, a cikin zuciyar kasan cewar ainahin sunan ta ya ɓace mashi koda aka kawo masu kalaci baici komai ba zuciyar shi ta azazzale shi da san ganin kyakkyawar fuskar ta, mahaifinsa ketayi mashi magana amman baiji shiba hakan yasa ya dafa shi a firgice ya jiyo yana kallan shi, Sarki Lahazu ne ya zauna yana tambayar shi damuwar shi da yai niyar bazai faɗa mashi damuwar shi ba amman saiyaga kwara ya faɗa mashi. "Abba ina cikin damuwa marar misaltuwa, naga abinda raina keso kuma ina son inkasan ce dashi."


Da sauri ya tambayi ɗan nashi abinda yake so. " Abba ina wannan baiwar da tazo tabani ruwa harta kijamani jiki ina sonta kuma auren ta zanyi."


Wani irin shuumin murmushi yayi yana kalan ɗan nashi sannan yace. "Dalilin zuwan mu kenan naga yana boyeman wani sirri nashi ta wannan hanyar ne kadai zan gane daganan zan datse alakar mu dashi, dan haka kasama ranka kasamu kuma zan ƴanta maka ita da karfina sannan in aura maka ita, inasan ka natsu kadaina bin matan banza nikuma zan aura maka ita." dan dadi Yarima Haishir baisan sadda ya makale mahaifinsa ba yana murna a haka Sarki Baidulƙais ya iske su zaunawa yayi yana murmushi yace. "Ɗana wannan irin farin ciki na menene.?  Mahaifinsa yai murmushi yace. "Yau zakaji abinda ke damun ɗan naka nasan zakai mashi maganin damuwar shi." cikin gamsuwa yace. "Badamuwa indai dukiya ke badashi zan mashi."

Murmushi mugunta sukayi suna kallan juna suka kashe ido da Yarima Haishir sannan ya fita alamun maijin kunyar iyayen nashi da dariya suka bishi suna mamakin kalar halin ɗan nasu. 


Haka rayuwa takanse a cikin Masarautar Raifasu danko Bahijja ta rabu da zuwa wajen Sarki Baidulƙais hankalin ta kwance wani lokacin kuma gimbiya Yazira kesa a kirata ta saka ta aiki saidai duk wannan abun Sarki bai sani ba.


A Masarautar Daborah kuwa Malik ne tafe da hadiman shi sai koɗa shi sukeyi suna kirara shi ba inda suka dosa sai wani ƙayataccen lambu mai matukar daukar hankali da saka nutsuwa yana isa daman n gyara inda zai zauna sai kuɗa shi bawan shi Ashir yakeyi yana. "Zaman ka lfy Yariman Daborah, gaba lfy baya lfy alkhairyn Allah ya tabbata da aminci su lulubeka Yarima Allah yakareka daga murmushin makiya angaishe da Yarima Malikussaif ɗa ga Saifulislam magajin Sarauniya Zulaiƙa yar Abdulhamid Allah yakara girma."


Haka yaita koɗa Malik, shima zama yayi gefen uban gidan nashi, hakan yasa ya ɗagama sauran bayin nashi hannu alamar su bashi waje shida Ashir suka kebe sannn ya fara magana. "Ashir inasan insaka wani aiki amatsayin ka na amintaccen bawana."
Cikin zumuɗi Ashir yace. "Ranka shidade zaman da nike kenan aikin ka shine nasara agareni umurnin ka abun tunkaho a gareni ina sauraron ka magajin gobe." "inasan ka badda kamanni ka shiga Masarautar Raifasu ka tabbatar min idan Bahijja na cikin Masarautar kazo ka faɗamani saidai karka yarda ka faɗama wani inda zakaje sannan karka ce ni na aikeka kaidai kabi ta yanda zaka ɓadda kama ka ziyarci Masarautar Raifasu kome kenan ina sauraran ka."

Shiru Ashir yayi yana mamakin maganganun Malik ya ɗauka anrufe babin Bahijja ganin yanda Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta shure maganar ta amman shi Malik bai mantata ba gaskia ya cika ɗan halak bai mance halacci ba. "Shikenan ranka shidade, zuwa gobe zan shirya in shiga cikin Raifasu dan tabbatar maka da abinda kakeson gani dan gane da Bahijja saidai fatar mu musameta a can ɗin."?  . "kwarai kuwa Ashir fatana kenan." murmushi Ashir yayi yana cigaba daba mai gidan nashi kwarin guiwa sannan yaciga ba da bashi labarai masu dadin gaske basu suka baro lambu ba sai marece likis sannan suka wuce sashen Yarima Malik,yana zama a falo baiwar shi Aliya ta haɗa mashi ruwan wanka ya shiga bathroom shi kuma Ashir ya tsaya cikin falo yana kula da uban gidan shi.


A wajen Yarima Haishir kuwa kwana yayi yana tunanin Bahijja a cikin zuciyar shi ɗan ganin da yaimata yaji ta shiga ranshi fiye da tunanin shi a haka dai har gari ya waye wajen karfe goma ya tashi ya shirya ya fita fa'da, can ya iske mahaifin shi anata fadanci yana zuwa yakai gaisuwa wajen Sarki Baidulƙais sannan ya gaida mahaifin shi, kowa yayi shiru a fa'da  kamar ba kowa Sarki Baidulƙais ne yafara magana. "Inasan Sarkin fa'da da Sarkin taska ku zaun aku duba nawa fa'da zata bukata da kuma wannn Masarautar musamman wajen kayan masarufi na yau da gobe."  da sauri Sarkin zagi yafara cewa. "Ranka shidade, gyara ginzi toran giwa gaba lfy baya lfy." Sarki Lahazu yace. "Kamar yanda kace hakan yabasa kyau yakamata ace kunduba komai kafin shekara takare tunda za'aci kasuwar Qazub sai kuyi duk abinda yakamata." 
Cikin gamsuwa Sarki Baidulƙais ya amince da shawarar da abokin nashi yabashi,haka sukaci gaba da ƴan shawarwari daga karshe Sarki Baidulƙais ya tashi suka tafi sashen shi,a saman hanyar komawa sashen Sarki Baidulƙais, su hango Bahijja ta taho kanta akasa tana wasa da duwatsun dake hannun ta hankalin ta kwance, shiko Yarima Haishir koda ya ganta ya kafeta da shanyayyun idanun sa masu ruɗawar wa, magana Sarki Baidulƙais kemashi kojin shi baiba saida Sarki Lahazu ya taɓo Yarima Haishir sannan yadawo daga dogon tunanin da ya tafi, bin inda yake kallo yayi saiyaga Bahijja ta taho tanata yan wasannin ta, ji tayi ta bangaji mutun ta tafi zat faɗi da sauri Haishir ya rikota tafaɗo jikin shi duk tsoro yagama kamata kirjinta yafara dukan tara tara, shiko Baidulƙais daurewa kawai yayi gudun kada girman shi ya zube a gane akwai wani abu aranshi dangane da Bahijja yasa ya daure yana maijin haushin Haishir a ranshi, da sauri Bahijja ta kwace hannun ta da Hashir ya rike, ta sauke kanta kasa dan bata san suna haɗa ido, dukawa tayi tagaida Sarki Baidulƙais  da Lahazu sannan ta tafi jiki a sage duk ta tsargu, shiko Haishir da kallo ya bita, jiyake kamar ya bita, shima bin mahaifin shi yayi suka tafi yana ta tunanin Bahijja a ranshi, itako cikin taskar bayi ta shiga ba inda ta tsaya sai wajen Indo isketa tayi tana wanki suna gaisuwa da umman ta tadawo suna labari da kawar ta rabi duk labarin Yarima Malik take bata danma duk ta manta wasu abubuwan da suka faru, itako tana aiki tana taya Bahijja labaran da take mata har ta gama aikin ta suka wuce wajen Zainab suka dasa sabuwar fira, ji sukayi ana buga karaurawa da sauri suka tashi suka fita waje turus sukayi ganin bayi masu yawa anbaza su cikin rana ana dukan su, ga Bahijja da tsoran bugu dan danan idanun ta suka ciko da kwalla tafara ja dabaya, Sarkin dogarai ne ya daka mata tsawa batasan ta zube ba tana bashi hakuri itama cikin sahu ta shiga a kaci gaba da gana masu ukuba.

Banda bugun su ba abinda akeyi, Sarkin bulala yakama Zainab yafara duka sai ihu takeyi baidaina dukan taba saida ya sauke mata bulala talatin koda Bahijja taga yanda akaima kawarta take ido ya raina fata dan tsoro har saida ta fara sakin fitsari a wando, shiko baki ɗaya ranshi ya gama  ganin haka yasa ya ɗauko katuwar bulalar shi zai shauɗama Bahijja ta rike shi kam da karfi ta ɗare saman shi tana kuka kamar ranta zai fita ba yanda baiba dan ya yakice ta daga jikin shi amman ya kasa sai garza uban ihu da kuka takeyi, ganin taki sakin shi yasa ya banbaro hannun ta daga rikon da taima shi ya fara tsula mata ita a jiki sake fasa kara tayi mai ruɗarwa, hakan yasa Yarima Haishir zai tafi wajen Sarki Baidulƙais ya hango Sarkin bulala nata dukan wata yarinya tana sakin ihu da sauri ya iso wajen su ganin yanda Bahijja keta zille zille tana kuka hakan yasa ya dukama Sarkin bulala tsawa rai ɓace yace. "Sakarta baka da hankali ne zakaimata wannan uban dukan baka ganin yarinya ce."? 


Zubewa kasa Sarkin bulala yayi yana neman afuwa. "Ranka shidade tuba nike kaiman afuwa umurni daga Gimbiya Yasira." rai ɓace Yarima Haishir yace. "Daman tace ka duketa ne ko kuwa ita kadai ka raina da zakai mata irin wannan ukubar har haka."? "Aaa ranka shidade aiki za'a sakasu a taskar bayi zasu gyara wajen Gamji tun da safe basu fara aikin ba har yanzun shiyasa aka dakesu."....."yimin shiru wannan sakarci muddin baka duka kanemi afuwar taba ni kadai nasan abinda zan maka." jiki na rawa ya duka har kasa yana neman afuwar Bahijja, itako daman duk ta ruɗe dan taji dukan da akai mata duk da bada yawa aka bugeta ba, batasan sadda ta tace ta yafe mashi ba, hakan yasa Yarima Haishir yace su tashi su tafi haka kowa ya watse, itako Bahijja banda garzar kuka ba abinda takeyi ga sheshekar kuka tanayi Yarima Haishir yace ta tashi ya fara bata hakuri, godiya taimashi haka su Zainab suka kamata suka koma cikin taskar bayi.

Suna shiga cikin taskar bayi ɗakin su suka nufa da ita, sai shashsheka takeyi alamun taci kuka tagaji da godewa Allah, Zainab ce ta dafa kafaɗar Bahijja tace. "Haba kawata kibar wannan kukan inaga ke akaima wannan dukan ai kilan mutuwa zakiyi ko, dole ki ɗauki dangana inda har kikazo a baiwa ba yanda zaki iya." share kwallan da suka taho mata tayi ta kalle Zainab tace. "Zainab kenan da ace kinji irin kuncin da nikeji a zuciya ta dabaki faɗi hakaba, tabbas ina cikin tashin hankali anrabani da Masarautar mu da iyayena da masu kaunata nan inma ina cikin tashin hankali marar misaltuwa da zaki buɗa kirjina tabbas da kinsha mamaki yanda zuciya ke dokuwa kamar ta fito." cikeda tausayin bahijja Indo tace. "Keko wane hali kike ciki har haka."? 


"Hmmm kunga zan kwanta jikina baimin dadi anjima zanje inma gimbiya Yazira wanki kar in makara." cikin rashin yarda Indo tace. "Kawai bakisan kifaɗamana damuwar ki kilan baki daukemu yanda muka ɗauke kiba." murmushin karfin hali Bahijja tayi tace. "Ba haka bane kawai dai nagajine kuma ba wani hali bane irin yanda ake gallazamana azaba duk da karancin shekarun mu, dan haka karku damu ba komai."

Zainab ce tai saurin cewa. "Eh kam dole ashiga cikin hali fata dai gamji ya fidda mu a halin nan." ganin Indo zata kara wata maganar yasa Zainab ta riko hannun ta suka mike tareda yima Bahijja bankwana suma suka shiga gidanjen su, cike da rashin jindadin abinda Zainab tai mata suna fita Indo tace. "Haba Zainab ya zaki fiddoni bayan nasan ba abinda Bahijja kesan faɗa bane kawai dai ta boyemana damuwar tane." "Hmmm hakane sai dai zakiji mutuwar Sarki a bakin Zainabu ba kije ki tambayi Umman ki, kilan tasan abinda Bahijja ke boyewa kinga shiga ta gida indan huta kafin mufara aikin bautar kamar jakuna." bata jira cewar Indo ba ta faɗa dan karamin gidan su mai daki biyu, wanda kusan haka gidajen bayin suke kufai kufai wani gidan ma mutun biyu ke ciki har ukku, dayaki taskar bayi tana da girma dan dai bata kai turakar bayin Daborah girma ba. 

Washe gari da marece Sarki Baidulkais ne da Sarki Lahazu zaune a saman wata kilisa ta alfarma tasha kayan marmari jere suna fira a cikin lambu gwanin dadi sai Sarki Lahazu ya kalli aminin nashi yace. "Abokina daman insan insanar dakai wata magana." cikin kulawa ya kalli Sarki Lahazu yace. "Inajinka menene." "daman akan maganar da nace zamuyine shekaranjiya ɗanka Haishir yaga yarinya yanaso kuma yana kaunarta da zummar aure shine nace bari infada maka amatsayin ka na uba." duk da yasamu faɗuwar gaba amman sai ya samu kanshi da murmusawa yace. "Kai gaskia naji dadi gamji ya dafa a ina yaga yarinyar."? 


Numfasawa Sarki Lahazu yayi yana cewa. "A cikin Masarautar ka yaganta kuma na fahimci yayi nisa akan son ta gaskia." ɗan shiru Baidulkais yayi yana nazari shima Lahazun shi yake kallo yana nazartar wani abu ga abokin nashi kafin yace. "A Masarauta ta.? Wacece wannan."? dan ɗaga kafaɗa Sarki Lahazu yayi yace. "Wata baiwa ce....baijira ya ida maganar shiba yace. "Baiwa fa kace kamar Yarima Haishir inashi ina auren baiwa."?  kallan raini Lahazu yaima Baidulkais sannan yace. "Ai wannan baiwar ko aya tazo ya cancanta asota kodan kyawunta usulin ta da ɗabiarta kaga ba wani abu bane idan aka ƴan tata Shikenan kaga ya samu mata uwa uba takoma Masarauta ta sai naso sannan za'asan ba cikakkiyar ƴa bace baiwa ce ya kaga wannan salon abokina."? 

Shiru yayi yanda yaga Lahazu yana yaba kyawun surar baiwar ba abinda ke idanun shi ke hakaito mashi sai surar Bahijja da kyaunta dan danan yaji kirjin shi na dukan tara tara dakewa yayi yace. "Hakane saidai ya faɗa maka sunan baiwar kuwa."?  da eh ya amsa ma Sarki Baidulkais a kagare yace. "Wacece ita."?  "Bahijja sunan ta wata yarinya bazata wuce shekara sha  biyu ba a duniya." da sauri Baidulƙais ya mike yanajin wata irin juwa na dibar shi dakewa yayi bakin shi na rawa yace. "Bahijja baiwa ce ta amana inama ba itace Yarima keso ba danaba..... "Kaga abokina menene da kabamu baiwar amana aimu ƴan tata zamuyi kuma ta auri yariman Waisu dan haka saidai in akwai wata kwantacciya a kasa ban saniba." murmushi yayi mai ban takaici yace. "Hmmm bazaka ganeba Lahazu."
"Inba komai kabawa ɗana Bahijja yana sonta."
"Yanzun dai kaga yarinya bata sani ba kuma kaga..."kaga baiwa harwani ra'ayine da ita anya nakasa gane manufar ka akan Bahijjan nan da kake ta kumfar baki akanta." baisan sadda yace. "Sonta nike." baki ɗayan su suka kalli juna danko Lahazu tufa ke hannun shi baisan ya saketa ba, da sauri baidulƙais ya dawo hayyacin shi yace. "Ina nufin matsayin baiwa ta hadimata ban shirin rabuwa da Bahijja a halin yanzun kodan kankacin shekarun ta amman..."Hmmm kasanni nasanka kasan abinda zan'iya haka kaima kardai ace ba abinda nike zato bane ke tsakanin ka da ƴar cikin ka Bahijja inko har zargin da nikeyi ba haka bane to kafansar ma ɗana baiwar ka Bahijja ko nawa ne ni Lahazu zan biya." cikin fushi baidulƙais yace. "Karka faɗamun magana abinda kake zato sam ba haka bane, Bahijja sunan ba ni nahaifeta dan haka wannan itace maganata ta karshe kabar zancen Bahijja zanyi tunani akai, komai kenan zakaji." "Amman yaza kace haka inaganin....ɗaga mashi hannu Badulƙais yayi zuciyar shi na dukan tara tara jiyake kamar ya shake Lahazu musamman da ya fahimci yana zargin sa akan neman Bahijja kamar yanda haka yake a zahiri sai dai shima baiji zai iya sadaukar ma Haishir Bahijja yana sonta kuma ya ɗauki aniyar sai ya ɗandani zumarta mai tsannin zaƙi. 


Murmushin gefen baki lahazu yayi sannna yace."Shikenan yanda kace ina sauraran ka, kuma zan sanar ma Haishir halin da ake ciki sannan kasani kamar yanda kace haka ina jiran tsammani dan haka zuwa gobe zamu koma gida dan jiran sakamako, nabarka lfy." yana ida faɗar haka yai tafiyar shi yana fidda iska mai zafin gaske, shima zama yayi rigijib jiki sake yana jin wasu kwalla masu zafin sun taho mashi a kirjin shi na duka a haka yayi shiru yana saƙa abubuwa kala kala a ranshi.


Wajen karfe shidda Bahijja ce ta fito sashen gimbiya Yazira  rike da wasu kaya a hannun ta sai sauri takeyi ta tafi sashen Jakadiya, tana shiga wata hanya taji anrike mata hannu, wani irin faɗuwar gaba taji ya kamata, zatayi ihu taji an doɗe mata baki, baki ɗaya idanun suka fito kamar zasu faɗi kasa idanu kawai ta zuba mashi tana kallan shi, wani irin kwarjinin ta yaji yakamashi baisan sadda ya cire hannun shi daga bakin taba, yana so ya faɗa mata son da yake mata amman ya kasa da sauri ta anshe hannun ta, jikin ta na rawa tace. "Ranka shidade barka da marece, damn Jakadiya ta aikeni zan wuce." kamar wanka gele ya bata hanya ta wuce duk jikin shi yayi sanyi yana jin wani abu gami da Bahijja, itako sai sauri takeyi tanajin kafar ta na harde mata harta shige gidan Jakadiya, shiko inda tabarshi yakasa ko motsi da kyal yaja kafa ya tafi, masaukin su, yana shiga ya iske mahaifin shi yaje ya dawo ya kasa ko zama tambayar shi damuwar shi ya fara bai iya cema shi komai sai yace. "Kashirya gobe zamu koma gida." wani mummunan faduwar gaba yaji yace. "Abba taya zan tafi ba Bahijja."? 
"Karabu da ita." dukawa yayi yace. "Na rantse da Gamji zan iya mutuwa inba Bahijja." kallan sakarai yaimashi cikin kosawa yace. "Haka nace karabu da ita ai munci ribar zuwan da mukayi nasamu abinda nazo nema, dan haka gobe zamu tafi mubashi lokaci yayi tunani akan Bahijja kafin mu ɗauki matakin da yadace akan shi kaga iyakar abinda nace kenan bansan musu." badan yaji hankalin shi ya kwanta ba yabar maganar ya shiga daki sai tunanin Bahijja yakeyi a cikin zuciyar shi.

Masarautar Daborah, Yarima Malik ne kwance baisan bacci yai awan gaba dashi yana cikin bacci ya fara mafarkin waiga wata yarinya nan tana ta gudu a cikin daji wasu mutane sun biyota da gudu ta boye bayan wani katan icce ta leken mutanen da suka biyota, sai taji ankamata ance mun kamata dan haka yau sai mun kasheta suna ta dariya itako sai kuka takeyi tana wul kafafu tana cewa Ya Malik kataimakeni zasu kasheni......ji yayi ya tashi kamar an kwara mashi ruwa dan zufa yana son ya tuna abinda ya faru amman ya kasa sai muryar yarinyar yakeji akunnen shi tana cewa ya taimake ta baki daya kanshi ya kulle, daren ranar Malik bai koma bacci ba sai yafara nafilfili har asuba saida ya dawo sallar asuba sannan ya kwanta bacci wahalalle ya ɗauke shi, bai tashi ba sai goma na safe lokacin har Ashir ya gama shiri yana jiran uban gidan nashi ya fito ba Malik ya fitoba sai wajen sha ɗaya cikin shigar alfarma ya fito na milk din kaya da ratsin kofi riga da wondo na ƴan india ne a jikin shi sai hula, koda ya fito falo gaishe shi Ashir yayi kallan da Malik yaimai ya tabbatar da yagama shirin tafiya Masarautar Raifasu, zaman yayi saman wata kujera ta alfarma sannan ya amsa da. "Lfy, yanzun zaka tafi kenan, anshi wannan kayi guzuri Allah ya tsare hanya.cikin jin dadi Ashir ya amsa yana godiya sannan yaima Malik sallama ya tafi, bai jima da fita baiwar shi Aliya ta shigo tafara gabatar mashi da kayan karin kumallo sannan ta shiga tafara gyara mashi daki, yafara kalaci kenan yaji ana sanar da shigowar Saifulislam yana shigowa Malik ya mike yana rungume mahaifin shi sannan suka zauna atare suka yi karin kumallo sannan suka cigaba da fira ta ɗa da mahaifi yana tambayar dan nashi damuwar shi.


A Masarautar Raifasu kuwa Sarki Lahazu da Yarima Haishir har sun gama shirin su tsaf su da bayin su, ba dan Sarki Baidlƙais yaso ba yaje sukayi ban kwana duk yanda Yarima Haishir yaso ya sake ganin kyakkyawar fuskar Bahijja  ta gagare badan yaso ba suka tafi zuciyar shi cike take da kaunar Bahijja har aka buɗe masu gate suka kama hanyar tafiya zuwa Masarautar Waisu.




Sarki Baidlƙais ne zaune a sashen shi yakasa zama yaje ya dawo har Jakadiya tazo baisan da zuwan ta saida ta sake gaidashi karo na huɗu sannan ya kalleta a firgice ko amsa gaisuwar ta baiba yace. "Ina Bahijja turomani ita yanzun Jakadiya." tana san tayi magana tana shakka, haka ta tafi sashen gimbiya Yazira ta isketa tana wanki kamar kullin faɗa mata sakon Sarki Baidlƙais tayi dai dai nan uwar bayi Uwani ta fito da sauri ta koma ta gayama gimbiya Yazira kiran da Sarki kema Bahijja, batasan ta iso wajen su Jakadiya ba tace aiki tasa Bahijja, dukawa sukayi ta gayamata kiran da Sarki kema Bahijja, cikin izzah tace. "Shikenan ke kuma nabaki minti biyar kije ki dawo." da sauri Bahijja ta amsa da toh, itako gimbiya Yazira tana komawa daki alqabba ta saka tana dakon zuwa sashen mijin nata dan bata yarda da kiran da yaima Bahijja ba, itako Bahijja jiki a mace ta tafi Jakadiya na kwantar mata da hankali ta tafi, a falo ta same shi ya kafeta da mayun idanun shi itako sai wasa take da hannun ta harta shigo ta gaida shi ta duka kasa ta raɓe kamar wata mage, kallan ta yayi yace..... 




 *Ƴar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*



Babi na 27&28




"Miyahanaki zuwa wajen aiki ko kina nufin kindaina yima uban gidan ki hidima!"? Yanda Bahijja taga yai maganar ba alamun wasa a fuskar shi shiyasa Bahijja takara shiga taitayin ta, kanta a kasa tace. "Tuba nike ranka shidade ina aikin uwar ɗakina ne gimbiya Yazi....  ba tareda ta karasa sunan ba ya daka mata uwar tsawa ƴan hanjin ta ne suka juya dan tsoro yace. "Daga yau baki da aikin kowa ko bautama kowa da sunan wahala saini daga ban amince ki kara aikin wani ko wata ba daya wuce nawa ni Baidlƙais inba hakaba kiyi kuka da kanki dan saina maki hukunci mai tsauri kinajina!"?  Da sauri tace. "Eh ranka shidade naji kaimin afuwa basan sake ba." basuyi aune b sukaji ana sanar da zuwan  gimbiya Yazira tana zama gefen mijin ta tagaida shi, a dakile ta amsa kallam Bahijja tayi tace. "Ke tashi kibamu waje!" a hankali Bahijja ta tashi zata tafi jitayi yace. "Bahijja." juyowa tayi a sanyaye ta taho baijira ta karaso ba yace. "Je kigyaraman daki na." 


Wani mugun kallo gimbiya Yazira ta aika mata sannan tace. "Ranka shidade nasaka ta aiki kuma ai Bahijja tayi ƙankanta da gyaran ɗaki." kallan da bankaɗa mata ne yasa ta haɗiye sauran kalaman ra sannan yace. "Ban umurce ta datayima kowa aiki ba da ya wuce nawa dan haka daga yau na haramta mata yima kowa aiki a cikin Masarautar Raifasu sai nawa kaɗai kuma ba ruwanki da girman ta ko ƙanƙan tarta umurni nane da fatan kinfahimta."? 



A sanyaye gimbiya Yazira ta ɗaga mashi kai itadai Jakadiya tana jin duk abinda ke faruwa nata ido duk da tasan ba karamin musifa yake jefa Bahijja ba duk da mugun kudurin shi akan ta...... 




A turakar bayi kuwa Binta ce dasu ladidi keta share share kamar ba gobe duk da kasancewar Binta uwar bayin Daborah hakan bai hanata aiki ba kuma ba ruwan ta da zalintar kananan bayi acewar ta dui daya suke ba wanda yafi wani a cikin su, suna cikin aikine duk sun gaji suka zauna gefen wata bishiya Ladidi ce ta kalli Binta tace uwar bayi ya kamata ace izuwa yanzun munsan halin da ƴarmu Bahijja ke ciki tunkan ta manta da suwaye iyayen ta kinga akalla ta ɗauki lokaci mai tsayin gaske a Masarautar Raifasu."



"Hmm Ladidi kenan kina bani mamaki yanzun mu harmuna da wani gata ko galihu da zamu iya dawowa da Bahijja a hannun mu aini inaji araina kilan muda Bahijja sai darussalam na fiddata araina saidai munaima Bahijja fatan alkhairy akowane hali take munai mata fatan nasara Allah ya jibinci lamarin ta." " Ameen saidai duk da haka bansan kina fadar haka kisa aranki ƴarki zata dawo gareki." "Hmmm Ladidi kenan Allah yasa, ko yanzun ina tsoron abinda kaje yadawo shiyasa bansan ana zancen nan." "kinjiki taya za'a hanaki maganar ƴarki, kuma shi Yarima Malik baice komai ba bayan akan shi kuka rasa yarku." mikewa Binta tayi tana takaicin kalaman Ladidi, tace. "Mubar maganar nan Ladidi Yarima Malikussaif yaimana komai tunda yana tausan halin da ƴarmu ke ciki." tayaya cewar Mairo wadda tunda aka fara maganar takasa cewa komai dan mamaki halin Binta taga batasan laifin Yarima Malik ko kadan. "Hakane duk da hakan bazai anfanemu ba saidai Yarima ya tambayeni inda Bahijja take kuma nasanar dashi abinda nasani, saidai tsoro na ɗaya hukuncin da Saraunya Zulaiƙa zata ɗauka akaina kasancewar ta kama mu muna maganar dan Allah kusama zuciyar ku hakuri muyima Bahijja addu'a kowane hali ta tsinci kanta ta ɗauki dangana da hakuri kunji inda na tsaya a lamarin Bahijja, saidai kusani inasan ƴata tilo a duniyar nan da muke ciki saidai ba yanda muka iya kaddara ta riga fata!." ta karashe maganar tana share kwalla a idan ta suma gogewa sukayi kowa ya kasa cewa uffan basuyi aune ba sukaji an rufesu da duka haka suka mike sunaci gaba da aiki basu jimaba da cigaba da aiki ba saiga wata baiwa wadda ake cema Hamziya tace ma Binta Saraunya Zulaiƙa nasan ganin take taji jikin ta yai sanyi suma su Mairo duk sun shiga ruɗani haka ta tafi tana mai addua a cikin zuciyar ta batasan me zata iske ba a yau kuma......




tana isa wajen Saraunya Zulaiƙa ta isketa ta ɗaura kafa ɗaya kan ɗaya tana shakar kamshi duk hadimai masu mata fiffita suna tayi masu tausa nayi haka Binta ta isa wajen Saraunya Zulaiƙa takai gaisuwa shiru ne ya biyo baya na dakikai sannan taimata kallan kaskanci tace. "Sai yanzun kikaga damar zuwa kiran nawa."? 


Jitayi jikinta ya tsinke ta sake kallan Saraunya murya na rawa tace. "Allah yataimaki uwar marayu yanzun kiranki ya riskeni ko sakan ban karaba na taho amsa kiranki saidai wanda nayi a hanya kimin aikin gafara Binta na tuba.!" 
A watse ta tsirta yawu akan fuskar Binta sannan tace. "Basai na fada maki abinda zakiyi ba amman bansan ko kanki na rabawa ba!"  godiya Binta tayi ta mike tafara daddan nama Saraunya Zulaiƙa takafaɗa a hankali, shiru ne ya biyo baya, itako Binta ganin yanda Saraunya Zulaiƙa ta ƙaskantata yasata tafara kwalla wanda batasan tanayin suba sannan tasa hannu tana share miyan da Saraunya Zulaiƙa ta watsa mata a kan fuska, jin da tayi Binta ta daina yimata tausa na dakikai yasa dakama Binta tsawa tace. "Baki da hankali insaki aiki ki tsaya kina haukane kinkosan agaban wa kike!"?  Baiwar Allah Binta batasan sadda ta duka kasaba tana neman afuwa, haka Saraunya Zulaiƙa ta ɗauke Binta da maruka har ukku dai dai nan Saifulislam ya shigo ta tsaya ko motsi ya kasayi itako Binta rike kuncin ta tayi tana maiba uwar dakin tata hakuri duk ta ruɗe dan ta maru wanda harta fara sabawa da azabar Saraunya Zulaiƙa akanta tace. "Uwar gijiyata kiyi hakuri Binta nagodiya ina tuba Allah ya huci zuciyar Saraunyar Daborah girman kine!." buɗe baki Saraunya Zulaiƙa tayi zatayi magana kenan taga Saifulislam wanda mamaki ya gama kamashi hakan yasa tace. "Ɓace mani da gani shasha sha kuma gobe kizo aiki da wuri kinajina." "angama ranki shidade uwar marayu mahaifiya ga adalin Yariman Daborah Allah yakara girma nabarki lfy." haka Binta ta tafi zata wuce ta duka harkasa tagaida Saifulislam, cikeda sa tausayin ta ya amsa sannan ta tafi shi kuma ya karaso cikin falon Saraunya Zulaiƙa, sauran bayin na ganin haka kowa ya bar falon daga shi sai ita ya rage, itako Saraunya Zulaiƙa tana ganin ya zauna ta zauna gefen shi tanai mashi barka da shigowa a takaice ya amsa take taga sauyi akan fuskar mijin nata cikin kulawa tace. "Abu Malik miyasame ka bansaba ganin wannan fuskar taka mai cike da haiba a cikin ɓacin raiba ko fushi lfy ka faɗaman damuwar ka."?  Da bainiyar cewa komai ba sai kuma yace. "Bakomai ina murna zanzo ingana da farin cikina sai kuma nazo naga saɓanin hakan kardai ace har yanzun baki wofintar da al'adar kaka da kakanni akan wa'annan bayin ba."? 
Shiru tayi daman tasan sai ya tanka itadai ta kasa gane halin Saifulislam shidai komai tausayi shiyasa take yima Malik uzuri, amman a zahiri sai tace. "Aaa bahaka bane narasa gane mugun halin Binta taya zata... "Kinga Ummu Malik basai kince komai ba nasan wacece Binta kema kinsan hakan, saidai kash bai kamata muzama masu butulci akan iyalan Abdulmajid ba duka da yanda suka sadaukar da ƴarsu ɗaya tilo akan ɗanmu ɗaya tilo Malik duk da itama ƴace kamar shi amman hakan baisa sun sota ba sai sukaso namu ɗan, hakan bai nuna maki darajar suba da kimaba agaremu."? "Haba Abu Malik kadai danganta wannan baiwar yarinyar Bahijja da ɗana Malik jinin sarauta kaka da kakanni karka manta itafa baiwa ce yar gado akan bauta da bayi haba mijina baiwar!"?  Cikeda takaici ya kalli Saraunya Zulaiƙa yace. "Hakane Bahijja baiwa ce saidai kisani daga malicci ɗaya suke kilama tafi Malik daraja sannan kisaka a ranki kamar kullin kuma ba zan fasa faɗamaki ba Bayima Ƴaƴane kamar kowa kuma suma suna tasu darajar ki gyara shawara nike baki a matsayin ki na mata agareni mafi soyuwa a cikin zuciya ta.!"


Shiru tayi tana karajin tsanar Binta da ƴarta nakara ruruwa a cikin zuciyar ta kamar kara hura wutar ta akeyi zatayi magana sukaji shigowar Malik wanda tunda suka fara maganar su Bahijja yanajin su ganin kar maganar tayi tsayi asamu saɓani tsakanin iyayen nashi wanda bai fatar hakan yasa shi saurin shigowa gaidasu yayi cikin girmamawa, haka mahaifin nashi ya amsa yana mai jin dadin ganin ɗan nashi itako Saraunya tana hake dashi a ciki ciki ta amsa wanda hakan yasa Malik baiji dadi ba basu jimaba suna fira d mahaifin nashi wanda Saraunya ta zama ƴar kallo hakan yasa Saifulislam yaimasu sallama yatafi shi kuma Malik ya koma wajen mahaifiyar shi ya yafara fadanci yana bata hakuri da tausassan lafazi da takiyin magana jin yanda ya dage yana koɗata sai kace ba Malik dinta yasa ta sakkowa daga fushin da takeyi dashi tace.....




"Haba Malik yakakeso inyi taya zan zuba ido kana magajina amman kana sakin jikin ka da kaskantattun mutane kabani mamaki abinda naji kuna cewa da Binta akan wannan banzar baiwar Bahijja." duk baijin dadin yanda mahaifiyar shi ke wulakanta mutane amman sai ya boye damuwar shi yace. "Kiyi hakuri Ummu kema kinsan ina tareda ke, dan haka indai akan Bahijja ne kisa a ranki namanta da ita bankara zancen ta farin cikin ki shinawa Ummu na!" yakarasa maganar shi yana kwantawa a saman cinyar ta itama shafa sumar kanshi takeyi wadda tai kwance tayi luf da ita, tace. "Kaji dan halak kagama wankemani zuciya ta Allah yaimaka albarka."
"Ameen Ummu na." cewa tayi. "Yanzun damuwa ta ɗaya shine yanda mahaifinka ya fita yana fushi dani akan wannan lalatattar baiwar Binta." da sauri Malik ya tashi zaune yana mai boye damuwar shi ga mahaifiyar shi yace. "Haba Ummu kidaina damuwar kanki akan Binta itafa baiwar kice kibarta da abinda kr damuntama na bauta, amman ki kwantar da hankalin yau zanje gun Abbuh zan bashi hakuri komai zai wuce." cikin jindadi tace. "Yauwa masoyina abin alfaharina Allah yatsareman kai farin cikina." da amin ya amsa a ranshi yana misalta son zuciya irin na mahaifiyar shi tana addu'a Allah ya tsare shi batasan taga ya cutu, amman ga ƴar wasu can na cutuwa ko ajikin ta dayake ita baiwa ce, da haka yaimata sallama ya tafi sashen shi, itako tana da kwarin guiwar cewa Saifulislam zaidaina fushi da ita dan tasan ba abinda Malik zai nema wajen mahaifin sa ya rasa da haka ta shiga daki dan hutawa. 

Itako Bahijja lamarin Masarautar Raifasu yakara ƙamari a gareta kullin cikin tsangwama take a wajen gimbiya Yazira ba dare ba rana hakama idan ta dawo taskar bayi ba abinda ya sauya cikin tashin hankali take kwana dan Uwani uwar bayi kullun dare sai ta shiɗema Bahijja bulala talatin haka take kwanciya jikin ta a fashe wani bima bacci gagararta yakeyi kuma gari na waye haka zata tashi ko karin kumallo batayi zata tafi sashen Sarki  Baidulƙais kamar yanda ya umurceta ta gyara mashi ɗakin shi sannan ta hada mashi ruwan wanka, haka zata koma falo ta takure har sai ya fito ta cikaga ba da yimashi dannar kafafu, idan Jakadiya ta shigo kawo mashi abinci Bahijja ce zata bashi abinci, kullin a takure take duk da yanzun yadaina yunkurin ta ɓamata jiki koda kuwa a ɗaki ya isketa tanai mashi aiki saidai yaita kallan ta hakan kesa Bahijja na shuga damuwa duk ta rude, dan bala'in tsoron Baidulƙais takeyi a rayuwar ta.... 



Watarana da marece Bahijja ce zaune a cikin taskar bayi tana wanke ƴan kayan ta kafin gobe ta tafi aikin ta kamar yanda ta saba, tana cikin wankin ta, batasan hawa ba batasan sauka ba taji anzabga mata bulala a tsakiyar baya, a zabure ta mike tace. "Inalillahi wa'inna ilaihir raji'un." tana juyowa taga uwar bayi tsaye tana mata kallan banza sake zabgama Bahijja bulala tayi aiko ta kwalla uwar kara cikin kankanin lokaci duk bayin dake kusa dasu Bahijja suka fito itako Bahijja duk ta gama ruɗewa dan bakaɗan takejin dukan n ratsa taba har kwakwalwar ta dan a zaba, koda Bahijja ta fahimci da gayya Uwani ke dukan ta batasan ta mikeba tafara zabga uban gudu batasan ta fita taskar bayi ba, aiko nan Uwani tasa wasu bayi su kamo mata Bahijja itama bin Bahijjar tayi, itako Bahijja batada burin da ya wuce taga ta isa wajen Jakadiya tasan ita kadai ce mai taimakon ta tana cikin gudu taji ta bangaji mutun tai bayaya ta fadi kasa a ruɗe ta sake mikewa tana mikewa taga gimbiya Yazira tsaye ta rike kugu tanai mata kallan tuhuma, cikin Bahijja ne ya kaɗa take yawun bakin ta ya ɗauke batasan ta fara kuka ba tafara ba gimbiya Yazira hakuri, saiga Uwani ta isa sai haki takeyi koda taga Bahijja tsugun ne agaban gimbiya Yazira tasan kwanan zancen tabbas yau kashin Bahijja ya bushe duk da batasan me taima gimbiya Yazira ba, a gadaran ce gimbiya Yazira tace. "Ke dan ubanki wace kalar mayyar yarinya ce ni zaki banka ki gogaman kashin tsiya!" take Uwani tace "yau naga munafukar yarinya, jamgi ya taimakeki gimbiya ta umurnin ki nike jira." da sauri Bahijja tafara gilgiza kai tace. "Ranki shidade ki rufaman asiri kiyafeman karkisa a dakeni ki taimakaman bankula dake ba kiyi hakri!" kamar jira gimbiya Yazira ta fallama Bahijja mari tace. "Dan ubanki wato baki kulani dani ba kenan dan kinmaidani sakara idaninki a tsakar kai suke da zakice mani haka."? 


tunkan Bahijja tayi magana tafarajin saukar bulalai kamar ba gobe a kiɗime Bahijja ta rike bulalar da Uwani ke dukanta ta ita sai rawa jikin Bahijja keyi, sake ɗauketa da mari Uwani tayi tace. "Dan ubanki sake mani bulala kar in lahanaki." itadai bata saki ba tace. "Baba Uwani kiyi hakuri ku hukuntani da hanyar bani aiki mai wahala amman banda duka, ban lafiya." jitayi gimbiya Yazira ta kara wanketa da wani marin tace. "Wato ke ga tsinanniya har saikin zaɓi abinda kikeso aimaki kenan kina baiwar."? bata kara cewa komai ba, itako gimbiya Yazira tunda ta fahimci ba abinda Bahijja tafi tsoro a duniya irin duka tasama ranta kullin saitasa andaki Bahijja koda zata mutu ta ɗauki aniyar duk wannan hasken fatar da take takama sa ita saita maidata baka dan azaba,haka Uwani taci gaba da labtar Bahijja itako sai kwalla takeyi wasa wasa saida akaima Bahijja bulala tafi hamsin duk karfin Bahijja yagama karewa zuwa yanzun ko kukan ma takasayi, ana cikin dukan Bahijja saiga Jakadiya ta fito daga wajen Sarki Baidulƙais zata tafi gida, taga anacin zarafin Bahijja tana isowa ta iske gimbiya Yazira suna haɗa ido gimbiya Yazira ta watsama Jakadiya wani mugun kallo ba shiri ta sauke nata idon, kamar taima Bahijja kuka haka Jakadiya taji tagowar da zatayi ga azaba ta isheta suka haɗa ido da Jakadiya kwallan da take makalewa ne suka zubomata ganin ƴarta Bahijja cikin azaba, zuwa tayi gun gimbiya Yazira taimata magana a hankali wanda bansan metacema gimbiya Yazira ba sainaga ta dakatar da Uwani daga dukan Bahijja cikin tsawa tace. "Daga yau kisake aikata irin wannan kuskuren kuma gobe da safe kizo sashena kisameni kafin kije iskancin da kika saba." godiya Jakadiya taima gimbiya Yazira kmar jira da sauri ta isa wajen Bahijja, itako Uwani takema gimbiya Yazira baya tayi suka tafi sashen ta, itako Jakadiya ɗaga Bahijja tayi ta shiga da ita sashen ta, tana zuwa tafara yimata fita duk t fita hayyacin ta duk jini jikin Bahijja ranar ba Jakadiya tai Bahijja sai tsakar dare saida taba Bahijja kulawa ta musamman harta samu tayi bacci sannan itama ta kwanta cikeda tausayin Bahijja a cikin zuciyar ta........




Masarautar Ƙairu kuwa Sarki Danwar ne zaune anata fadanci ba abinda suke tattaunawa akai sai yanda zasu fuskanci Masarautar Daborah dan ɗauko takobin Bairu duk da sunsan ɗaukar takobin Bairu bakaramin aiki bane a garesu, suna cikin zaman saiga ɗan aike daga Masarautar Waisu yakawo ma Sarki Danwar wasika, koda yazo akai mashi iso wajen Sarki Danwar yana zuwa yakai gaisuwa sannan ya fadi abinda ke tafe dashi yaba Sarki Danwar wasika,yana ansa yaba magatakar dan fa'da  ya amsa tareda bashi izinin karanta wasikar. 




 _Daga Sarki Lahazu zuwa ga Sarki Danwar ina mai sanar dakai zuwa Masarautar Daborah dan kaima Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ziyarar ban girma tareda kasuwanci mai girman gaske, saidai zamuyi tafiya ukku ga wannan watan na Fabrilu, kahuta lfy ina saurarenka daga Masarautar Waisu._ 


Koda magatakarda ya gama karanta sakon Sarki Lahazu bakaramin mamaki bane ya bayyana a fuskar Sarki Danwar ba yasan ba wata dangantaka dake tsakanin su da Masarautar Daborah banda cin zarafi da kwantacciyar gaba mai karfin gaske amman miyasa Sarki Lahazu keso sukaima Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ziyara baccin yasan basu jituwa, ko Sarki Baidulƙais yasan da wannan ziyar tunda a tare suke, a wani bangare na zuciyar shi kuwa yana mamakin abinda Sarki Lahazu ke shiryawa akan Masarautar Daborah duk da shima yanada nashi mugun nufin akanta, sai kuma ya yake shawarar ansar tayin da Masarautar Waisu tazo mashi dashi duk da shima burin shi yaga ya nakasa Masarautar Daborah ta hanyar rabasu da takobin Bairu da haka ya kalli magatakarda yace. " maida mashi da sako akan na amince da tayin su, gamji yakaimu sannan aba ɗan aiken kudi domin guzuri yana gama faɗar haka ya mike tareda maya mashi baya ya tafi sashen shi domin nazari, shiko magatakarda yana gama rubuta wasikar yamika ma wannan bawan sannan yakama hanyar komawa Masarautar Waisu.....



 _Daborah_ 



Yarima Malikussaif ne zaune a sashen mahaifin shi suna tattaunawa akan lamarin Bahijja duk da mahaifin nashi ya nuna gamsuwa akan maganar da Malik yaje mashi da ita saidai baiji zai barshi ya tunkari Masarautar Raifasu yasan za'a iya salwantar mashi da rayuwar ɗa,Malik ne yayi magana yace.



"Abbu inaneman alfarma agareka kaimin izinin zuwa ɗauko Bahijja ta dawo wajen iyayen ta, bamu san a wane hali take ciki a halin yanzun ba, kodan addinin ta Abbu dan Allah.!" shiru yayi yana nazarin maganar Malik duk da yasan abinda ya faɗa gaskia ne amman baiji zaibar Malik yaje Raifasu, a zahiri ko cewa yayi. "Naji abinda kace saidai kaje zan nemeka idan nayi nazari komai kenan zakaji abinda na yanke akai." "Abbu daman abinda Ummu taimaka kayi hakri kayafe mata." murmushin manya Saifulislam yayi yace. "Ba abinda mahaifiyar ka takeman wanda ke ɓataman rai, saidai ɗabi'un tane banso musamman kan yanda take mu'ammala da talakawan ta duba da itace shugaba a wannan Masarautar." "hakane Abbu insha Allah zata daina." "Allah yasa yanzun kabar wannan maganar zan nemeka." cikin jin dadi Malikussaif yace shikenn Abbu Allah yakara girma." da Ameen ya amsa suna cikin fira Huzaifa da Yazid suka shigo, suka gaida Saifulislam, sannan suka fita tareda Malikussaif ba inda suka dosa sai cikin lambu.....



Koda gari ya waye dakyal Bahijja ta tashi tana tashi ta ɗebi ruwa tayi alwalla tayafa wani zani tagabatar da sallah duk da bawani iya gabatar da addini sosai tayi ba amman ba laifi ta iya duba da ba gaban iyayen ta takeba kuma Masarautar da take ba addinin musulunci sukeyi ba, tana cikin sallah Jakadiya ta shigo dakin tun tanajin haushin yanda  Bahijja ke ibadar ta har abin yafara burge Jakadiya, tana idasa sallah tafara addu'a tafara cewa. "Allah ka kawomani ɗauki ka fiddani cikin wannan ukubar danike ciki kamayar dani wajen iyayena ya Allah kasaka baba da alkhairy, Allah karabani da kaidin Sarki Baidulƙais da matar sa da duk wani azzalumi tayi tana hawaye sannan ta shafa a fuskar ta, tana gamawa ta mike taga Jakadiya tsaye saida gaban Bahijja ya fadi amman sai taga taimata murmushi itama maida mata da martani tayi nan tace. "Baba ya gajiyarki kinata ɗawainiya dani Allah yabiyaki Babata." murmushi tayi ta dafa kafadar Bahijja sannan tace. "Ƴata kinaso ki koma wajen iyayen ki ni kuma banji zan iya rayuwa babu ke Bahijja  yan shekarun danayi dake banjin zan iya rabuwa dake duk da kinada wayonki kika rabu da iyayen naki ammn ba sosai ba, yanzun zaki iya gane mahaifiyar ki Bahijja."?  


Murmushi tayi tace. "Eh Baba zan iya ganesu saidai sunan Masarautar mune ya shigeman a zuciya ta."
" hakane kam kinada shekara tara aka rabaki da iyayen ki Bahijja baici ace kin manta dasu ba, amman yanzun zanje wajen aiki kema kitashi duk da bakijin dadi kije wajen gimbiya Yazira anjima zan baki wani labari ƴata." cikin jin dadi Bahijja tace. "to Baba shikenan sai anjiman Baba zazzaɓi nikeji ina tsoran abinda zan tadda wajen gimbiya Yazira da Uwani." "kiyi hakuri Bahijja tunda harkikazo a baiwa baki da wani ƴanci ko jin dadi haka zakije watarana sai labari ƴata." cikeda gamsuwa suka fita ita Jakadiya ta shiga wajen Sarki Baidulƙais ita kuma Bahijja ta wuce wajen gimbiya Yazira, Jakadiya na shiga ta fara rabama bayi aiki, shiko Baidulƙais tunda ya tashi ba wanda yake son gani kamar Bahijja yana shirin yaga shigowar ta saiga wata baiwa ta shigo dukawa tayi ta gaida shi, sannan tace. "Ranka shidade anturoni dan ingyara maka ɗaki." wani wawan kallo ya jefama baiwar tai saurin sadda kanta ƙasa tace. "tuba nike gamji ya huci zuciyar ka." kofa ya nuna mata hakan yasa taja kafafun tafita jiki a sage, shiko wani irin iska ta fidda mai zafin gaske, bata jima da fitaba saiga Jakadiya ta shigo itama gaida shi tayi sannan tace. " gamji yabaka hakuri naga baiwar dana turo ta fita shine nace.....ɗaga mata hannu yayi yace. "Jamra yaushe kika fara karyamani doka a cikin wannan Masarautar kodan kinga nabaki mahimmanci a matsayinki na Jakadiya ta wadda ta kwashe shekaru masu yawa tanai mani hidima.!" 
"Rankashidade...."dakata Jamra ina Bahijja ban hanaki saka kowa yaiman aiki ba inba Bahijja ba."? "tuba nike Bahijja tana wajen gimbiya Yazira sannn kuma ta tashi batajin dadi." baisan sadda ya ɗago ya watsama Jakadiya wani mugun kallo ba yace. "Maza kikiramani Bahijja nan da minti ukku nabaki inbahaka nan  ba zaki gamu da fushina!" ai da sauri Jakadiya har tuntube takeyi bata san ta isa wajen gimbiya Yazira ba tana zuwa duk bayin dake wajen suka gaida Jakadiya sannan sukabar wajen itama gaida gimbiya Yazira tayi nan ta faɗa mata sakon Baidulƙais bakaramin haushine ya kama gimbiya Yazira ba ta kalli Jakadiya tace. "Jamra na fahimci hadin bakin ku ɗaya da maimartaba wai mike haɗin ki da wannan munafukar yarinya ko kema dake za'a haɗa bako aci amana ta."? "Haba ranki shidade kinsan ba yanda za'ai inbujirema umarnin maimartaba amman ba yanda za'ayi inci amanarki." "kije tana baya." godiya taima gimbiya Yazira sannan ta tafi, tana zuwa ta iske Bahijja da Indo suna noma cikin lambu duk inda ciyawa suka fito cikin shuke shuke Uwani sai bala'i takema Bahijja tana ganin Jakadiya ta gaidata nan Jakadiya takira Bahijja suka tafi hakan bakaramin taɓama Uwani zuciya yaiba, suna isa suka tadda Baidulƙais zaune falo gaishe sukayi sannan Bahijja ta shiga cikin ɗaki tafara aikin ta...... 


tana cikin shara taji shigowar Baidulƙais bakaramin tashin hankali Bahijja ta shiga ba, jitayi yace. "Ke baiwa zonan." jikinta ne yafara kyarma tazo wajen Baidulƙais ta tsugunna tace. "Ranka shidade gani." cikin fushi yace. " waya baki ikon zuwa aiki wajen gimbiya Yazira."? murya na rawa Bahijja tace. "Ranka shidade itace tabani umurnin hakan tunjiya." 
Sake kallanta yayi a sheƙe sannan yace. "Daga yau inna sake ganin ki, kinje wajen gimbiya Yazira na rantse da rawanin kakana Gursu saina halakaki karkiga ina baki kulawa kina yanda kikaga dama zan iya kasheki kinajina."? 
Jiki na rawa tace. "Naji insha Allah bazan kuma baah." ta ida maganar ta a rarrabe da sauri yace.
"Minaji kinacewa."? "Bazan koma ba tuba nike." gyaɗa kai yayi ita kuma tacigaba da aikin ta baki ɗaya jitake kamar ta faɗi kasa dan kafafun ta sunyi sanyi dayawa tarasa wacce irin musuba ce ke bibiyar rayuwar ta yanzun shikenn tasan itama kasheta za'ayi kamar yanda aka kashe mata ƴan uwa dan sunyi yunkurin guduwa tasab tabbas dole gimbiya Yazira saitasa taje wajen ta inba hakaba abanza zatai mata illah da wannan tunanin taji Sarki Baidulƙais yace...... 




 *Ƴar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*




Babi na 29&30



"Daga yau da karfe bakwai ki tabbata da kinzo aiki kuma bazaki bar wajen aikin kiba sai shidda na yamma  kinajina."? da toh Bahijja ta amsa haka ta ida gyaran dakin sannan ta fita zuwa falo nan ta iske Jakadiya komawa gefe tayi ta zauna tana jiran fitowar uban gidan nata.... 


 Bayan kwana biyu gimbiya Yazira ce zaune a dakin Sarki Baidulƙais ta, sai wannan kisisina takeyi kamar wata karuwa sai nanike mashi takeyi, shiko sai yakice yakeyi daga jikin shi, cikin kissa tace. "Mijina nikeso inasan wata alfarma agareka." batareda da ya kalleta ba yace. "Inajinki."


"Daman inasan wannan baiwar ka koreta ta koma Masarautar su nidai bataimani ba." "wakenan? Ƴar ƙinƙinace tazo mata tana sosa keya tace. "Bahijja mana ban kaunar yarinyar nan ko kadan natsaneta kataima.......daga mata hannu yayi yace. "Kinga wannan wane irin shashanci ne kinada hankali kuwa da zakice wai in maida Bahijja Masarautar su, kinsan wacece ita ko kinsan dalilin dayasa na anshi Bahijja karfa kimanta ɗana na cikina narasa mafi soyuwa agareni, daga yau kar inkarajin kin faɗi wannan maganar.!"


Jiki sanyaye tace. "Wai daman ba haka nike nufiba inason daman...."kinga Yazira kibar wannan zancen karma ki ɓataman rai."

Badan gimbiya Yazira tasoba ta hakura cigaba da bashi labarai tayi har rana ta fito jinanturo kofa yasa baki ɗaya suka maida hankalin su bakin kofa, Bahijja ce ya shigo cikin ɗakin tafara tattara kayan da tagani a ƙasa, gyaran muryar da taji anyi shi ya tabbatar mata da Sarki Baidulƙais yana cikin ɗakin daga kai tayi taga ita suke kallo wani irin mummunan faɗuwar gaba taji ganin irin kallan da gimbiya Yazira ke aika mata, a sanyaye ta duka ta gaidasu, wani ƙayataccen murmushi ya sakarma Bahijja sannan ya amsa itako gimbiya Yazira kallan kyama sa kaskanci ta dinga yima Bahijja a dakile ta amsa dan batasan mijin nata ya ɗauki wani abu a wajen ta, tashi Bahijja tayi zata fita waje Sarki Baidulƙais ya dakatar da ita, jitayi kamar ta saki fitsari a wando dan tsoro, maganar da yaimata shiyadawo da ita a tunanin ta, dawowa tayi ta duka har kasa ta sadda kanta kasa sannan yace. "Fara aikin ki ina zakije."?  "Ranka shidade inaso infara wani aikin ne kafin kugama." kamar baiji abinda tace mashi ba yace. "Haɗamani ruwan wanka." ba tareda ta bari sun haɗa ido da gimbiya Yazira ba ta tashi simisimi ta haɗa Sarki Baidulƙais ruwan wanka. a ɗarare tace. "Ranka shidade nagama." takoma gefe a takure ta rakuɓe itako gimbiya Yazira cike da kissa ta kama hannun mijin nata suka shiga bathroom kamar jira Bahijja tayi sauri ta gyara gadon tabar dakin fita falo takoma nan ta iske bayi sai aiki sukeyi tana zama wata baiwa tana shara tafara share Bahijja jitayi ta caka mata tsintsiya akafa da sauri ta tashi tace. "Haba baiwar Allah inkikaiman rauni fa." kamar jira tafara bala'i. "Karki rainamani hankali kebakida tunani miyasa bazaki tashi bakoni zan ɗaukeki."?  shiru Bahijja tayi itako sai jaraba takeyi kamar zata mari Bahijja, daman mugun haushin ta takeyi, tana cikin faɗa gimbiya Yazira ta fito tasha ado ta iske sai jaraba takeyi, suna ganin ta suka duka suka kawo gaisuwa."
Kallan tuhuma ta cigaba da yima Bahijja sannan tace. "Waike Bahijja wace irin hatsabibiyar yarinya ceke wai dame kikeji ne."? "Kimin aikin gafara daman...."daman me lallai yarinyar nan bakida hankali, ke Labuda inkin gama kizo kisamani a bangare na." "angama ranki shidade." 
tafiya tayi tana sane ta buge kafar Bahijja ta tafi, itako Labuda tacigaba da yima Bahijja masiba tashi tayi ta koma gefe ɗaya nan ma binta tayi tacigaba da bala'i itadai Bahijja batace komai ba tana cikin bala'in ta sukaga Baidulƙais yana kallan su.

Kowa saida yasha jinin jikin shi baki ɗayan su, suka zube ƙasa suna neman afuwa baice masu komai ba wucewa yayi ya koma cikin falon shi ya zauna kafa ɗaya kan ɗaya sannan yasa aka kira mashi su Bahijja suna isowa yaima Labuda wani mugun kallo saida ta firgita jitayi kamar ta saki zawo a wando kallan Bahijja yayi wadda tagama tsorata dan tunda yace zai kasheta intakara yima wani hidima take kara tsoron lamarin Baidulƙais acewar ta ko laifi tayi zai iya kasheta, maganar da taji yayi taji ta dakar mata dodon kunne a ruɗe ta kalle shi, shima kallan ta yayi yace. "Eh Mararta nace." yanda taga fuskar shi ba alamun wasa shiyasa ta mike ta mari Labuda a hankali duk tsoro ya kamata saboda uwar Labuda aminiyar Uwani ce Uwar bayi shiyasa take shakkar taɓa Labuda, tsawa Baidulƙais ya dakama Bahijja aiko duk takara ruɗewa yace. "Ko sai na gwada akan fuskar ki sannan zaki mararta."? 


Hannun Bahijja na kyarma ta kwadawa Labuda wani irin masifaffan mari mai ruɗarwa, hatta Bahijja saida ta gigita dan batai tinanin zata iya marin Labuda ba, ji tayi yace karamata, kwalla Bahijja takara dan tsoro haka ta sake wanke ta da wani marin saida yasa taima Labuda mari yakai huɗu sannan yasa aka kira mashi Sarkin bulala saida akaima Labuda bulala talatin  danko Bahijja kamar ita ake duka dan tsoro ga wasu kwalla dake sintira asaman kumatunta dan bata fidda rai ga duka ba, aiko saitaji Sarki Baidulƙais yace. "Yimata bulala ashirin." take ciki ya ɗuri ruwa sai mursiku takeyi Sarkin bulala na tunkaro Bahijja ta fasa kara da kuka tana neman ɗanewa jikin shi bakaramin mamaki ya kama Sarki Baidulƙais ba yana mamakin kalar tsoro na Bahijja inaga ita aka daka kila sai ta mutu ganin tana neman cakume Sarkin bulala yasa shi dakatar dashi, yabashi izinin tafiya da wani irin kallo yabi Bahijja duk ta gama fita tinanin ta baki ɗaya sai zabga uwar zufa takeyi ganin yanda duk damuwa ta bayyana a kan fuskar Bahijja yace. "Ke baiwa bata hakuri." haka Labuda taba Bahijja hakuri, sannan yace ta tashi ta tafi, kallan Bahijja yayi sannan yace. "Tashi kije gida, wane irin abune haka ya kamata kirage tsoro kodan lafiyarki." da sauri ta gyaɗa mashi kai ta mike ta tafi shiko da wani irin shu'umin murmushi yabita ganin yanda takeda tsoro ga wata irin masifar shi'awar Bahijja yanayi a wani ɓangare na zuciyar shi yana ganin bari yabari takara kwari sannan ya cimma burin shi.


A Masarautar Waisu kuwa yarima Haishir ne keta zagaye zagaye yakasa zaune yakasa tsaye tunda suka dawo daga Raifasu kullin da Bahijja yake kwana yake tashi a cikin zuciyar shi ganin lamarin na neman yafi tunanin shi hakan yasa ya tafi sashen Mahaifin shi ya iske shi kishingiɗe yana shan kankana yanda yaga Yarima Haishir kaɗai hakan ya tabbatar mashi da ba lfy, cikin kulawa ya kalli dan nashi yace. "Lafiya ka tsaya akaina meke damunka Ya Haishir."?



"Zaman dirshan yayi kamar wani zautacce yace. "Baba daman wannan itace kaunar da kake nuna mani kabarni da son yarinyar nan a zuciya ta ko kasan yanda nike masifar kaunar Bahijja, da ace a wannan Masarautar take da yanzun nagama kashe kishir ruwana akan ta."
Cikin jin haushin maganar Haishir Sarki Lahazu yace. "Ban haneka da neman matan banza ba wato itama wannan yarinyar bada aure kake son taba kenan."?  Shiru yayi aranshi ko cewa yayi nidai indai zanjini a tareda ita nayi nasara ai banda damuwa kotaya tazoman dai dai ne, Amman a zahiri cewa yayi. "Gamji ya huci zuciyar ka, amman inaso ka amso mani Bahijja daga wajen wannan wawan sarki Baidulƙais din." cikin gamsuwa yace. "Baka da damuwa ɗana koda zan rasa raina yanzun so nike nan da jibi mutafi Masarautar Daborah dan sanin halin da muke ciki hakan yasa na aika ma Sarkin Ƙaisu da wasika dan bansan Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tayi tunanin kawai wata akasa shiyasa kasanta mace ce mai kaifin tunani da basira uwa uba wannan mijin nata akwai fikira." "shikenan Baba amman kai kaɗai zakaje ko."? "Aaa Haishir dakai zani inasan kasaki jiki da Yarima Malik sannan kayi abota dashi dan cimma burin mu,dan haka ya kamata ka sake tako, ya kamata a halin yanzun kamaida masoyi makiya, sannan makiyin ka kamaida shi masoyin ka ta hakane kaɗai zakayi nasara kan abokan gaba ka hanasu sukuni ka rike wannan ilimin Ya magajina ɗana abun alfaharina kaidai daure kashiga tsara ka koma Jarumi kamar Yarima MalikusSaif shikenan zakayi sunan aduk faɗin duniya da nahiyar mu harma da kasarmu ta Gambia."


Wani irin dadi ne ya rufe Haishir saijin dadi yakeyi yana mamakin kalar fikira da dubara ta Mahaifin  nashi...... 


Washe gari da safe tunda Bahijja ta tashi ta tafi wajen aikin ta, akan hanya suka hadu da Uwani sai maman Labuda da wani mugun kallo suka dinga jifar Bahijja jiki sage ta samu tacigba da aikinta kamar yanda ta saba..... 



A Masarautar Daborah kuwa yauma Malik ne zaune a falo ya rasa gane damuwar shi kullin tunanin shi halin da Bahijja ke ciki musamman a ɓagaren addinin ta, tunda ya aiki Ashir kullin tunanin ranar dawowar shi yakeyi dan a halin yanzun harya fara sirin tafiya wajen neman Bahijja batareda kowa yasan kudurin shi ba.... 



Yau da marece Ashir ya shiga cikin Masarautar Raifasu a sirrince batareda da kowa yasan ya shigo ba haka ya shige cikin bayin Masarautar yayi basaja dan genema idan shi abinda Yarima Malik ya turoshi nema a cikin Masarautar Raifasu, a ranar ya iske wani bawa mai suna Ƙubar yana ansar bakar ukuba a wajen Sarkin bulala dan ya buge kayan gimbiya Yazira ba tareda ya gani ba shine ake gana mashi azaba tun rana har yamma hakan ya ɗagama Ashir hankali duk da yasan ba wani abu bane dan anmasa wannan dukan, saidai a ganin shi wannan ba wani abun da za'ace har anmashi irin wannan ukubar ba, suna cikin kallo ya haɗu da wani bawa Anzir nan yake tambayar Ashir daga ina, a wayance yace nan yake shima baisan Anzir din ba, bakaramin mamaki yayi ba ganin kaf taskar bayi ba wanda baisan shi amman ga Ashir yace baisan shi ahaka suka fara sabawa koda dare yayi tare suka tafi wajen ƴan uwan shi suka kwanta.......




Washe gari da rana haka Ashir ya kara fitowa yana ƴan dube dube ko Allah zaisa yaga Bahijja a cikin Masarautar Raifasu koda cikin taskar bayi ne amman bai ganta ba, hakan yasa ya fito yawo a cikin taskar bayi yana cikin tafiya ya samu wata bishiya ya zauna dan hutawa, itako Bahijja ta gama komai zaune take tamaima Baidulƙais fita yana kishingiɗe sai wannan cika yakeyi yana batsewa, nan Jakadiya ta shigo da bayi kowa ɗauke da abinci haka Bahijja ta zubama Baidulƙais abinci saidai wannan karon Allah yasota da kanshi yaci ita kuma tacigaba da yimashi fita.... 


Shiko Ashir koda ya zauna gefen wannan bishiya yana cikin tinanin ta inda zaifara duba Bahijja yaji ankira sunan shi a ruɗe ya waiga yana kallan mai kiranshi wanj bawa ne yagani daga cikin bayin su waɗanda akaba Baidulƙais suda Bahijja da sauri ya riko hannun bawan yana mai rufe mashi baki suka zauna gefe cikin ɗimauta Ashir yace.

"Tasi'u daman kunan nan hardakai aka bada."? Mamaki bayyane a fuskar Tasi'u yace. "Eh Ashir munanan muna fama da ƙangin bauta da wahala." "ina sauran mutanen mu."? Numfasawa yayi sannan yace. "Sabi'u Hashim da Kudur duk an kashe su da mashi a gaban kowa aka kashe su." cikin firgici yace. "Kisa misukayi har haka."? "Hmmm yunkurin guduwa sukayi shine akai masu wannan ukubar." "yanzun dai saura ku bakwai ina sauran suke."? "Kaga Ashir ni kaina ina iya kwana ukku banga ɗan uwana ba a cikin su, ba'asan aga muna huɗɗa da junanmu shiyasa ma kaga muna nisantar junan mu, yau kwana ukku daga Hudu sun gaisa da Mahadu ranar dukan da Sarkin horo yaimasu banyi zatan zasu rayuba, kaga nima nan da kake ganina atareda kai zan iya rasa raina bari intafi bakin aiki na." rike shi Ashir yayi yace. "Kai tsaya akwai aikin da Yarima Malik ya aikoni anan Masarautar bari intambayeka wani abu." cikin mamaki Tasi'u yace. "Minene wane aiki ne."?  "Ina Bahijja tana cikin Masarautar Raifasu kuwa."?  "Kwarai  kuwa Bahijja ta nan cikin taskar bayi sannan itace hadimar Sarki Baidulƙais dan haka ni kaina ganin Bahijja wahala yake mani." murmushi Ashir yayi sannan yace. "Kana nufin Bahijja tana Masarautar nan kenan."
"Shiyasa na tabbatar maka da hakan saidai banda hanyar da zaka iya ganin ta, sannan kasani duk cikin babu wanda yakai Bahijja fuskantar kalubale da wahala a cikin wannan Masarauta, acewar wasu daga cikin bayi wai Baidulƙais son Bahijja yakeyi yana son cika burin shi, wannan dalilin yasa gimbiya Yazira take takura Bahijja da duk wasu mabiyanta kaji kaɗan daga cikin abinda nasani da Bahijja dan raban da inga Bahijja nakai sati biyu koda na ganta ba abinda zan iya yimata saidai fatan alkhairy."

Jikin Ashir ba karamin mutuwa yaiba yace. "Tasi'u duk da ka tabbatar mani cewa Bahijja na nan saidai kasani bazan iya komawa uban gidana ahaka ba batareda naga Bahijja ido da ido ba, dan haka kasani zan zauna zuwa gobe ne." "shikenan zan baka masauki amman kasani ni dakai muna cikin haɗari saidai akan aikin Yarima Malik zan iya bada raina kyauta adalin Yarima." "naji dadi insha Allah ba abinda zai samemu yanzun katafi wajen aikinka zuwa anjima munyi magana."
"A ina zaka ganni." "nan zamu haɗu wajen magariba." gyaɗa mashi kai alamar toh haka Tasi'u yatafi Ashir najin tausan su a ranshi duk da yasan ko Daborah bayi bawani jin dadi sukeyi ba ammn in Masarautar ku ba laifi kanada ƴanci ba kamar inda ba Masarautar kuba da haka ya shige cikin bayi yana cigaba da nazartar su.


A wajen Bahijja kuwa kamar kullin yauma tagama aikin ta baki ɗaya hakan yasa ta fito bayan gida itada Indo suna firar su, kasancewar yau Sarki Baidulƙais tunda yagama karin kumallo ya tafi fa'da kuma ya shaidama Jakadiya bazai dawoba sai faɗuwar rana shiyasa yau Bahijja ketajin dadi, sauran bayi ko masuyimata hassada sai haushin ta sukeji ga ba damar su duketa dan duk labari ya karaɗe taskar bayi da kewayen ta Bahijja ta mari Labuda hakan yasa kowa ya shiga taitayin shi akan hayema Bahijja shiyasa suka kara yarda Baidulƙais son Bahijja yakeyi, suna cikin firarsu Indo tacema Bahijja. "Kawata niko nazo maki da wata gulma da nikeji anata yi a cikin taskar bayi." cikeda mamaki Bahijja tace. "Wane labari ne wannan kinsan ni ban cika son ƴan tsugunne tsugunne ba musamman idan abu bai shafeni ba." cikin ɓacin rai Indo tace. "Ke matsalata dake gwalisa tunda nike dake na taɓa kawo maki zancen wasu."? "Aaa Indo karki ɗauka cin zarafi ne da ankama mutun yana gulmar wasu musamman ta gidan Sarauta yanzin an kama mutun aci zarafin sa ko kuma akasheka abanza ko duka kingan ninan shiyasa ban saka kaina cikin matsala da damuwa, amman inajinki minene." "shikenan kinsan a kewaye Masarautar nan ana cewa Sarki Baidulƙais yana sonki shiyasa yasa kika mari Labuda dan kawai taimaki faɗa." da sauri Bahijja ta ɗaura dara daran idanuwan masu ɗaukar hankali akan Indo tace. "Kinga Indo zantambayeki." "inajinki." "minene matsayin ki a cikin wannan Masarautar."? 
Kallan kin rainani Indo taima Bahijja sannan tace. "Bawai kuma ƴar matemakiyr Uwar bayi." murmushi Bahijja tayi sannan tace. "Toh tsaya kiji idan baki daina ɗaukar jita jitaba wlh ko Sarkin bayi ya haifeki zaki iya rasa ranki, dan haka ni Baidulƙais baitaɓa yiman wata magana da ta danganci so ba, duk da ni bansan ma mi ake kira da sonba ammn kamar yanda kika faɗa daga lamari ne mai girma dan haka ni Bahijja na karyata wannan zancen banza ne."
"Amman Bahijja baikamata ace kina boye abinda kika saniba." cikin mamaki Bahijja ke kallan Indo tana nazari kamar ya so ko abinda Baba ke fadamani kar in saki jikina dashi shine son, toh inko har hakane tabbas indai ya nunamani wannan son zan iya mutuwa kenan tabb aiko zan iya guduwa bari anjima intabayi Baba asalin Masarautar mu bazan iya zamaba in mutu nabani Allah dai yasa wannan mugun mutumin ba son zai nuna manba da nashiga ukku! tana cikin wannan tunani taji Indo ta taɓata a firgice ta kalleta tace. "Kinga Indo tashi mukoma bakin aikin mu."? "tayaya bayan baki tabbatar mani da zargin da ake maki ba." dayake Indo ta girmi Bahijja nesa bakusa ba zatakai shekara sha takwas itako Bahijja bata wuce shekara sha ukku ba, shiyasa tafita sanin abubuwa, cewa tayi. "Bansan komai ba bansan kina karyatani nidai natafi wajen aikina sai anjima." haka tabar Indo cikeda mamki aranta......




Shiko Ashir koda ya dawo wajen wannan bishiyar zama yayi yana jiran isowar Tasi'u baijima ba saiga shi ya iso, tashi yayi suka dunguma cikin masaukin su koda suka isa zama sukayi ga wata irin yunwa Ashir naji amman ba abinda zasu samu suci kallan Tasi'u yayi yaga duk ya galabaita ga wahala kuma ga yunwa tashi Ashir yayi ya duba yaga kudin da Yarima Malik yaba shi dan yayi guzuri ya fiddo ya kalli Tasi'u yace. "Kaga inazamu samu abinci kaima yunwa kakeji kafin sauran yan uwa su dawo suma nasan da yunwa zasu dawo." kalan shi Tasi'u yayi cikin jimami yace. "Kaiko dai Ashir kana bani mamaki da dariya wayagaya maka munada ƴanci da zamuje kasuwa musayi abinci? ai sai ainahin ƴan wannan Masarautar sukeda wannan damar suma a sati sau ukku suke cin kasuwa muko bamu da wannan gatan karma kasa mu shiga cikin hali." a kiɗime Ashir yace. "Wai baku da damar da zakuyi komai kamar sauran bayin Masarautar Raifasu? aina ɗauka bawa bawane ba wanda yafi wani kuma sunan su ɗaya." "Aaa kayi kuskure sunan akwai nuna kabilanci, Sarauniya Zulaiƙa itace ta maida bawa bawane a wajen ta kuma ba'a bakin komai yake ba, shi Sarki Baidulƙais bawa ba ɗaya bane, sannan a wajen wahala yafi tausan nashi bayin saboda basuyin aikin wahalar damu keyi kuma akwai ranaku ukku a sati daya ware masu ta ƴanci bayi dan su sama damar wanke suturar su da kayan bukatu mufa? Kaga ko anan kasan yafi Sarauniyar mu, kishin bayin sa duk da baimasu da wasa yana zalintar su, saidai akwai lokacin da suke hutawa kuma a ranakun da rana kowa ke tashi aiki dan yin tasu bukatar, saidai mu bamu tashi sai marece saboda tsabar kiyayar da sukema Masarautar Daborah da nuna kabilanci." take jikin Ashir yakara sanyi dafa kafaɗar Tasi'u yayi sannan yace. "Gaskia kam kuna cikin hali duk da ko a Masarautar Daborah kuke ba abinda da zai sauya saidai Sarauniya Zulaiƙa bata yarda bawan ta ya kwana bai ciba kuma tana bada lokacin da kowa zai samu ya shirya, amman a sati sau ɗaya sabanin nan da bayi suka fimu ƴanci dan dai nuna kabilanci, yanzun ina sauran bayin mu suke."? 
"Hmmm Ashir kenan basu gama aikiba har  yanzun." "kamar yaaah....yanda Ashir yaja maganar shiya tabbatarma Tasi'u maganar taba Ashir mamaki da tsoro ƴar dariya yayi marar sauti dan baida karfin jikin da zaiyi mai sauti yace. "Eh suna aikin bauta basu dawoba nima dan nagama sannan uban gidan nawa yana da saukin kai ba laifi." "ikon Allah yanzun isha'i tayi so ake suyi aikin tsakar dare komai ake nufi? ko sallah ba wanda yayi a cikin hattani." kallan bakada aikinyi Tasi'u yaima Ashir yace. "Kaga malam sallah da kake gani ba kullin mutun zai samu yayi taba idan har aka kama mutun nayi kashe shi zasuyi shiyasa sai dare ya raba muke tashi muyi dukan su wani bima daga zaune zakayi basaja kayi kamar mai gyangyaɗi dan haka kaima ka boye kanka kayi sallar ka nan wajen."
"Naji alwalla fa." "idan aka hanamu dibar ruwa mukanyi taimama muyita." ikon Allah cewar Ashir sannan yace. "Idan kuka bukaci shan ruwa fa."? "Hakuri maganin zaman duniya saidai akwi wata makobciyar ku nan Saude wanibi itake sammana ruwa, dan na fahimci tanada tausayi, sannan wasu lokutan idn Allah yasa zamu hadu da Bahijja sai in Allah yasa taje gidan ta gaskia tanaima Bahijja mutunci."



Cikin jin dadi, Ashir yace. "Yanzun ka sanar dani labari mai dadin sauraro, tabbas yanzun nasan zamu hadu da Bahijja a cikin Masarautar Raifasu, kaga inajin kishin ruwa kuma naga dakin naku ba ruwa yaza'ayi."?  "Eh wannan mai sauki ne zamu iya zuwa rafi can gefen lambu amman cikin Masarauta ne idan munci sa'a abarmu ku dibi ruwan." daukar batiki sukayi zasu fita saiga su Laminu sun shigo a wahalacce kallan kallo sukadinga yima Ashir duk mamaki yagama kamasu kamar a mafarki haka suke kallan Ashir rungume shi sukayi kowa fuskar shi cikeda mamaki ganin kallan da suke mashi yasa Ashir cewa. "Kuke mani kallan tuhuma yanzun dai ku huta bari muje mudawo ɗebo ruwa anjima mungaisa." gyaɗa masu kai sukayi sannan suka fita duk da dare yayi amman kana gane mutane, haka suka fita har suka isa wajen ɗibar ruwa suka ɗebo kan hanyar dawowa ne suka haɗu da Zainab itada Indo suma sun ɗebo ruwa a hanya suna labarin Bahijja Indo ke cewa. "Zainab nifa gani nikeyi akwai abinda Bahijja ke boye mana akan Sarki Baidulƙais nidai ban yarda da hidimar da take mashi ba." ɗan karamin tsaki Zainab taja sannan tace. "Kinga Indo kirabu da zancen Bahijja daman ai kowa yasan Sarki son Bahijja yakeyi." ji sukayi ance ƴan mata,har kundawo ɗebo ruwan kenan, sai ina zuwa har haka."?  A daburce Zainab ta kalli Indo sukace. "Eh mundawo." ji sukayi ance. "Ina Bahijja nagan ku, ku biyu." duk Indo tasha jinin jikin ta Zainab nason taga mai masu wannan tambayar amman sun kasa ganin fuskar shi, cewa sukayi. "tana gidan Baba Saude can mukaga ta shiga." Tasi'u ne yace. "Hakane daman sakon Jakadiya zamu bata amman da safe munbata."duk yanda Zainab taso taga abinda akeson aba Bahijja ya gagareta karshe ma suka neme su ko sama ko kasa basu gansu ba, sai abun ya zamar masu kamar a mafarki, suko su Ashir suna dawowa sukayi salla sannan suka dinga fira kowa dandai yace masu zuwa jibi zai koma gida da haka kowa ya kwanta a gajiye, itako Bahijja tana gama sallah tafara fira da Baba Saude rabi na iyayenta ne cema Saude tayi. "Baba Saude niko inasan inkoma wajen iyeyena da yaya Malik dan har ya fara shige mani, saida yanada mutunci sosai abin damuwar ko nace zantafi bansan inda zan samu garin muba, tunda na manta sunan shi." itako Saude murmushi tayi duk da ba addinin su ɗaya da Bahijja amman tana son ɗabi'un ta, "haba ƴata dakin bar wannan zancen ni kaina gashi bansan ko ina aka sameki ba da nafada maki amman daga yau karki kara yin wannan maganar a gaban mutane za'a iya cutar dake." cikin gamsuwa Bahijja tace toh, da haka sukayi bacci.



Washe gari da safe Bahijja ce tagama shiri zata tafi wajen aikin ta tunda gari ya waye Ashir ya sake ɓadda kama yana kallan mai shige da fice itako Bahijja sallama taima Saude ta fito tacigaba da tafiya baki ɗaya hankalin ta nakan hanyar ta, jitayi Zainab ta kwalla mata kira, tsyawa tayi tajirata tana isowa suka tafi, tunda kunna Ashir suka jiyo kiran da akaima Bahijja ya fito waje ko zaigan ta yaga babu kowa hanya yabi yana dubawa can ya hangi wulgin ƴan mata biyu sadda zaikai wajen su har sun shige wajen aikin su da alama gidan Sarauta ne suka shiga, haka ya jiyo zai koma cikin taskar bayi, jiyayi ance kai ɗan Saurayi daga ina...... 




 *Ƴar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*
