*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*



Babi na 31&32


Cikin sarkewar murya Ashir yace. "Am daman daman nazo...."kaga anshi wannan kwandon ka kaima Mai Martaba yanzun nan." baijira abinda zai ceba, Sarkin bayi ya juya yatafi wata irin ajiyar Zuciya ta sauke jiki sanyaye ya shige cikin sashen Sarki Baidulƙais yana shiga ya taddashi zaune saman wata irin kayatacciyar shimfiɗa, dukawa yayi yakai gaisuwa wajen Sarki Baidulƙais, kallan shi yayi cikeda kulawa sannan yace. "Bawa waya aikoka da wannan kwandon."? 
Shiru Ashir yayi yakasa magana yarasa mizaicema Sarki Baidulƙais dan baisan sunan wanda yaba shi sako ba, zaiyi magana kenan sukaji ance. "Ranka shidade nagama haɗa ruwan." da sauri Ashir ya ɗago idanun shi ya azasu a fuskar Bahijja wani mummunan faɗuwar aba yaji, ga wani irin girma  da kyau taimashi ganin irin kallan  da yaga Baidulƙais naima Bahijja shiyasa yace. "Ranka shidade...."ɗaga mashi hannu yayi yace. "Kije madafa kice ahada mani farfesun ƴanciki ki tahomani dashi." cikin sarkewa murya tace. "Angama Ranka shidade gurfanai ya taimakeka." mikewa tayi zata tafi karab suka haɗa ido da Ashir hakanan taji faduwar gaba ya kamata, sake kallan shi tayi shima kallan nata yakeyi ya sakar mata da murmushi, tafiya tayi tana mamakin wannan wane kalar bawane itada bata taɓa ganin shi ba a cikin Masarautar Raifasu koda yake ai bayin ne dayawa daman ba kowa zaka saniba da wannan zancen ta tunkari madafa dan cika umurnin Sarki. 

Shiko Baidulƙais sake kallan Ashir yayi yace. "Bawa wane aiki kakeyi."? Jiyayi gaban shi ya faɗi karfin hali yayi yace. "Ranka shidade ina kula da wajen dawakai ne da basu ruwa." ya ida maganar yana sosa keya duk ya takure, cikeda mamaki yace. "Daman an sauya Gunzar daga kula da dawakai bansani ba? Nayi mamaki koda yake kilan yanzun tsufa yafara ka mashi, ko kaima ɗansa ne." a daburce yace "eh aaa daman ba...."ya kake magana a daburce kamarya eh aa." buɗa bakin Ashir keda wuya saiga Jakadiya ta shigo itama zubewa tayi takwashi gaisuwa sannan tace. "Ranka shidade Sarkin dawakai Gunzar yace insanr dakai ana bukatar sauya wasu dawakan asamu matasa." cikin mamki Sarki Baidulƙais ya kalli Ashir yace. "Amman yanzun wannan bawn ke faɗaman cewa Gunzar ya aje aiki, kuma saigaki da wannan sakon ya abin yake Jamra."? 
Kallan shi Jakadiya tayi tasan bata taɓa ganin wannan fuskar ba kuma ba laifi tasan rabi da kwatan bayin Masarautar Raifasu ganjn yanda Ashir ya raina gaskiar shi hakan yasa take Jakadiya tagane akwai abinda ke tafe dashi saidai bata nuna hakan bka a fuskar  taba saboda batasan tai sanadiyar shigar wani a matsalata a zahiri sai tace. "Ranka shidade kasan tsufa na Gunzar inaga ya shafa'a kuma kasan sai anayi ana kara daura yara a hanya, shiyasa kaga hakan." shiko Ashir ka shine kawai baiba dan tsoro da yagama cika mashi ciki harya hango ankashe shi ganin abinda Jakadiya tace yai saurin cewa. "Kwarai kuwa ranki shidade hakane kam, bari inje bakin aikina kahuta lfy uban gidana." dakatar dashi Sarki Baidulƙais yayi ta hanyar daga mashi hannu sannan yace. "Inkaje kacema Gunzar nabada izinin hakan amman ya nemi Sarkin yaki, daga nan kukara kulawa da dakai sosai yanda yakamata, saidai minene sunan bawan."?  Jiyayi kamar ya ruga ya rasa mizaicema Sarki Baidulƙais dabara ta faɗo mashi yace. "Tarib." gilgiza kai Sarki Baidulƙais yayi alamun gamsuwa hakanan yaji Ashir ya kwanta mashi a zuciya aranshi ko yace ya kamata kazoma mai kulawa da al'amurana dan dagani zakayi Jarumta amman a zahiri bashi izinin tafiya yayi, sannan ya kalli Jakadiya yace. "Jamra daman kinsan wannan bawan."? Gilgiza kai tayi tace. "Gurfanai ya taimakeka bansan wannan bawan ba yau nafara ganin shi." 
"Shikenan idan kin fita kibiya wajen Gunzar kice inasan ganin shi." da toh Jakadiya ta amsa sannan ta tafi madafa dan ganin wainar da ake toyawa.

Shiko Ashir koda yafita bakaramin ajiyar Zuciya yayi ba dan ya bala'in tsorata tunowa yayi da abinda Yarima Malik ya cemashi kada yabari kowa ya ganshi a cikin Masarautar Raifasu kawai ya dubo in Bahijja na nan, amman gashi har magana da Sarki Baidulƙais yayi addu'a yafara Allah yasa kada ya shiga damuwa ba inda ya koma sai cikin taskar bayi yaje masaukin su Tasi'u ya kwanta ganin kwanciyar bazata yimashi yasa shi fitowa ya shiga gidan Saude isketa yayi kwance da alama bacci takeyi tunani yayi mizaice mata da yazo wajen ta hakan yasa ya fita yana fitowa suka haɗe da juna ganin zai wuce ta yasa tace. "Kai baka ganin mutane ne da baka bada hakuri." wani irin mugun kallo ya watsa mata nakinyi kadan zaiyi magana yaga wasu bayi zasu wuce sun gaida Uwani cikin girmamawa hakan ya tabbatar ma Ashir cewa wannan tanada wani matsayin na daban waya zuciyar cemashi tayi kodai itace Uwar bayin su dagani ma kodan kayan dake jikin tama, hakuri yabata zaitafi tace. "Kai Saurayi daga ina."?  "Nan nike Baba inaga kilan baki kuladani bane shiyasa." yakarasa maganar cikin garaje dan ya kosa yabar wajen, zatayi magana ya tafi duk mamaki ya gama kama Uwani tana mamakin waya haife wannan bawan mai nagarta inki har siyoshi akayi ina masaukin shi da bata taɓa ganin shi ba sai yau,  da wannan tunanin ta shige gidan Saude tana ta bacci tadata Uwar bayi tayi.... 



Shiko Ashir ya ɗaukarma ranshi gobe zaibar Masarautar dan yaganema kanshi komai da yakeso ya gani, itako Bahijja kamar yanda Sarki Baidulƙais ya umurce haka ta kawo mashi farfesun yan ciki da kanta ta bashi sannan yace ta cinye sauran haka takoma gefe taci abincin ta sannan ta ajema Jakadiya itama taci ta rage sauran ta juye a leda da niyar kaima Baba Saude dan tasan da wahala idan yau ta karya, koda marece yayi tashi tayi taima Sarki bankwana ta koma taskar bayi itako Jakadiya gidanta ta wuce  tana isa ta iske Uwani a wajen Saude gaidasu tayi sannan ta zauna hakan yasa Uwani ta aiki Bahijja wajen gimbiya Yazira duk yanda taso ta faɗamata abinda Sarki Baidulƙais yace amman Uwani taki ta saurari Bahijja, haka ta tafi da wasu kaya a hannun ta, haka ta tafi har bakin kofar shiga sashen gimbiya Yazira saidai takasa shiga koda ta shiga ba kowa sai bayi ƴan kaɗan ta iske dubawa tayi falo batanan hakan yasa ta tambayi wata baiwa tace tana aikin dakin ta, komawa tayi gefe shiru ba gimbiya Yazira hakan yasa ta shiga gudun kada Baidulƙais ya shigo ya sameta a cikin sashen gimbiya Yazira, tana tura kofa ita kuma gimbiya Yazira ba shirin fitowa bangeje juna sukayi turaren dake hannun gimbiya Yazira ya fashe fadi kasa ya fashe ita kuma Bahijja duk kayan da Uwani tabata sun ɓaci tsoro yakara kama Bahijja batasan tace, inalillahi wa'inna ilahir raji'un ba, wani mugun kallo gimbiya Yazira ta watsa ma Bahijja take ta kifa mata wani marayan mari tasake kifa mata wani har sau ukku saida Bahijja ta faɗi wata baiwa tasa takira mata Sarkin bulala haka tasa akaita jibgar Bahijja tun tana kuka harta kasa saida yagama gibgarta sannan tasa yakaita taskar bayi ya ɗaure ta jikin bishiya yaci gaba da dukanta, sannan tace zagar masu gurfanai tayi koda yaji haka bai ko ɗaga kafa ba wajen dukan Bahijja ba haka yakaita taskar bayi ya ɗaureta da bishiyar kusa da gidan Saude yaita duka Tasi'u ne yadawo wajen aikin shi ya tadda wannan aika aika da sauri ya shige wajen ya shaidama su Ashir kafin kace me wajen ya cika da bayi itako Bahijja harta canza kamanni dan duka ga azaba tana sha, duk wanda Sarkin bulala yafada mashi abinda Bahijja ta aikata sai ace daman akasheta itako sai kalmar inalillahi wa'inna ilahir raji'un take ambata da karfin gaske koda su Ashir suka fito baisan yaima Bahijja kwalla ba dan tausayi ranar anma Bahijja bulala tafi ɗari a jikinta labari ya bazu har cikin fa'da, hakan yasa Sarki Baidulƙais yaji, ranar Bahijja haka ta kwana ɗaure a wulakance tunda  uwar safiya Sarki Baidulƙais yasa aka taho da Bahijja, koda ta isa wanke ta da mummunan  mari yayi daman gimbiya Yazira tagama gayama Sarki Baidulƙais karya, cikin fushi yace. "Wato ke Bahijja dankinga mahimmancin dana baki shine zaki fara zagin gurfanai ko, dan nabaki damar kiyi addinin ki bashi ke nuna kici zarafin nawa addinin ba dan haka yau zaki ɗandani kudarki shine kikaje wajen iyalina dan cin fuska." baiwar Allah Bahijja ko magana ta kasa haka Sarki Baidulƙais yasa aka kara dukanta sannan aka bata aikin wahala ranar haka ta yini tana aikin wahala itako gimbiya Yazira sai murna takeyi tasamu damar data raba Bahijja da mijin ta a saukake ranar Jakadiya da kyal tai bacci dan tausayin Bahijja hakma su Ashir



Sarkin dawaki tsugunne a gaban Sarki Baidulƙais yana kwasar gaisuwa sannan yace. "Gurfanai yakara girma kiranka ya sameni." cikin kulawa yace. "Gunzar  daman ka ajiye aikine bansani ba."?  Cikeda mamaki yace. "Ranka shidade ai bantaɓa fashin aiki ba saidai lalura mai tsanani. " mamaki fal a fuskar Sarki Baidulƙais yace. "Amman jiya wani matashi yace mani daga cikin zuriar kane ka aje aiki sannan kuma sunan shi Tarib nayi mamaki dan banyi tsammanin kana da ɗa kamar wannan ba." duk kan Gunzar yagama kullewa cewa yayi. "Ranka shidade kaima kasan ƴaƴana matane sannan namijin bai wuce shekara sha ukku a duniya Gaksia banda masaniya akan wannan bawan dakake magana akai." "lallai kam anyi wasa da hankalina kenan, kuma bai baka sako naba." "eh banga kowaba duk jiya sai Jakadiya kuma nina bata sako zuwa wajen ka." 
Cikeda da bacin rai Sarki Baidulƙais yace. "Shikenan na yarje kuje a sauya dawakai sannan kuma kuje da Sarkin yaki sannan abasu kulawa ta musamman yana gama fada yaba Gunzar damar tafiya sannan yace ya turo mashi uban bayi, mamaki sosai ya addabi Baidulƙais aranshi ko yana mamakin wanda yaimashi wasa da tunani har haka yana cikin al'ajabi ya tsunkayi maganar Sarkin bayi dukawa yayi yagaida Mai Martaba sannan yace. "Humtu inasan kasa ataramani bayin Raifasu kaf  a babban fili dakaina zanzo akwai wanda nike nema." da sauri yace. "Angama kashi lafiya." yana tafiya ba inda ya sauka sai cikin taskar bayi



Kowa ya gama hallara a wani katan fili dake wajen taskar bayi kowa sai alajabi yakeyi jin kiran da akace Sarki naimasu hatta Bahijja dogarwa basu bari ba duk da batajin dadin dan ta bugu akan sharrin da gimbiya Yazira tai mata, bayan sun fito su Tasi'u ne suka fito koda Ashir yaga Sarki Baidulƙais yataho cikin filin shida Jakadiya take ya raina gaskiar shi kowa ya maida hankalin shi wajen Sarki Baidulƙais hakan yasa Ashir yabi wajen wani katon icce ya boye yana hangen abinda akeyi, take Sarki yafara duba bayin daya bayan daya amman ba Ashir ba alamin shi nan Sarki Baidulƙais yafadi dalilin tarasu, kowa yai mamaki musamman su Tasi'u da suka nemi Ashir sama ko kasa basu san ya zille ba, take Uwar bayi ta tuna da bawan da bugeta daman bata amince dashi ba, hakama Sarkin bayi ya tuna yaba wani bawa kwandon kayan marmari yakai ma Sarki Baidulƙais, duk kansu ya kulle itako Jakadiya tana ganin shi tagane bako ne taki fadane gudun kada Sarki Baidulƙais yasa a cutar dashi, take wutar Bahijja ta ɗauke tabbas ta ganshi wajen Sarki shekaran jiya saidai tarasa ina tasan wannan bawan kowa da abinda yake sakawa a ranshi ɗago kan da Bahijja zatayi sukayi ido hudu da Ashir bayan wani katon icce saidai batajin zata iya tonashi tasan basu da imani, bata gama wannan tunanin ba taji Uwar bayi tace. "Ranka shidade tabbas koni naga wannan bawan kuma banyarda dashi ba raina nabani kamar akwai abinda yazoyi nafi zargin ko mutanan bayin nan ne na Masarautar Daborah musamman kamar yana bibiyar Bahijja ne dan naga yana shawagi tsakanin gidan Saude da masaukin bayin Daborah." wani irin ɓacin raine ya turnike Sarki Baidulƙais dayaji zancen Uwani kallan Bahijja yayi wanda tai saurin sunkuyar dakanta ƙasa wani irin tsoron Baidulƙais ke kara shigar Bahijja sawa yayi aka kamo Bahijja duk ta ruɗe aka gurfanar da ita a gaban shi duk yanda Bahijja taga Ashir hakan baisa ta nuna shiba duk da taga ananeman yimata ƙage akan abun bata da wata masaniya akan shi, sai raba ido takeyi kamar marar gaskia wani mugun kallo Baidulƙais ya jefama Bahijja sannan yace..


"Mike tsananin ki da wannan bawan."? Baki daya can cikin Bahijja sun game bakinta duk ya bushe dan tsoro a ruɗe tace. "Ranka shidade Bahijja batasan wannan mutumin dake magana akaiba asalima bantaɓa ganin shiba, sai a wajen ka daya kawo maka kayan marmari na rantse bansan waye shiba.!"ta ida maganar zuciyar ta na bugawa da sauri dan tasan yanda Baidulƙais kejin haushin ta zai iya yimata lahani akan wannan maganar da ake kokarin dangantata da ita, jitayi yace. "Ni zakima karya ko Bahijja bayan cin mutuncin addinina dakikeyi yanzun kuma ke zaki ha'ince kikawo wani mutun ko, mikike nufi nagane dan ki gudu ko."? A razane Bahijja takalle shi take hawaye masu zafi sukai nasarar wanke mata fuska, shiko Baidulƙais suna haɗa ido da Bahijja yaga ta aka mashi dara daran idanuwan ta sai yaji taimashi kwarjini kuma baiji zai iya cigaba da hukuntata a wannan lokacin, ji yayi tace. "Wa'iya zubillahi kaicona da cin amana taya zan gudu bayan a halin yanzun ko hanyar garinmu bansani ba asalima na manta sunan garin mu..... Baijira cewarta ba yace. "Yimin shiru minene wai'ya mi ko sunan bawan kike so kifaɗa, Sarkin horo, ka kaimin ita gidan kaso saidai bance kadake taba a jeta zuwa nan da ɗan kankanin lokaci tukun." ba Bahijja hatta su Ashir bakaramin tsoro yakama shib ganin za'a kai Bahijja gidan kaso, da yaya  niyar fitowa gani yayi kwara yakaima Malik abinda ke faruwa ya ɗauke ta baki ɗaya Masarautar zaifi, danko bayin Daborah har sunfara kuka sunsan Bahijja batada masaniya akan Ashir haka aka kama Bahijja tana kuka eu Zainab da Indo na kuka danko Saude har kanta ke mata ciwo, shi kanshi Baidulƙais din yanajin abun har ranshi dan duk kanshi ya kulle ya rasa mike faruwa haka a baibai, nan aka sallami sauran bayi, yace bayin Daborah su tsaya yasa Sarkin bulala ya dinga dukan su kamar jakai ba'a kyale suba sai dare sannan akace gobe ma da karfe bakwai su gurfana a wannan filin dan cigaba da karbar horo mai tsanani dan basu rasa sanin Ashir, shi kanshi Ashir yasha kuka dan tausan ƴan uwan nashi musamman Bahijja haka  ya dawo tsakar dare wajen su Tasi'u yafara basu hakuri....




"Tasi'u kuyi hakuri akan abinda ya faru tabbas nasan zan sakaku cikin tashin hankali musamman Bahijja naso inbayyana kaina abinda yasa ban bayyana kaina ba, nayi tunanin halin da zamu dawwama a ciki nida Bahijja dan haka zantafi domin sanar ma Yarima MalikusSaif abinda ke faruwa da Bahijja." murmushin karfin hali Tasi'u yayi yace. "Haba Ashir kakosan hadarin dake cikin wannan Masarautar miyasa baka gudu ba gudun kada a kamaka, sakon Yarima Malik yafi tsayawar d zakayi domin bamu hakuri har yanzun kasani munakan yima Yarima Malik hidima saboda a dalilin shi harmuka kasance a cikin wannan Masarautar dan haka kaje kafaɗa mashi halin da Bahijja ke ciki dan tafimu bukatar taimako." wani irin tausayi suka bashi, ganin basu damu da duk wani azaba da zasu fuskantaba indai akan Yarima Malik, cikin kulawa yace. "Shikenan yan uwana zan tafi saidai kusani muna taredaku kuyafeman." rungume juna sukayi kowa na hawaye su sunaji kamar su bisu dan duk sub rabu da iyayen su wasu kuma matan su da abokan su, da haka Ashir yai bankwana dasu cikin daren nan yabi ta wata hanya yafita cikin Masarautar Raifasu ga dare yayi tsakiya duk tsoro ya kamashi haka ya isa wani ɗan kauye ya kwana daman ana ya aje dokin shi ya hau yakama hanyar komawa Masarautar Daborah da abubuwa iri iri a zuciyar shi ya saka wannan ya kwance wannan  aranshi..... 


Itako Bahijja koda aka rufeta a cikin gidan kaso, ba abinda da takeyi sai ka mai tsuma zuciya tarasa wacce irin kaddara ke bibiyar ta na rabuwa da iyayen ta ko kuwa na kwana cikin ƙangin bauta da azaba tana cikin wannan tunani taji an cakwumota da karfi wata katuwar matace tagani ta zare mata idanu tace. ''Ke wacece da zakizo nan gidan kinaima mutane kuka kinsan baki iya kalar wannan rayuwar kika yarda kikazo nan, uban wayasa kikayi laifin da aka kawo ki nan wajen."?  Wani irin tsoro ne yakara kama Bahijja cikin disasshar muryarta tace. "Kiyihakuri nayi shiru dan Allah kada ki cutar dani." wani irin kallo taimata tace. "Mikikace."? Rufe baki Bahijja tayi tace. "Kimin aikin rai....  Bata jira cewar Bahijja ba tasa kafa ta halbata sannan tafara dukan Bahijja jin ihun da Bahijja ta saki yasa Sarki gidan yari ya rugo da gudu ya iske wannan matar sai dukan Bahijja takeyi kukan kura yayi ya anshe Bahijja ya datsa mata mari yace. "Bakida hankali kokin san wacece ita Sarki Baidulƙais yace kada asake a daketa shine ke fitsararra zaki duketa, zatayi magana yafara lallasa ta saida ta duku kamar ganga sannan ya sauyama Bahijja waje sai kuka takeyi a haka baccin wahala ya kwashe Bahijja ga wani irin sanyi da zazzaɓi duk ya addabeta.



Itako Jakadiya tana komawa gidan ta sai uban kuka takeyi sai addu'a takema Bahijja fatan ta tafita cikin wannan ukubar da take ciki tarasa dalilin dayasa kowa ya tsani Bahijja musamman gimbiya Yazira dda Uwani uwar bayi ta tabba ba yanda za'ayi Bahijja ta aibata abin bautar su, ita takara yarda da ubangijin Bahijja shine na gaskia ba abinda suke bautamawa ba icce marar anfani ranar haka Jakadiya ta kwana da tausayin Bahijja a cikin zuciyar ta.......



Danko Sarki Baidulƙais bai runtsaba tunanin wannan bawan yake aranshi yana jefama kanshi tambayoyi miya kawo shi miyazo yi sannan miyake bukata sannan miyahaɗa shi da Bahijja ina yasan Bahijja kodai Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ce ta aikoshi dan tafiya da bayinta inhar hakane tun yaushe yake cikin wannan Masarautar tawa lallai dole in hukun ta Sarkin kofa taya zaibar kowa na shigowa a cikin Masarautar shi kamar marar tunani, yana cikin tunani zuciyar shi ta raya mashi anya ba Sarki Lahazu ya aiko shi ba dan ya ɗauki Bahijja tabbas hakane ba ko tantama aiko yacika munafuki wato dan ɗanshi nason Bahijja shine zai bullo ta wannan hanyar lallai sai ya raina mashi hankali kenen shi zaima zamba cikin aminci kenan ranar dai haka Sarki Baidulƙais ya kwana ya saka wannan ya kwance wannan ya rasa matsaya wata zuciyar nacemashi Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ce wata nace mashi Sarki Lahazu ne, daga karshe yace bari a cigaba da bayin Daborah horo har sai sun faɗi abinda suka sane game da wannan bawa Tarib da haka ya kwanta kamar mai bacci



Koda gari ya waye haka gimbiya Yazira takira Uwani tana zuwa ta duka ta gaida gimbiya, sannan ta kalleta tace. "Uwani ya aka kwana akan wannan tsinanniyar yarinyar nan."? "Gurfanai yakara girma ai tana gidan kaso da kanshi Sarkin da take takama dashi yasa aka garƙameta tana can da mugun halinta, dandai abin haushi yace kada a sake a daketa a cigaba da tsarera kadai." shu'umin murmushi yayi yace. " kibarsu Uwani yanzun zan aikeki wajen Sarkin horo kikai mashi wannan sakon." takarda ce taba Uwani ta amsa sannan ta koma cikin daki sai jin dadi takeyi


Kamar yanda aka umurci su Tasi'u haka suka gurfana a cikin filin nan kamar wasu dabbobi Sarkin bulala ne ya fito da tafkekiyar bulalar shi yacigaba da dukan ta kamar zai kashe su haka yaita dukan su yana tambayar su waye wannan bawan da yazo wajen su, duka kamar zasu mutu, ana cikin dukan su saiga Sarki Baidulƙais yazo da sauri hadimai suka aje mashi kujerar zama ya zauna a gaban shi aka cigaba da dukan su ana tambayar su kowa cewa yayi bai sanshi haka ya sake kiran dogarawa sukaci gaba da dukan su tun suna magana har sun fara sikewa ganin zasu mutu wani bawa yace yanada magana, wani irin tsoro da razana suka kama Tasi'u Sarki Baidulƙais ne yace yanajin su cewa yayi. "Ranka shidade tabbas naga wannan bawan saidai bamusan wajen wa yazo ba yazo wajen mu muntaimaka mashi da ruwa yasha wannan shine abinda muka sani ya kalli wani daga cikin su yace. "Ko Lawi."? Shima gyaɗa kai yayi alamar eh wani mugun murmushi Baidulƙais yayi yace. "Kuci gaba da dukan su har safe in sukaki fadar gaskia akashe mani su basu da wani anfani a wajena ko kadan." da haka Baidulƙais yakama hanya ya tafi haka suka koma sashen Sarki Baidulƙais shida Jakadiya



Suna isa ya zauna itakuma Jakadiya ta zauna kasa cikin girmamawa tace. "Ranka shidade niko inasan inbaka wata shawara akan Bahijja."cikin kulawa yace. "Inajinki Jamra minene shawarar taki."? "Yawwa ranka shidade inaganin Bahijja batada masaniya akan wannan bawa Tarib da ake magana akai, sannan sai inga waannan bayi da ake hukuntawa da wahala idan su suka kawo wannan bawan a wannan Masarautar gani nike kawai akwai abinda yazoyi a wannan Masarautar." shiru yayi sannan yace. "Tabbas Jamra zan iya yarda ba ruwan Bahijja kan wannan alamari amman ban aminta da waɗan nan bayin ba da kike magana." "hakane ranka shidade saidai mizai hana bazaka fiddo Bahijja ba tunda kaima kanai kata kyakkyawan tazo."shiru yayi na ƴan dakikai sannan yace. "Bazan fiddotaba Jamra sai bukatar hakan ta taso wannan dalilin yasa nace kada wanda ya duketa."
"Amman ranka shidade....daga mata hannu yayi yaɗa mata yace. "Jamra ma fahimci kina shiga sabgar Bahijja ko miyasa."? "Ranka shidade kasancewar mai karanci shekaru a cikin bayi sannan kuma tanada amana da sanin yakama ta." "ko Jamra ashe kema kinsan halayen Bahijja kenan Zanduba amman ba yanzun ba, sannan karkiyi kokonto tabbas zaki yarda da abinda ake zargin waɗan nan bayin na Daborah kinsan basu da amana ko kadan daga su har Sarauniyar su da wannan wawan Yariman nasu wannan dalilin yasa ban rangwanta masu."



Shiko Ashir yana isa cikin Masarautar Daborah yasamu ya aje dokin shi ya shige turakar bayi bashi ya fitoba saida marece liqis sannan ya fito sallar magrib yayi daga masallaci bai zarce ko inaba sai sashen Yarima Malik iske shi tareda Huzaifa sai Yazid dukawa yayi yakai gaisuwa cikin kulawa ya amsa hakan yasa ya tashi yafara ƴan gyare gyare Huzaifa ne yace. "Ashir kwana biyu na rabu da ganin ka." cikin girmamawa yace. "Banjin dadi ne shiyasa sai yau nadawo bakin aiki." sannu sukayi mashi shidai Malik baice komai ba, basu jimaba sukayi mashi sallama suka tafi, tashi yayi yai wanka ya sake shiryawa sannan yakira Aliya yace. "Tashigo mashi da Ashir, bada jimawaba suka shigo sannan ta fita, kallan shi yayi yace. "Shine kafita daga infito wanka." sunkuyar da kanshi yayi sannan yace. "Allah yataimaki uban gidana ai wannan girman kane dan ka kimtsa shiyasa." murmushi yayi kai karamashi kyau da haiba sannan yace. "Sannun ka da hanya Ashir, bani labari." jiki sanyaye Ashir yace......

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*


Babi na 33&34


"Ranka shidade kamar yanda kaganni a gaban ka haka naga Bahijja gurfane agaban Sarki Baidulƙais tanai mashi hidima saidai....ɗaga mashi hannu yayi yace. "Kagama kawomani sako mai dadin sauraro Ashir amman inafatan ba wanda yaganka koda kuwa bayin Daborah ne."?


Numfasawa Ashir yayi sannan ya sadda kanshi ƙasa yace. "Ranka shidade ba takaran bayi ba hatta Bahijja taganni ita da uban gidan ta saidai bata ganeni ba kasancewar batai wayon sani ba." cikin rarrabewar kalma Yarima Malik yace. " ban...bane...ta...gan...kaba, kamar yaaa." ya ida maganar yana jan kalma, shi kanshi Ashir yanda yaji Malik yaima shi tambayar saida yaji cikin shi ya juya daurewa yayi yace. "Eh ranka shidade ta ganni." murtuke fuska Malik yayi sannan yace. "Inajinka." "Sarkin bayin Masarautar ne ya aikeni kaima Sarki Baidulƙais kayan marmari nan Bahijja taganni." "bance karka bari aganka ba."? 


A daburce yace. "Ranka shidade naso inga Bahijja ne sai na haɗu dashi shine ya aikeni." ɗan karamin tsaki Yarima Malik yaja sannan yace. "Banyi zatan zaka samu matsala akan abinda na umurceka ba, wannan dalilin yasa a cikin amintattun bayina na turaka ko kanada labarin Sarki Baidulƙais zai iya gane kaidin bakon fuska ne a cikin bayin sa koda kuwa sunkai dubu a lokacin."? 


Mamaki ne yakama Ashir a ranshi ko cewa yayi lallai kam kamar yasan abinda yafaru kenan nabani yazanyi yanzun cewa yayi. "Eh hakan tafaru ranka shidade." da sauri Malik ya mike tsaye ya goya hannayen shi a bayan shi ya kalli Ashir da wani mugun kallo yace. "Miyafaru."?  "Tabbas ankama bayin Masarautar mu ana tuhuman su akan sun sanni duk da naba Sarki Baidulƙais sunana a matsayin Tarib karshe wata uwar bayi cewa tayi ni bakone ga Bahijja taganni ta wajen masaukin bayin wannan Masarautar hakan yasa..."yimin shiru Ashir yimin shiru mizaka kuma ceman ko so kake kunnuwana sucigaba da sauraran banzan zancen ka so kake kacemani ankama Bahijja an bigeta bugun daya wuce misali tunda kagama ɓata komai ko kuwa cewa zakayi baki ɗaya bayin aka kama akaita bugu kamar za'a kashe su."? faɗaman mikake son kafaɗaman dakai nike.!" baki ɗaya jikin Ashir yagama mutuwa shikan shi yasan duk hakurin MalikusSaif dole ya fusata a kiɗime Ashir yace. "Tuba nike ranka shidade tabbas duk abinda ka faɗa haka take anacan ana tuhumar bayin Masarautar mu sannan itama Bahijja ankamata ankaita gidan kaso......bai bari kalaman Ashir sun dai daita ba Yarima Malik ya ɗauke Ashir da wani fitinannan mari saida ya faɗi cikin ɓacin rai ya nuna Ashir da yatsa yace. "Ashe kai wawane bansani ba kaico inama bakai na aika ba yanzun na sakamaka da sharri kagama shan wahalar yimin aiki yanzun gashi na saka maka dda mari, kai kuma kajefa mutane a ukuba ka saka Bahijja cikin bala'i ko kasan irin azabar da kabar Bahijja a ciki kuwa?  Miyasa baka baiyana kanka ba."? 


Tuni zufa tagama wanke Ashir kamar wanda yayi dambe da zaki danjin marin Malik yakeyi har cikin kwanyar kanshi baki ɗaya ji yayi ganin shi ya dusashe na ƴan dakikai sannan yace. "Kaimin afuwa ranka shidade tabbas nayi babbn kuskure saidai naso inmika kaina a lokacin da aka danganta Bahijja dani, abinda nayi tunani karkajini shiru shiyasa na fasa mika kaina duk da Bahijja taganni hakan baisa ta nunaniba lallai Bahijja ta cika mutun mai yawan halacci duk da batasan waye niba amman hakan baisa ta tunaman asiri ba balle aci zarafi, gaskia ne nagaza nagaza da nakasa bayyana kaina dan fansar Bahijja saidai duk nayi wannan abun dankai uban gidana."


"Aikin banza aikin wofi kayi a banza kwara ka fidda Bahijja cikin ukuba zaifimin dadi akaina ko kasan jin shurin ka shi zai tabbatar min da cewa ba lfy kai kuma daka fidda Bahijja cikin wannan mayuyacin halin amman yanzun naji Bahijja tana cikin Masarautar Raifasu amman tana cikin kalubale da tashin hankali, har yaushe kake tunanin yau ko gobe zanje ɗauko Bahijja bakasan sai nai dubarar barin Masarautar Daborah ba batareda kowa ya saniba, saidaina halin yanzun ka kasheman wannan tsarin nawa dole inje neman Bahijja kota wane hali koda hakan yana nufin ɓacin ran Sarauniya bazan iya bari inaji inagani rayuwar Bahijja ta halaka a sanadiya taba, kabani kunya Ashir kabani mamaki Ashir nayi danasanin aiken wlh blh Ashir badan kana bawana na amana ba da nabada misali akanka don ya zama izina ga sauran bayi da akanka Yarima MalikusSaif zai fara hukunci mai tsanani a cikin bayin Daborah saidai kaci arzikin aikin dakai mani baka cancanci haka, ammn ka buɗe kunnan ka kajini da kyau daga yau kar insake  ganin kafar ka  a cikin turaka ta koda wasa."

Da sauri Ashir yarike kafafun Yarima Malik yafara bashi hakuri yana kuka da hawayen shi kamar wani karamin yaro, saidai wannan lokacin tsananin tausayin Bahijja sun gama gauraye illahirin ɓargo da jijiyoyin jikin Yarima Malik baiganin kowa kojin komai sai tsananin son ganin Bahijja a tareda iyayen ta danjin shi yakeyi zai iya jayayya da mahaifiyar shi akan ceto rayuwar Bahijja, tureshi Malik yayi yace. "Ɓacemani dagani Ashir kada in illataka a banza da wofi tunkan sauran darajar ka ta zube a idanuna." duk da haka Ashir baikasa a guiwa ba ya sake rike kafar Malik yace. "Kayi hakuri ya shugabana dakayi fushi dani gwara kasa aita dukana koda kuwa za'aimani illah dan fushin ka yafi gwarwa shin wuta zafi a cikin zuciya ta na rokeka kaimin lamani in kai kaina a wajen Sarki Baidulƙais don fansar Bahijja, koda na rasa raina tabbas zanyi alfahari dan na cika umurnin uban gidana sannan ba isar mashi da sakon shi kuma Bahijja zata zama fansassa a wajena da sauran ƴan uwana bayi da suke a kalkashin ikon Sarki Baidulƙais na rokeka Yarima magajin gobe ka dubi shekarun da ba ɗebe ina tareda kai karka juyaman baya ka yafemani.!" duk da maganganun Ashir sun daki zuciyar Malik saidai baiji a lokacin zai iya yafema Ashir duk da ya girme shi amman baiji zai ɗaga mashi a wannan yanayin da yakeji ya tsani komai kuma bai shi'awar komai a dake yace. "Tashi katafi turakar bayi idan na yanke shawara zan nemeka, sannan bance ka kuma cewa komai ba." duk da Ashir bai yarda da abinda Malik yace mashi ba saidai baida zaɓi da ya wuce yabi umurnin uban gidan nashi sallama yai mashi ya tafi yana share kwalla duk ya shiga damuwa mai tarin yawa, Shiko MalikusSaif jiyayi wani kululu ya tsaya mashi a wuya ya rasa yanda zaiyi dashi ba shiri ya kwanta jin juwa na neman kada shi sai juyi yakeyi ga wata uwar yunwa da yakeji lokaci guda ya nemeta ya rasa hakan yasa ya mike kanshi na sarawa ya fita ba inda ya wuce sai gidan waziri nan ya haɗu da gimbiya Zahara'u gaishe shi tayi ko kallo bata ishe shiba balle tasa ran amsawa shigewa yayi a falo ya iske Huzaifa yagama cin abinci yanayin da yaga Malik kaɗai ya tabbatar mashi daba lafiya da sauri ya kama hannun MalikusSaif suka shige cikin ɗakin Huzaifa suka zauna gefen gado cikin kulawa yace "Yarima lafiya miyafaru faɗamani damuwar ka dan Allah." tashi Malik yayi sai dukan hannu shi yakeyi da tafin hannun shi yakasa cewa komai yaje ya dawi yaje ya dawo shima Huzaifa sake mikewa yayi jiki a mace yasake jefoma Yarima Malik tambayoyi iri iri, shi kanshi ya kosa yaji damuwar abokin nashi.

A wajen Bahijja kuwa zaune take duk ta takure ga wani uban cizan sauro duk yafasa mata jiki abinga ga fara duk jini ya kwanta kullin bata abinyi sai kuka da addu'a duk da ba'a bugun ta amman gidan yarin Masarautar Raifasu ya ta'azzara duk da kusan iri ɗaya  ne dana Masarautar Daborah, gidan yarine katoton gaske sannan kuma ginin kasane a gidan kasa akayi shi da dama idan aka kai mutun cikin shi ana iya mantawa da rayuwar shi baki ɗaya duk da baikai gidan yarin Masarautar Daborah girma ba, tana cikin tunanin duniya taji ankira sunan ta dagowa tayi Sarkin horo ne tagani ya kirata bayan budemata kofa da yayi daga rufewar da akai mata a wani karamin daki, tana fitowa baijira cewar taba taji ta wanke ta da mari har ukku da sauri ta rike kunci  hancin ta ya fara zubda jini tayi mamaki sosai duk da tasan Sarki Baidulƙais yace kar asake a duketa, jitayi yace. "Ke yar iska ko kina shiga dokokin gimbiya Yazira ko, barganin Mai Martaba yace kada a dukeki akan gimbiya Yazira zan iya halakaki sakarai dan haka yau kinma kanki baki ba abinci sai gobe mayya kin wani tsareni da dankwala dankwalan idanun ki." haka Bahijja  ta goge kwalla sannan tace. "Kaji tsoron Allah minai maka miya haɗani da gimbiya Yazira sannan dame kakeso inji da wannan kaddarar ko kuwa ta ƙazafi akan lamarin gimbiya Yazira zanji." cikin fushi ya sake wanketa da mari yace. "Wato ke bazakibarwbazakibar wannan addinin naki ba ko? Shine har anan ma zakimana shi da rashin kunya ko? Cikin gadara Bahijja tace. "Koda zaku kashe ni bazan bar addinina ba, dan na fidda rai da rayuwa dan haka ba tsoron mutuwa ko azaba zanjiba."


Ɗauko bulala yayi yafara wankar Bahijja saidai tasama ranta bazatayi kuka ba, haka yaimata bulala talatin sannan ya wurgata a  mazaunin ta ya rufeta, ya tafi hawaye ne masu ɗumi suka wankema Bahijja fuska, ta kalli jikin ta, murmushi tayi tace. "Tabbas Bahijja  kincika baiwa marar ƴanci da ace a gaban iyayena nike da bawanda ya isa yaimani kalar wannan ukubar danike ciki kwantawa tayi hawaye na zirara a saman fuskar ta.

A Wajen Sarki Baidulƙais kuwa zaune yake a fa'da shida magatakarda yaba shi  umurnin ya ruba wasika zuwa ga Sarkin Waisu Humzu cikin kankanin lokaci yagama rubutawa aka tada ɗan aike ya tafi Masarautar Waisu, bayan sun gama ƴan tsubbace tsubbacen su haka suka tashi ba inda ya tsaya sai filin da yasa anaba bayin Daborah horo na musamman koda ya isa ya iskesu sunyi laushi ligib haka yakara tambayar su waye Tarib saidai jiya iyau ba wani sauyi dan amsar su ɗaya ce basu san shiba, wannan karon kau da kanshi ya dukesu sannan ya shaidama Sarkin bulala itace damar su ta karshe muddun sukaki faɗar gaskia yabasu damar su halaka su baki ɗayansu haka ya koma sashen shi rai ɓace koda suka iso ya iske Jakadiya tagama haɗama shi kayan abinci zama yayi zaifara ci kenan saiga gimbiya Yazira itama zama tayi suka gaisa sannan tafara bashi abincin zai ƙarɓa kenan Bahijja ta faɗo mashi a rai, yaji bazai iya ciba yarasa dalilin dayasa yake tausayin Bahijja a zuciyar yasan dai baida burin da ya wuce ya ɗanɗani zumar Bahijja sannan taimashi aikin bauta, sawa yayi Jakadiya takaima Bahijja abinci a gidan kaso hakan ba karamin dadi yaima Jakadiya ba dan tanaso taga ƴar tata,haka ta ɗauka ta tafi gidan yari, itako gimbiya Yazira wani irin bakin ciki ne ya turnike ta afili ta bayyanar da ɓacin ranta tace. "Haba ranka shidade taya zaka ba baiwa wannan abincin mai daraja ina laifi kabarta ta amshi na gidan kaso!." wani irin kallo yaimata sannan ya mike tsaye yace. "Kina nufin inabarta yunwa ta illatata kenan ba haka nike ba duk abinda nikeyi inasane." yana gama faɗa ya shigewar shi daki tanasan shiga tana shakkar abinda zata tarar dan tasan halin mijin nata baida wasa ko kadan.

Itako Jakadiya koda ta isa haka mai kula da kofar gidan yarin ya bude mata gate ta shiga wuce masu laifi taitayi kananan masu laifuka su ba gidan kasa suke ba wata hanya sukabi sannan suka taka wani bene kamar zaka shiga cikin rijiya haka suka dinga bin wannan bene suka shige gidan kasa duk da safiya ce amman kace marece dan ba wani wadataccen haske ke gare shiba nan Sarkin gidan yarin yaimata iso zuwa wajen Bahijja tana isa ta isketa ta buga uban tagumi koda taga Jakadiya da sauri ta mike saidai ba yanda zatayi ta iso wajen Jakadiya sabida rufe kofar dakin Bahijjan take hakan yasa Jakadiya tasa aka bude Bahijja zama wani wuri sukayi sannan Sarkin gidan yari ya basu waje, cikin kulawa Jakadiya ta kalli Bahijja tana taɓa fuskar tace. ", Bahijja lafiya naga fuskar ki duk jini miyasa meki harhaka."?  
buɗe baki Bahijja tayi tace.

"Bakomai Baba bugewa nayi da ta'ga,kaina kemin ciwo shine hancina ya buge da ta'ga." kallanta tayi cikeda mamaki tasan Bahijja bata taɓa yimata karyaba saidai bata amince da abinda tagaya mata ba, saidai ta kyaleta, tace. "Ga abinci kici ƴata karki damu zaki fito bada jimawa inafatan ba abinda ke damunki."?  "Eh Baba bakomai." bata abinci tayi ta zuba mata kamar mayunwa ciya haka Bahijja takama cin abinci ba ƙauƙautawa wani tausayi Bahijja taɓa Jakadiya kamar tayi mata kuka haka taji,cikin kulawa tace. "Ƴata rabanki da kici abinci tun yaushe ko ba'a baki abinci, lemor abincin da Bahijja ta ɗauko yasa ta dakata sannan tace. "Baba ko yaufa saida akabani abinci kawai dai wannan abincin da kika girkomani akwai dadi yayi kama dana sarakai." takarasa maganar tana cigaba dacin abincin ta, kallan Bahijja tayi fuskarta ɗauke da murmushi sannan tace. "Eh abincin Sarakai ne Mai Martaba ne zaici sai yafasa yace akawo maki kici." ɗebe hannun ta Bahijja tayi take fuskarta ta sauya ture kwanan abincin tayi tace. "Baba da ace tun farko kinsanar dani daga inda abincin nan yazo da bazanciba, duk Masarautar nan dake kadai na amince Baba ke ke kaunata, kece Mahaifiya ta." tagama maganar kwalla ba zirara a idanunta itama Jakadiyar kwalla ta share sannan tace. "Ƴata ki aje duk wata kiyayya da ake maki baki da wani zaɓi daya wuce ki amshi duk abinda yazo daga gareki koda a hannun makiyinki ne Bahijja ba wanda zai cutar dake, saidai kada ki amince da gimbiya Yazira, amman Sarki Baidulƙais bazai cutar dake ba, saidai zai iya cika burin shi akanki, ta wannan fannin karki amince dashi azzalumi ne ki rike wannan a ranki ƴata." gyaɗa mata kai Bahijja tayi sannan ta rungume Jakadiya suna cikin magana Sarkin yari yazo yace ma Jakadiya lokaci yayi, da toh ta amsa sannan taba Bahijja ruwa tasha, kallan abincin gaban Bahijja yayi hakan ba karamin ɓatama Sarkin yari rai yaiba daurewa kawai yayi, sannan tayi sallama da Bahijja ta tafi, kallan Bahijja yayi yace. "Waya baki izinin cin abinci yau bana ce bazakici komaiba sai tace. "Daga Mai Martaba ne." komawa tayi cikin dakin ta yasa kwaɗo ya garkameta da mugun kuduri aranshi iri iri.


Koda Jakadiya ta dawo ba inda ta wuce sai madafa takai kwanuka sannan ta faɗi abinda za'a girma Sarki Baidulƙais na dare, haka takoma wajen Sarki koda yaga Jakadiya da kallo yabita na kindad, cewa tayi. "Ranka shidade koda nadawo daga wajen Bahijja na wuce madafa dan sanar da uwar tuwo abinda zakaci da dare." "sannu da kokari Jamra, ya kikabar Bahijja a wane hali take."? shiru Jakadiya tayi kamar mai nazari sai tace. "Ranka shidade ban amince da yanayin da naga Bahijja saidai dana tamabayeta damuwar ta tace mani bakomai." murmushi yayi yace. "Shikenan Jamra kin kyauta saidai kidaina takurata akan abinda take ɓoyewa a ranta, zaki iya tafiya kije ku huta kafin anjima." godiya taima Sarki sannan ta tafi, shiko a ranshi shima bai yarda da abinda Bahijja tafaɗama Jakadiya ba yanaji akwai abinda akemata yasan bazata faɗa ba dan rashin galihu da ƴanci bazai bari ta faɗa saidai yasan yanda zai bullowa lamarin da haka ya tashi ya shiga daki ya cigaba da yan tsafe tsafen sa da yasa ba.



Daborah


Yarima Malik yaka makura wajen zirga zirga har Huzaifa yagama saka rai da sanin damuwar MalikusSaif, suna cikin haka kowa yayi shiru wani uban ajiyar zuciya Yarima Malik ya saki sannan ya kalli Huzaifa yace. "Mezakace ga baiwar da batasan hawaba batasan sauka ba ake azabtar da ita akan wani dalili wanda bata da ilimi akanshi."?  Take jikin Huzaifa yai sanyi yasan tabbas Umman Bahijja na cikin ukuba kenan to miyafaru har haka kallan Yarima Malik yayi sannan yace. "Gaskia anzalince ta sannan zata kasance cikin tashin hankali koda kuwa zata kashe kanta tabbas gaskiar ta abin korarwa ne saboda baiwace marar ƴanci da galihu."
"Ko zaka iya taimakonta."? Cikin mamaki yace. "Kwarai kuwa musamman yanda batasan komai ba, saidai wacece wannan baiwar da ta ɗaga hankalin Yariman Daborah har haka."? "Wakake tunanin zai ɗaga mani hankali ya hanani sukuni injishi tamkar jinin jiki banda Bahijja!" da sauri Huzaifa ya kalli MalikusSaif yace. "Inakasamu labarin Bahijja kana nufin tana Masarautar Raifasu."? "Kwarai kuwa Huzaifa hakan yasa goben nan nikeso ka kashirya zamu tunakari Masarautar Raifasu dan ɗauko Bahijja!" bakin Huzaifa na haɗe haruffa yace. "Tayaya kakeganin zamu tafi."? "Ko bazakaje ba intafi da Yazid."? Murmushi yayi sannan yace. "Allah ya huci zuciyar magajin gobe ina nufin ya zamuyi da Sarauniya."? ''Koda hakan ya nufi yafeni saina ceto rayuwar Bahijja." "Aaa MalikusSaif kada kabari tausayin Bahijja yayi rinjaye akan ka karabu da Mahaifiyar ka, kasan ta hanyar da zaka bi, mutafi." "kaga Huzaifa ko kasan cewa a halin yanzun Bahijja na gidan kaso a garkame." "subhanalilah agarin yaya."?  Hmmm cewar Malik sannan yace  "ta hanyar wannan shasha shan Ashir nasaka shi aikin dubomani idan Bahijja na Masarautar Raifasu hakan yasa ta shiga cikin ukuba dan anganshi wajen masaukin su ba ita da sauran bayin da muka bashi kaga dole inje incire Bahijja cikin wannan musibar duk fa akaina ta shiga wannan halin.

Wani irin tausayin Yarima Malik ya kama Huzaifa dan shi a halin yanzun abokin nashi yake jima tausayi ganin yanda ya damu da baiwa kamar Bahijja marar daraja a cikin Masarautar Daborah tabbas akwai wani baban almari shi yafara tunanin abokin shi bawai tausayin Bahijja kadai yake yiba harda so saidai yasan baisan yana son taba abinda yasani tausayin ra yakeyi itada iyayen ta, jin shirun yai yawa yasa MalikusSaif ya kalli Huzaifa yace. "Ko baka da abin cewa shiyasa karakani da idanu har haka."?  "Hmm Malik kenan akwai babban lamari da nike hange tsakanin ka da Bahijja, amman gaskia abinda ya kamata kafara tunkarar Abbuh akan wannan maganar." "kaga Huzaifa shima bakin shi ɗaya da Sarauniya kawai dai shi yanajin tausayin su, saidai son shi a gareni bazai barshi ya banni inje ɗauko Bahijja ba a ewar shi baisan insaka rayuwata a cikin haɗari kaga dayake ni ɗane ita kuma Bahijja ba ƴa' bace baiwa ce." "Aaa Yarima karka faɗi munanan kalamai akan iyayen ka, abinda yafi kamar yanda nace kaje ma Abbuh da wannan maganar abinda yace sai munema kanmu mafita zuwa gobe sa safe ina ganin hakan zaifi." cikin gamsuwa Yarima Malik ya amince da shawarar abokin nashi tashi yayi yace bari yaje wajen Mahaifin nashi dan baison lamarin ya wuce safiya baije wajen. Bahijja  ba.


Koda ɗan aiken Sarki Baidulƙais ya isa Masarautar Waisu yana zuwa Sarkin gida yaimashi iso zuwa wajen Sarki Humzu, yana zaune saman wata shimfiɗa ta alfarma shida ɗanshi Yarima Haishir sai matar gimbiya Ƙamariya, yana zuwa suka zube suka kwashi gaisuwa, cikin kulawa suka amsa Sarkin gidane ya faɗa mashi dalilin zuwan bakon nashi ɗagama Sarkin gida hannu yayi a lamar ya tafi dan ko ba'a faɗa mashi ba daga ganin wannan yadin yasan kayan bayin Raifasu ne, kallan Yarima Haishir yayi hakan yasa ya karɓi takardar dake hannun wannan bawan budewa Yarima Haishir yayi sannan yafara karantawa. 


 _Bayin mamakin cin amanata dakayi ba Humzu ni naɗaukeka amini kuma ɗan uwa amman kai saɓanjn haka dan haka kasani bawan ka daka turo dan ya sace Bahijja haƙar ka bata cimma ruwa ba dan kana so kaba danka Haishir auren ta shine zaka biyo ta wannan hanyar ta kasani duk abinda zakayi Baidulƙais yana ankare dakai dan haka Bahijja tawace malakina kuyi gaggawar rufe babin Bahijja tunkan ayi baku daga Sarkin Raifasu zuwa ga Sarkin Waisu....._ 


Wani irin huci Haishir ya furzar kallan baka da wayo Sarki Humzu yaima Haishir sannan ya kalli wannan ɗan aiken duk da agaban shi aka karanta sakon yace ya tashi ya tafi sannan inyaje ya gaishe da Sarkin su, ba Haishir ba hatta ɗan aiken da gimbiya Ƙamariya saidsaida sukayi mamakin jin amsar da Sarki Humzu yace aba abokin nashi haka ya tashi ya tafi zuwa Masarautar Raifasu, yana tafiya Yarima Haishir yace." haba Baba taya zaka bada wannan amsar ga abokin gaba tunda a halin yanzun ya tashi daga masoyi."


Hmmm murmushin girma Sarki Humzu yayi sannan yace. "Yaro yarone hakan bakaramin ciwo zaima Baidulƙais ba, ba'a nuna ɓacin rai a gaban abokin gaba yanzun ba zargin mu yake ba akan kaima baiwa Bahijja farmaki ba, ni nafi zaton Sarauniya Zulaiƙa keson ɗaukar baiwar ta kasan itace fansa akan Yarima HusbarulƘais dan haka tunda harya jefomani wannan mummunan kalaman saina rabashi da Bahijja cikin kankanin lokaci."dariya Yarima Haishir yayi yace "shiyasa nike alfahari dakai Babana kanada hikima shikenan Bahijja zata dawo gareni inyi yanda nike so da ita." gimbiya Ƙamariya ce tace. "Kaji wawa inakai ina baiwa kana ɗan Sarkin Waisu magajin gobe." ɓata rai Yarima Haishir yayi yace. "Umma da acekinga Bahijja ina tunanin  a wannan duniyar da muke ciki ba wata ƴa mace da takaita kyau, kina tunani abune mai sauki Sarki Baidulƙais zai tsaya yana gargaɗi akai kema kinsan Bahijja abice mai matukar daraja akowace Masarauta tasamu baiwa kamar ta, ta cancanta ta zama cikakar gimbiya kodan ta zama abun tunkaho a cikin duniya da wannan kasar tamu ta Gambia  mai albarka


Yanda Sarki Humzu yaga yanayin Haishir ya tabbatar  dacewa yai nisa ga son Bahijja dan haka zai iya kokarin sa dan ansota dan haka dole ya nunama Yarima Haishir cewa Bahijja fa baiwa ce mai cikeda rikici sarkakiyawa da kalubale ya tabbata Sarki Baidulƙais bazai bar Bahijja kodan yanda yake fasiƙi sannan shima Haishir ko wane lokaci burin shi Bahijja tazama mamora a gareshi koda ba auren ta yaiba, sannan uwa uba yasan muddin MalikusSaif yana numfashi sai ya kwace Bahijja ta koma kalkashin ikon mulkin su kodan addinin ta, da haka yaita sake sake sannan yace ma Haishir. "Ɗana kabi asannu lamarin Bahijja akwai ruɗarwa karka zira jikin akanta saidai kasa aranka gab muke da kwato Bahijja a cikin wannan Masarautar tamu ta Waisu kodan incusama Baidulƙais bakin ciki a cikin zuciyar shi.


Shiko Yarima MalikusSaif yana isa wajen Saifulislam ya iske Mahaifiyar shi ta fito daga cikin ɗakin mahaifin nashi daga ganin zataje fa'da yanda yaga tayi shirin gaidata yayi yana mai ɓoye damuwar shi a kan fuskar shi, duk da haka saida Sarauniya Zulaiƙa taji ranta yabata ɗan nata na cikin damuwa rike hannun shi tayi cikin nata sannan tace. "Yariman Daborah kamar akwai abinda kedamunka, faɗaman mike faruwa.?" kirkiro murmushin karfin hali yayi sannan yace. "Ummu bakomai kawai kaina naimani ciwo ne kuma nasha magani." cikin kulawa tace. "Sannu ɗan albarka ya kamata ka huta gaskia shiyasa naga karabu da zuwa fa'da kenan."? Da eh ya amsa Mahaifiyar tashi, cikin kulawa tayi bankwana da ɗan nata baki ɗaya hankalin ta yanakan Yarima Malik hakadai ta tafi fa'da, shiko yana isa ɗakinll Mahaifin nashi yai sallama ya iske shi kan sallaya, da murmushi ɗauke a fuskar shi ya amsa cikin so da kauna sannan yace. "Yarima daganin ka kana cikin damuwa kuma akwai abinda kake ɓoyema Abbuhn ka kuma abokin shawarar ka faɗaman damuwar ka ɗana."?


"Abbuh Bahijja itace damuwa ta kuma matsala ta.!" wani mummunan faɗuwar gaba ta riski Saifulislam, aran shi ko cewa yayi anya ɗan nan yanada hankali miyakawo maganar Bahijja daman Malik bai cire Bahijja aran shi ba duk da bata cancanci hakan daga garemu ba amman rigimar dake tareda Bahijja ba karama bace, Allah dai yasa Malik ba wani abu yake ji dangane da Bahijja koda yake baiwa ce fa kuma hadimar shi, Aaa bata yuyuwa ko Mahaifiyar shima bazata lamunce da hakan ba, wata zuciyar tashi tace. "Kai ko Saifulislam miyakawo wannan maganar da bata da tushe mtsw daman haka zuciya take a zahiri kuwa cewa yayi. "Dana miyakawo maganar Bahijja." "haba Abbuh baka ganin halin da take ciki antabbatar mani cewa tana cikin hali yanzun haka tana gidan kaso kaga dole mutaimaketa Abbuh." Cikin kulawa Saifulislam ya kalli ɗan nashi sannan yace..... 




 *Ƴar Mutan Kankia ce❣️*

*BISSIMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*

🔚🔚🔚 of Book One


Babi na 35&36



 *Wannan page din nakine kiyi yanda kikeso dashi Pharteemarh knk inaimaki fatan alkhairy ta wajena🥰* 



 ~Kusani inayinku irin sosai din nan Bayima Ƴaƴane Comments Group kusani inajin dadin yanda kuke mani ruwan Comments Allah yabar so da kauna inai maku fatan alkhairy da duk wani masoyina wanda nasani da wanda bansani ba duk ina maku son fisabilillah Allah yabar kauna 🤝🏻~ 




"Tabbas Malik yakamata ace zuwa yanzun Bahijja tadawo wajen iyayen ta saidai nayi mamakin yanda har kasan tana cikin wannan mawuyacin halin saidai wannan ba abun la'akari bane, mafita itace yanda za'ayi a anso ƴar mutane tabbas Baidulƙais yaci amana bayyanan niyar wanda da wahala muyafe mashi ita, saidai mahaifiyar ka tasan da wannan zacen. "? Cikin daburcewa Yarima Malik yace. "Kullin abinda take gargaɗina kenan in mance da rayuwar Bahijja hakan yasa na yanke shawarar dakaina zanje ansar Bahijja Abbuh ba faɗamaka dan insamu albarka da adduarka agareni."


"Hakane fatan alkhairy Saidai inaso kasanar da mahaifiyarka wannan zacen." "shikenan Abbuh naji dadin addu'arka Allah yakara girma da ɗaukaka." "Ameen ya Allah, saidai abinda zan faɗamaka kasani cewa,ban yarjemaka kabar Masarautar nan ba, kai kaɗai zan haɗaka da jarumai da zasu kasance dakai." da sauri ya kalli mahaifin nashi yace. "Abbuh kayi hakuri bansan tafiya da wasu jarumai ni kaɗai zan tafi."
"Shikenan saidai karka manta ka tabbatar ka gayama mahaifiyarka kafin kayi wannan tafiyar." da toh ya amsa mashi sannan yaimashi sallama ya fita, sashen shi ya wuce ya zauna dan yasan tana fa'da sai 'yan sake sake yakeyi yanaso yaje wajen mahaifiyar shi amman yana tsoron abinda zai iske wajen shi yasan zata amince da kudurin shi.



A wajen su Yarima Haishir kuwa yana gama shiryawa ya wuto wajen mahaifin shi yace. "Abba zantafi Raifasu dan cika umurnin ka kasamani albarka." fuskar shi ɗauke da murmushi yace. "Gamji ya shiga lamarin ka sannan ka kiyaye karka bari a samu matsala ɗana." toh Abba,da haka suka hau dawakan su shida bayin shi guda ukku. 



 _Masarautar Raifasu_ 



Bayin daborah ne aketa ganama azaba kamar ba mutane ba daga cikin bayin wani bawa ne da ake cema Nulwa yacema Sarkin bulala ya tsaya zai faɗi abinda yasani duk wanda ke wajen saida sukaji wani mugun faɗuwar gaba, kallan bayin yayi yace. "Inajinka." "Ranka shidade nasan wannan bawan da kuke tuhumar mu akan shi, wato Tarib, ba sunan shi Tarib ba sunan shi Ashir daga cikin mu yake yazo daga Masarautar Daborah wannan shine gaskiar abinda nasani kataimakeni karka kashemu muntuba."
Cikin ɓacin rai yace. "Daman kusan da wannan zancen kukayi shiru.!" jiki na kyarma Nulwa yace. "Aaa daman zamu faɗa." daga hannu Sarkin bulala yayi ya watsama Nulwa mari, yaci gaba da faɗa da dukan su, ji sukayi Sarki Baidulƙais yace. "Dakata da dukan su." zama yayi saman wata kujera ta alfarma yace. "Nulwa kace kasan bawan nan."?  Da sauri ya ɗaga kai yace. "Eh daga wajen mu yake sunan shi Ashir."
Kallan shi yasake yi yace. "Miyakawo shi a wannan Masarautar."? Raba idanu Nulwa yayi ya kalli Tasi'u duk ya raina gaskiar shi wani mugun kallo ya watsama Nulwa, sauke kanshi yayi kasa yace. "Yazo dan ya duba idan Bahijja na cikin wannan Masarautar." shiru yayi yana nazari duk kanshi ya kulle jikin shi yai sanyi yace. "Waya turoshi."? Da sauri Tasi'u yace. "Ranka shidade ba wanda ya turoshi kawai dai hanya ce ta biyo dashi shine ya shigo." da sauri Nulwa yace. "Bahaka bane Yarima Malik ne yazm turoshi.!" 


Mikewa Sarki Baidulƙais yayi yace. "Koda naji ainasan daman kune munafukan." dan haka aita bugun su kar a raga masu sannan a kaiman su gidan kaso a rufe mani su, bance abasu abinci ba ko kadan dan haka duk wanda ya nemi yaimaku taurin zuciya ku kashe shi." da sauri Nulwa ya rike hannun shi yace. "Karufamana   Sarki Baidulƙais yace. "Kaga wannan bawan kasare mani kanshi sannan ka kawo mani shi a cikin fa'da ta." take cikin Nulwa ya duri ruwa haka aka tisa keyar su har cikin gidan yari sai kuka sukeyi......



Koda aka isa dasu gidan kaso haka aka dinga jibgarsu kamar jakai sannan Sarkin horo ya sauke kan Nulwa da kanshi yakaima Sarki Baidulƙais, fa'da ce tamkan da mutanen koda ya iso da kan Nulwa ya aje gaban Sarki Baidulƙais yace. "Ranka shidade angama cika umurnin ka." kallan magatakarda yayi yace. "Rubutama Sarauniya Zulaiƙa wasika kaba bawa yakaimata sannan asaka kan wannan bawan cikin buhu a haɗa mata dashi, yanzun yanzun." sannan ya kalli Sarkin horo yace. "Je kacigaba da abinda ba saka bance a ragama wa'annan bayin ba saidai karka daki Bahijja." haka ya tashi ya tafi...... 



A Masarautar Daborah kuwa Yarima Malik ne yaje wajen Sarauniya Zulaiƙa iske ta yayi kwance a saman makeken gadon ta na alfarma yana shigowa bayin dake dakin suka zube suka kwashi gaisuwa ɗaga masu hannu yayi alamar amsawa baki dayansu suka fita, zama tayi gefen gado tana kallan ɗan nata, a hankali ya iso wajen ta daka ganshi kaga mai damuwa, shima zaman yayi gefen ta sannan ya gaidata cikin girmamawa, kallan shi tayi tana nazarin ɗan nata sannan tace. "Yarima mike damunka."?  Shiru yayi yarasa dame zai fara, ganin shirun yayi yawa yasa ta sake jefo mashi tambaya akaro na biyu, a hankali ya buɗe bakin shi kamar baisan magana yace. "Ummu kimin aikin gafara bansan yanda zaki ɗauki wannan maganar ba.!" murmushin manya tayi sannan tace. "Kaɗama mahaifiyarka abinda ke zuciyarka dan ita kadai yafi cancan ta kasanar mawa Yarima." cikeda gamsuwa yace. "Ummu inasan zuwa Masarautar Daborah domin anso Bahijja." da sauri Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta mike tace. "Banhaneka maganar wannan baiwar ba miyasa ka takura kanka akanta? Ko bakasan minike yawan faɗa maka ba Malik."? 


A zuciyar shi kuwa cewa yayi, gaskia Ummu saidai kiyi hakuri dan ba abinda zai hanani ceton rayuwar Bahijja, amman a zahiri sake marairaicewa yayi yace. "Ummu koda tana cikin musiba."?  "Koda zata rasa ranta koda hakan na nufin rayuwar ta zata lalace ta tarwatse koda hakan ya na nufin daina numfashin ta, wai ko kamanta itadin baiwa ce? Ko manta itadin marar daraja ce? dan haka kada insakejin bakin ya ambaci kalmar Bahijja."


Wani mummunan faɗuwar gaba ta riski Malik kallan mahaifiyar shi yayi sannan ya duka ya rike kafarta cikin sanyin murya yace. "Ummu Malik dan Allah kitausaya mani kibari inceto rayuwar Bahijja karki manta alkhairyn da taimani a rayuwa.!" "yimin shiru, yimin shiru Malik wlh badan bansan inga bacin ranka ba dakasha mamaki, inka kuskura bakin ma yakara furta kalmar Bahijja saina wanke ka da mummunan mari sha sha sha, ɓacemani dagani.!'' a hankali MalikusSaif ya mike jiki sage kamar wanda kwai ya fashemawa a ciki, duk da haka bai kasa a guiwa ba ya faraba mahaifiyar shi hakuri yace. "Ummu kiyi hakuri Allah ya huci zuciyarki sannan duk abinda kikaji game dani na ɓacin rai ki tausaya ki yafemani sannan kisama ɗanki albarka Ummu." baijira cewarta ba ya fita ranshi nai mashi ƙuna,  ba inda ya wuce sai sashen shi ya fara shirin barin Masarautar Daborah a sirrance bayan kiran Huzaifa da yasa akai mashi.


A wajen Bahijja kuwa Jakadiya ce zaune a gefen ta suke magana kasa kasa saidai dagani kamar sirri sukeyi Jakadiya ce tafara magana. "Bahijja inasan inmaki wata magana tabbas ƴata tunda naganki naji kiyimani har cikin zuciyata sannan kuma addinin ki yaimani dan haka a yau nikeson inshiga cikin wannan addinin naki ƴata." cikin farin ciki marar misaltuwa Bahijja ta kalli Jakadiya ta rungume ta tace. "Babata naji dadi marar misaltuwa da naji wannan batun daga gareki dan haka addinina abune mai kyau da jinkai ga tausayi dan mahaifina yasha faɗamani hakan, yanzun duk abinda nace ki faɗa." cikin jin dadi Jakadiya tace. "Toh ƴata." Bahijja ce tayi shiru tana ɗan tunani da alama wani abun take tunawa tace. "Kice Ash hadu alla ilaha ilallahu wa ash hadu anna muhammadar rasulillahi." haka Jakadiya ta maimata rungume ta Bahijja tayi tace. "Wannan shine makullin shiga addinina, ma'anar kuwa shine kince kin shaida babu abin bautawa da gaskia sai Allah kuma annabi Muhammadu baiwan Allah ne kuma manzon sane, daga yau kinzama cikakkar musulma Baba,  saidai banda ilimi sosai a cikin addini na,duba da yanda aka rabani da iyayena, amman kibari idan Allah yasa na fita  wannan gidan zan nemi Tasi'u shi zai kara koya maki komai dangane da addinin mu." sake rungume juna sukayi suna hawaye cikin so da kauna. 


 gimbiya Yazira ce da kanta taje gidan yari ta harɗe kafa saman kafa tasa Sarkin horo yana ganama Bahijja azaba bugun ta yaci gaba dayi itako Bahijja ta dukar da kanta kasa tana kwalla, ga zaginta gimbiya Yazira nayi kowa hankalin shi yayi wajen mugunta itako Bahijja azaba ta isheta ji sukayi ance. "Mi zangani haka wannan wane sakarci ne."?  Cewar Sarki Baidulƙais atake gimbiya Yazira ta ruɗe danko Sarkin horo zubewa yayi kasa yana neman afuwa, danko Sarki Baidulƙais yagama kulewa ranshi yai mugun ɓaci baisan sadda ya wankama Sarkin horo mari ba, itako Uwani sai kyarma takeyi kiran Sarkin bulala yayi yasa shi yaima su Uwani dukan tsiya sannan yasa aka garkame su gidan kaso ita da Sarkin horo, isa danko gimbiya Yazira gumini yagama wanke mata jiki sai kyarma takeyi jiki sake tafara magana. "Ranka shidade daman abinda yasa kaga nazo gidan....wata irin tsawa ya dakama gimbiya Yazira sannan ya wanketa da maruka har ukku cikin fushi yace. "Daman na lura kintsani Bahijja baki kaunar ta na fahimci haushin ta kikeji daman sharri kike mata akan gurfane nasan bazata aika abinda ake tuhumar ta dashi ba, dan haka yau ba sai gobe ba nakara ganin kin shiga sabgar Bahijja bani bake sannan naimaki katangar karfe da Bahijja dan haka maza kiɓaceman dagani! " yakarasa maganar ranshi ɓace, itako da wani marayan kuka ta fashe tafara ba Sarki Baidulƙais hakuri, baki ɗaya wani irin tsanar gimbiya Yazira yaji wanda baitaɓa jin irin shiba"kayimani halacci kayafemani karka rabu dani Abban Husbarul kai kadai gareni da rabuwa da ɗana zanji ko kuwa da juyaman baya da zakayi ka yafemani.!" shiru yayi kamar mai nazari sannan yace. "Baki san wannan ba, ita Bahijjar me taimaki kika tsaneta dan haka baki ba Bahijja, sannan Uwani daga yau na fiddata a uwar bayi kuma na yanke mata hukuncin gidan kaso na wata shidda munafukai."kallan Bahijja yayi wadda tunda Sarki Baidulƙais yafara magana ko kallan shi bataiba saboda ta tsane shi bata san kallan fuskar shi, sawa yayi aka kaita sashen shi. 



 _Daborah_ 



Koda Huzaifa yazo wajen Yarima Malik ya iske shi yagama shiri kallan Huzaifa yayi yace. "Huzaifa kaje kashirya cikin daren nan zamubar Masarautar nan." cikin gamsuwa Huzaifa yace. "Ranka shidade aituni nagama shiri na baka da damuwa saidai kabari kafar mutane ta ɗauke sai mutafi." murmushin jin dadi Yarima Malik yaima Huzaifa sannan yace. "Yawwa na wajena naji dadi kamar wane lokaci kake ganin zamu tafi."? "Ranka shidade idan naga dama tabada zanzo da kaina in gayamaka saidai ince ka tsaya cikin shiri, fata Allah yasa muyi wannan tafiyar cikin sa'a." da Amin Yarima Malik ya amsa yana cigaba da abinda yakeyi shima Huzaifa fita yayi.


Koda aka kai Bahijja sashen Sarki Baidulƙais sawa yayi aka kira mashi Jakadiya yace, takai Bahijja wajen Saude dan bata kulawa ta musamman, tashi Jakadiya tayi daga dukiyar da tayi suka fita da Bahijja suka tafi taskar bayi, ranar haka su Zainab sukaita murna Bahijja ta dawo garesu, nan aka haɗa mata ruwan wanka ta shiga tayi sannan ta kwanta koda dare yayi Bahijja ce tafito ta tafi wajen Jakadiya tadata tayi tace. "Baba yazamuyi idan aka gane kin karɓi addinina kuma kinga anka su Tasi'u bare ya koyar dake komai yaza'ayi."? "Haba Bahijja kada ki damu kema kinsan yanda zaki ɓoye addinin ki kina kankanuwar ki bare kuma ni kada ki damu kanki ki sani zan kiyaye kuma na aminta da addinin ki shine na gaskia." wani irin dadi Bahijja taji ta rungume Jakadiya tana kuka wanda batasan ko na mene ba, hakuri Jakadiya tafara ba Bahijja sannan tace. "Yawwa ƴata koyaman abinda kika sani a cikin addinin musulinci nan Bahijja taimata bayanin sallah dai dai gwargwado, harta nuna mata, ba ita ta koma wajen Saude ba sai cikin dare.


A Wajen Malik kuwa ko bacci yakasa burin shi yaga Huzaifa ya shigo bai ida tunanin da yake ba saiga Huzaifa haka suka kama hanya suka fita a sirrince kowa ya hai saman dawakai suka kama hanyar tafiya Masarautar Raifasu.


Tunda Baidulƙais ya tashi ya fito zuwa falo yana fitowa yasamu Jakadiya zaune tana jiran fitowar uban gidan ta, yana zama saman ƙayatattar shimfiɗar shi ta gaida shi, amsawa yayi sannan ya kalli Jakadiya yace. "Jamra ya Bahijja ta kwana."? Cikin girmamawa tace da sauki saidai yau ban haɗu da ita ba."ɗaukar ruwa yayi yasha yana ajiyar zuciya yace. "Kije ki karamani Bahijja." "Angama ranka shidade."tafiya tayi ta tafi taskar bayi, itako Bahijja tana zaune da Saude suna fira Saude ke cema Bahijja "niko inasan intambayeki ƴata."? Murmushi ɗauke a fuskar Bahijja tace. "Inajinki Baba Saude."
"Ƴata ya sunan Mahaifin ki."? A bazata tambayar tazoma Bahijja kallan Saude tayi sannan tace. "Abdulmajid sunan shi." gyaɗa kai tayi sannan tace. "Mahaifiyar kifa."? Sake bata amsa tayi da Binta sunan ta cewar Bahijja, shiru Saude tayi tana nazari wasu kwalla ne suka wankema Saude fuska tai saurin gogewa cikin damuwa Bahijja ta fara tambayar Saude damuwar ta ganin tana hawaye, itako Saude kukane yaci karfin ta takasa magana da kyal Bahijja ta lallashi Saude tayi shiru sannan tasake cewa. "Baba Saude miyasaka ki kuka har haka."?  Ajiyar zuciya Saude ta sauke sannan tacema Bahijja "ƴata wani babban lamarine na tuna shekaru da daɗewa har yau abin dashi nike kwana nike tashi nakasa mantawa dashi a rayuwa, duk lokacin da kike taredani sai injiki kamar jinina nike jinki ƴata." cikin tausayawa Bahijja tace. "Menene wannan abun kidaina damuwa faɗamani kilan inada taimakon da zan maki."wani irin kaunar Bahijja taji tana fisgarta sannan tace."hakane ƴata saidai ki saurari abinda zan faɗamaki....tafara magana kenan sukaji Jakadiya ta shigo gaidata sukayi sannan Bahijja ta kalli Jakadiya cikin sakin fuska tace Baba ina kwana inata kewarki." itama murmushi taima Bahijja sannan tace. "Ƴata kenan ya karfin jikin naki."? "Da sauki Baba." "yawwa Bahijja kizo Sarki na kiranki." "take duk wata walwalar Bahijja ta kau baki ɗaya yanayin ta ya canza ganin kallan da Jakadiya taimata yasata tashi taima Saude sallam a suka tafi, a hanya Jakadiya ta dinga kwantarma da Bahijja hankali da kalamai masu dadin sauraro koda suka isa jin yanda ta tsargu ya tabbatar mata da Baidulƙais ke kallan ta sake sadda kanta tayi harta iso wajen shi tareda Jakadiya suka gaida shi, sai kallan Bahijja yakeyi sannan ya amsa, itako a takure take a hankali ta saci kallan shi taga ita yake kallo addu'a tafara, ji tayi yace. "Mike maki ciwo Bahijja."? Take taji ta tsani ko wace kulawa daga gareshi,  "bakomai." sake kallan ta yayi yace. "Kintabbata dda bakomai."?  da eh ta amsa tana wasa da hannun ta, shiru ne ya biyo baya bai fasa kallan Bahijja danji yake kamar ya haɗeta duk ranar da ya kalli Bahijja sai yaji muguwar shi'awarta ta addabi rayuwar shi idanun shi suka sauya kalla yai karfin hali yace. "Kinci abinci kuwa."? Saita samu kanta da cewa. "Eh naci." badan ya yarda ba ya kalli Jakadiya wadda duk abinda ke faruwa tana jin su yace. "Jamra kije madafa ki ansoma Bahijja abinci daga gani ko a gidan kaso ba'a bata abinci." da sauri Bahijja ta kalli Baidulƙais suka haɗa ido, taki sauke nata ji take kamar ra shake shi ta huta dan duk abinda zaiyi baitaɓa burgeta azzalumi ne baida imani ko kadan, jitayi yace. "Bahijja lafiyarki ko baki bukatar abincin ne."?  Sake bashi amsa tayi da eh, murmushi yayi yasan karya takeyi dan yasan ba abinda taci, cigaba yayi da hadimar shi kmar baisan ya ajiye Bahijja ba, shiru kowa yayi itadai sai kalan kasa takeyi tana sake sake iri iri Shiko kallanta kawai yakeyi yana mamakin irin girman da take karawa ji sukayi wasu bayi sunzo sun aje abinci da kallo Bahijja ta bisu, badbada jimawa ba Jakadiya ta shigo yasa Bahijja ta zuba abincin sannan yace taci abincin satar kallan shi tayi taga ba alamar wasa haka tafara yagunar abincin kamar anai mata dole har ta gama sannan yace taje gida.


 _Daborah_ 



Washe da safe, Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ce zaune a fa'da anata fadanci ita da ƴan kasuwa ne ana shawara akan yanda za'a gudanar da cin kasuwar shekara da za'ayi a cikin babbar kasuwar Gambia wadda take a kalkashin ikon Masarautar Daborah, kowa yana bada shawarar shi akan yanda yakamata abada tsaro duba da yanda take cika da baki daga kasashen duniya sassa daban daban suna cikin magana saiga Sarkin gida ya shigo fa'da ya zube ya gaida Sarauniya sarkin zagine yace. "Angaisheka Sarkin gida meki tafe dakai."?  Sake sunkuyar dakan shi yayi sannan yace. "Ranki shidade kinyi bako daga Masarautar Raifasu yana bakin fa'da." kallan kallo ne ya biyo baya kowa yana mamakin jin sakon, ɗaga kai tayi alamar a shigo da bakon koda Sarkin gida ya fita yaima bawan Sarki Baidulƙais iso har cikin fa'da baki ɗayan su suka sake kai gaisuwa sannan ya fita, Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ce ta kalli bawan da alamar tambaya, yanda yaga ta kalle shi saida yaji gaban shi yai mummunan faduwa dan baiga alamun wasa ba a fuskar shi sake dukar dakai yayi sannan yace. "Ranki shidade sakone daga Sarki Baidulƙais yace inkawo maki wannan wasikar, kallan magatakarda tayi da sauri ya karɓa sannan ya kalleta, ta bashi izinin karanta wasikar kamar haka. 



 _Inama ace baki yunkurin cin amanata ba Sarauniya Zulaiƙa nizakiyima zamba cikin aminci ki turo bawanki ya sace Bahijja, toh kisani kaidin ki da burin ki bai cimma ruwa ba dan haka ina gargaɗarki da fita harkata sannan kisani inaja dake Zulaiƙa duk abinda kike takama dashi ni Baidulƙais na wuce dake inkunne yaji gangar jiki ya tsira_


Wani irin bakin ciki ya tokare zuciyar Sarauniya Zulaiƙa take tasa aka sallami bawan, idanun ta sun kaɗa jajir kamar garwa shi dan ɓacin rai da har ya fita ya dawo da wani buhu a hannun shi ya duƙa yace. "Ranki shidade na manta yace abaki wannan, da sauri Sarkin fa'da ya karɓa budewa yayi ya fiddo kan Nulwa a cikin buhu ya aje agaban Sarauniya Zulaiƙa koda taga kan Nulwa mikewa tayi tasa kafa ta halba shi sannan tace. "Ni Zulaiƙa ƴa ga Abdulhamid jikanya ga Sarki Haris tattaɓa kunne ga Sarki Khamil ɗan Sarki Jabbar na Sarauniya Yanazu na rantse da Allah wlh blh sainaga bayan wannan wawan Sarkin ni zai aikoma wannan sakon har cikin Masarauta ta a fa'da ta, har an'isa da zai aiko mani da kan wannan jakin bawan har abin mamaki ne dan bayi sunci amanar shi koya manta basu da amana nima kaina amanata sukeci!  Nulwa yake kowa dan haka aje akiramn uban shi yazo ya ɗauke mani wannan jakin, sannan ina Sarkin horo."?  Da sauri yace. "Ranki shidade gani." "nabaka nan da minti biyar kaje ka tattaro mani kaf bayin Masarautar Daborah." bata jira cewar shiba ta sake halba kan Nulwa ta fita rai ɓace. 


A wajen Yarima Haishir kuwa da marece liqis ya isa Masarautar Raifasu saidai bai kaida shiga cikin Masarautar ba, shida bawan shi duk inda zashi tare suke zuwa sai bayin da ya taho dasu, suna isowa wani karamin kauye suka zauna yaba bayin shi kaya harda bawan shi mai mashi hidima haka suka saka kayan bayin Raifasu suka shiga dan sama masu masauki kafin tsakiyar dare su shigo cikin Masarautar.


Itako Bahijja ita da Zainab suka fito shan iska bayan taskar bayi suna fira sai shawagi takeyi ta rasa dalilin dayasa takejin dadin yanayin yau, ba abinda yasha masu kai suka cigaba da labari zama sukayi saman wani dutsi suna cikin firar su, Bahijja tace. "Zainab kinsan mine."? "Aa tace ma Bahijja, murmushi tayi tace. "Inaso nidake aga wanda zaifi gudu a taɓo wancen iccen magoran." takarasa maganar tana murmushi, itama Zainab murmushin tayi tace. "Agwada agani mana nasan ba fiki gudu, aiko suka fara ƴar rere da Bahijja kowa nason ya wuce ɗan uwan shi, suna cikin gudu Zainab ta gabza uban tuntuɓe aiko ta duke tana kuka itako Bahijja batasan tanayi ba sai haki takeyi takai wajen iccen mangaro tana isa ta taɓa iccen tana maida numfashi itayi an rufe mata ido ta kwala uban ihu, itako Zainab jan kafa tayi ta taho tana banyarda saidai acanza gani tayi an ɗauki Bahijja tana ta halbe halbe, anyi daji da ita, da gudu Zainab tabi tana ihu sadda zata iso har sun ɓacemata, komawa tayi taskar bayi tana kuka kamar ranta zai fita tayi gidan Saude.... 


Cikin kankanin lokaci labari yagama karaɗe Masarautar Raifasu anɗauke Bahijja, koda Jakadiya ta samu labari da gudu ta isa wajen Sarki Baidulƙais tana haki shima mikewa yayi yana jefama Jakadiya tambaya. "Jamra lafiya miyafaru."? "Haki takeyi da kyar tace. "Ranka shidade ansace Bahijja.!"  da sauri Baidulƙais ya mike yana jefoma Jakadiya tambaya "waya saceta."? "Ranka shidade bansani ba." kamar ya baki saniba."? "Ranka shidade Zainab ce takawo wannan labarin." baisan sadda ya fita ba Jakadiya nabiye dashi sawa yayi aka kira mashi Zainab ya tambayeta ta faɗa mashi komai, baki ɗaya jikin Baidulƙais yagama mutuwa ko motsin kirki yakasa sai al'ajabin lamarin yakeyi ko magana yakasa baki ɗaya kanshi ya gama kullewa tunanin shi ya tsaya cak yarasa abinyi.... 


Itako Sarauniya Zulaiƙa angama tara mata bayi kaf sun gurfana, tana zuwa ta kalle su ɗaya bayan ɗaya sannan tace. "Kudai baku da amana a cikin ku har za'a samu wanda zaije Masarautar Raifasu ɗauko Bahijja dan haka Sarkin horo aita dukan su har sai sun bayyana kansu inba haka ni kadai nasan abinda zan masu." bayin Allah haka akaita gana masu azaba itana gefe tanai masu kallan tsana, ana cikin dukan su saiga Ashir shima dukawa yayi nan Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace. A daka da dukan su, "Sarkin horo kaware mani Sarkin bayi Abdulmajid da Binta kaita dukan su, duk yanda akai su suka aiki wannan bawan da ake nema." wani irin tashin hankaline ya kama Ashir zaiyi magana Sarauniya Zulaiƙa ta ɗaga mashi hannu tace. "Jeka kira mani Yarima MalikusSaif a sashen shi." bayan da Ashir ya iya haka yaje sashen Yarima Malik, yaita sallama shiru hakan yasa ya shiga ɗakin shi nan ma shiru har wajen Huzaifa yaje bashi ba alama hatta Huzaifan akan hanya ya haɗu da Yazid ya faɗa mashi baiga Yarima ba shima cewa yayi daga lambu yake baigan shi haka Ashir ya koma ya zube Wajen Sarauniya Zulaiƙa lokacin anata dukan su Binta kamar za'a kashe su, cikin murya kuka Ashir yace. "Ranki shidade tabbas Yarima Malik bai cikin Masarautar nan ba inda ban duba ba." mikewa tayi ta aza mashi mummunan mari sannan tace. "Aikin banza aikin wofi Sarkin horo daina dukan su, aje a dubaman kaf Masarautar Daborah inda Yarima Malik yake."haka aka bazu bashi ba alamar shi, haka aka dawo aka shida Sarauniya Zulaiƙa bashi ba alamar shi, jikin ta na kyarma tace  a zuciyar ta. "Tabbas inko har zargin da nikeyi MalikusSaif ya tafi ɗauko wannan matsiyaciyar yarinyar Bahijja tabbas zaigamu da hushi, cikin ƙaƙƙausar murya Sarauniya Zulaiƙa tace "Sarkin Yaki ka ɗibi jarumai kubi sahun Yarima Malik, bance kai masu komai ba saidai duk Masarautar da kuka samu MalikusSaif ka aiko mani ɗan aike saina shafe babin su musamman Masarautar Raifasu." da angama Sarkin Yaki yafara haɗa jarumai.... 



Suko su MalikusSaif tafiya sukeyi mai nisan gaske ba inda suka tsaya sai wata inuwa dan hutawa kafin suci gaba da tafiya. 
A Wajen Sarki Baidulƙais kuwa ya saka bayin shi da Sarkin gida neman Bahijja a cikin kewayen Masarautar Raifasu dan tabbatar da zargin da yakeyi a cikin zuciyar shi duk da bai bayyanawa kowa kudirin dake cikin zuciyar shiba


Jaruman Sarauniya Zulaiƙa suka gama shiri kowa ya hau saman dawakai, tace. "Daga nan kufara bin hanyar Masarautar Raifasu bance ku ɓata lokacin kuba." haka suka kama hanyar  neman Yarima Malik......


 _Laifin dadi karewa masoyan na anan nakawo karshen wannan ƙayataccen labari ba Bayima 'Ƴa'ƴane littafi na ɗaya, wanda yake cikeda sarkakiyawa  da ban tausayi tsantsar zalinci da tarin ilimantawar wa, badan naso ba zan dakatar da wannan labarin saidan wani uzuri daya tasomani, na komawa Makaranta da zanyi, dan haka ina neman addu'o'in ku, ku sani inai maku son fisabilillah ❣️_ 



 
 *Sai mun haɗu a Bayima Ƴaƴane ne Book two wanda anan ne zaku samu amsoshi masu yawa da suka shige maku duhu* 



⏺️ Shin Yarima Malik zai isa Masarautar Raifasu dan ɗauko Bahijja? 

⏺️ An ɗauke Bahijja waya ɗauketa? 


⏺️ Jaruman Masarautar Daborah zasu cimma Yarima MalikusSaif? 

⏺️ Yarima Haishir zai cimma burin shi a Masarautar Raifasu? 


⏺️ Sannan Sarauniya Zulaiƙa zata gane wanda bawan da yaje Masarautar Raifasu? 


⏺️ Sannan wane kuduri ne Sarki Baidulƙais ya boye akan sace Bahijja da akayi? 


⏺️ Sannan wace badaƙala ce zata wanzu tsakanin Sarki Baidulƙais da Sarauniya Zulaiƙa da Sarki Lahazu? 



 *Waɗan nan tambayoyin dama wasu zasuzo maku a cikin Bayima Ƴaƴane book two insha Allah.*
